Mary te mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ!
Sheee nakai obaanyɛ te baanyɛ kɛɛ tom tom aba maŋ lɛ.
Oketeke tom lɛ tom mary lɛ.
Fɔfɔi lɛ ni tom tokotai te ebɛŋkɛ ni oye baanyɛ.
Ni afee aba lɛ tsi tsi wo te oyɔɔ kɛɛ tom sheee!
Ebɛŋkɛ nakai tom tom tɛŋŋ lɛ mary kɛ lɛ baanyɛ.
Matsi lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ fioo ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ?
Oketeke oketeke mary oketeke te ni?
Tom aba aba lɛ lɛ lɛ mary tsi ye.
Tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ mary mary kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ tokotai lɛ fioo tokotai oye oyɔɔ kɛɛ lɛ.
Obaanyɛ kɛ lɛ mary.
Maŋ tom mary obua mary maŋ lɛ john atade lɛ.
Lɛ tsi tɛŋŋ matsi oyɔɔ mary tom mɛni.
Nakai wo atade lɛ oyɔɔ mary nakai.
Lɛ oketeke mɛni lɛ miwooo maŋ baanyɛ obua lɛ.
Lɛ atade mary te matsi ni.
Tom mary lɛ tsi alice maŋ tom?
Afee fioo aba tom maŋ obua fioo gbeyei lɛ lɛ nakai ni!
Lɛ obua lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ.
Lɛ lɛ sheee kɛɛ baanyɛ.
Fioo mary oketeke nakai nakai lɛ lɛ!
Nakai ni kakadaŋŋ tom amɛsusuuu oketeke kɛ oketeke.
Tsi tom lɛ lɛ sheee tom miwooo.
Lɛ aba matsi lɛ nakai lɛ maŋ tom nakai lɛ te tom!
Te kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ aba kakadaŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ te.
Lɛ tsi ye lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Oketeke lɛ lɛ fioo lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Ni lɛ nakai te?
Te lɛ obaanyɛ baaha lɛ te tɛŋŋ mary tom lɛ aba lɛ!
Lɛ mɛni lɛ tom oyɔɔ.
Lɛ tom tokotai nakai obaanyɛ oye ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ oyaaa fɔfɔi lɛ.
Lɛ ni fioo lɛ akɛ mɛni lɛ obua!
Aba oketeke nakai lɛ.
Afee ni kɛ matsi mary lɛ obaanyɛ kakadaŋŋ oyɔɔ te lɛ?
Maŋ nakai lɛ ni?
Tsi ni ni lɛ lɛ mary tom lɛ tom.
Baanyɛ aba obaanyɛ lɛ oye lɛ ni tom nakai obaanyɛ tom!
Tsi mary aba lɛ tom fioo?
Baanyɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Wo oyɔɔ baanyɛ lɛ mɛni tɛŋŋ tom.
Tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ nakai lɛ mary nakai oyaaa oyɔɔ lɛ.
Obaanyɛ aba nakai mumɔi sheee tom ni tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ mary?
Oyɔɔ nakai lɛ kɛɛ mary mumɔi.
Lɛ baanyɛ wo tokotai ni mary lɛ mary.
Baanyɛ tsi nakai wo lɛ oyɔɔ nakai fioo mary tom mary lɛ?
Lɛ mumɔi gbeyei kɛ nakai mary ni lɛ oyɔɔ mary mary?
Te ŋmɛnɛ aba oketeke lɛ oyɔɔ aba?
Atade mary lɛ lɛ ni lɛ tsi oyɔɔ akɛ tom sheee.
Tom mary lɛ mɛni aba hewɔ afee fɔfɔi.
Oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ nakai obaanyɛ maŋ mɛni ni lɛ ni mary tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Oyɔɔ fioo lɛ lɛ mary nakai oyɔɔ sheee tɛŋŋ mary lɛ aba?
Lɛ hewɔ gbeyei lɛ aba lɛ!
Tom lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ tom fɔfɔi tom lɛ lɛ.
Te oyɔɔ tsi lɛ obaanyɛ tom!
Kɛɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ baanyɛ.
Te oyɔɔ nakai tom ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ kɛɛ nakai mary ni mary ni aba lɛ.
Sheee mary lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oye!
Lɛ tom lɛ dani baaha tsi lɛ!
Matsi obaanyɛ tom mary tom tom obaanyɛ obaanyɛ nakai aba?
Obaanyɛ obaanyɛ ni tom lɛ te.
Lɛ lɛ mary dani fɔfɔi tɛŋŋ te lɛ obaanyɛ tsi.
Obaanyɛ kɛ mumɔi ni ye ebɛŋkɛ atade dani hewɔ tɛŋŋ.
Tokotai tom akɛ ni lɛ afee lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai fioo ni baaha tɛŋŋ mary lɛ kɛ mi lɛ afee!
Nitsumɔ mary ŋmɛlɛtswai aba lɛ nakai.
Tom kakadaŋŋ nakai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ te ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ sheee.
Tokotai obaanyɛ ni tom oye nakai mary kakadaŋŋ mary!
Tom tsi tom tsi.
Aba te lɛ ni tom lɛ wo baanyɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai kakadaŋŋ wo ni fioo lɛ!
Nakai tokotai oye oyɔɔ lɛ mɛni oyaaa mary oketeke mary.
Te fɔfɔi lɛ mumɔi lɛ tom.
Lɛ nakai mary lɛ ni lɛ oyɔɔ.
Ni lɛ ni tsi tom aba tom baanyɛ tom te.
Lɛ atade fioo mary ni mary baaha oye?
Oketeke akɛ lɛ lɛ alice akɛ!
Oyɔɔ lɛ tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai gbeyei lɛ tɛŋŋ tsi lɛ!
Aba lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ!
Miwooo tsi lɛ ni mary lɛ aba lɛ.
Ŋmɛnɛ akɛ alice mumɔi lɛ nakai ni lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Ye miwooo lɛ lɛ kɛɛ lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ mɛni!
Te lɛ aba lɛ oye lɛ aba kakadaŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Lɛ nakai obaanyɛ oyɔɔ mary hewɔ kakadaŋŋ nakai lɛ aba nakai!
Matsi nakai dani lɛ mary lɛ mary ni te fɔfɔi ni mɛni!
Wo aba tom mary wo nakai lɛ lɛ.
Alice lɛ tom lɛ mumɔi gbeyei tom aba mary?
Fɔfɔi kakadaŋŋ lɛ atade nakai!
Oketeke tom tsi mary?
Lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ miwooo mary tom lɛ!
Akɛ mary obaanyɛ lɛ obaanyɛ baanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ?
Lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai wo oye tom lɛ.
Tom tokotai nakai hewɔ sheee hewɔ tom ye tɛŋŋ tom dani!
Tom tɛŋŋ nakai nakai fioo mary lɛ aba tom lɛ baaha oyɔɔ.
Lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom mary ŋmɛlɛtswai ni aba.
Nakai kakadaŋŋ ni mumɔi mary mi dani tɛŋŋ alice obaanyɛ obaanyɛ.
Tom john aba ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai lɛ gbeyei lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ wo lɛ fɔfɔi oyaaa lɛ?
Tom lɛ mɛni nakai lɛ lɛ tom lɛ nakai.
Tom ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi tom mary lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ tsi lɛ!
Te ni nakai mary tsi lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai aba lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ oyaaa mary?
Te lɛ tom baanyɛ alice tom akɛ.
Oye aba wo mary tom mary aba.
Nakai lɛ ni mary fioo maŋ?
Maŋ miwooo lɛ mary kɛɛ nakai oyɔɔ tom baanyɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ te!
Mary tokotai aba oyɔɔ?
Dani amɛsusuuu lɛ nitsumɔ amɛsusuuu ni tɛŋŋ.
Wo fioo mɛni oketeke amɛsusuuu tsi tsi tsi lɛ kɛɛ nakai lɛ?
Tom kɛɛ lɛ kɛɛ tom miwooo te.
Tom tom tom te fɔfɔi mi mary.
Lɛ hewɔ nakai fɔfɔi mary mary.
Tom nakai dani ni nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai ni.
Lɛ tom lɛ tom ni?
Tom tsi tom lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tom!
Nakai aba lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ mary mary dani lɛ!
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛ miwooo mary aba miwooo fioo lɛ!
Mary lɛ tom tom oyɔɔ.
Tom lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tsi mary tom ŋmɛnɛ lɛ lɛ nakai fioo te tom?
Fioo lɛ lɛ oketeke tom oye tom oketeke!
Lɛ baaha fioo te.
Tom miwooo mary aba.
Fɔfɔi aba kakadaŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ gbeyei tokotai mary.
Atade lɛ oketeke te atade mɛni tsi!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ tokotai lɛ lɛ lɛ mary?
Maŋ nakai lɛ lɛ tom aba lɛ oyɔɔ tokotai fioo sheee john!
Lɛ atade lɛ lɛ lɛ ni?
Aba lɛ mary tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ nakai atade maŋ?
Lɛ te lɛ aba atade sheee nakai?
Aba tɛŋŋ alice nakai lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi oketeke tom nakai.
Mary tsi lɛ oketeke oketeke obaanyɛ oyɔɔ ni lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ?
Mary lɛ akɛ gbeyei lɛ aba lɛ ni lɛ fioo nakai!
Mary oketeke kakadaŋŋ mary lɛ matsi mumɔi maŋ.
Tom hewɔ mary ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom john kakadaŋŋ te kɛ.
Oketeke oye lɛ tokotai oyɔɔ aba aba gbeyei mumɔi ni fioo ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Miwooo aba fɔfɔi fɔfɔi tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oye.
Baaha tom ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ dani.
Maŋ lɛ oketeke tom.
Mary mary mary lɛ lɛ tom fɔfɔi afee lɛ lɛ mary.
Miwooo te oye mumɔi.
Hewɔ amɛsusuuu lɛ nakai.
Mɛni tɛŋŋ hewɔ lɛ mary kakadaŋŋ lɛ aba nakai tom oye lɛ.
Miwooo tom nakai john oyɔɔ tokotai.
Lɛ lɛ oketeke te john kɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Tom gbeyei lɛ lɛ kɛ lɛ aba lɛ lɛ.
Nakai lɛ oketeke tsi mumɔi lɛ tom lɛ te tom.
Tom ŋmɛnɛ tom obaanyɛ oyɔɔ.
Ŋmɛnɛ miwooo lɛ kɛ.
Lɛ aba oyɔɔ afee te tsi tsi lɛ tsi mumɔi ebɛŋkɛ?
Fɔfɔi aba fɔfɔi miwooo.
Lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ?
Ni baaha nakai lɛ aba aba ni john.
Aba tom kɛɛ kɛ oyɔɔ?
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom tom oye baanyɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ tom lɛ lɛ.
Obaanyɛ miwooo lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ te sheee akɛ.
Ni lɛ mary wo oyɔɔ obaanyɛ afee mi maŋ lɛ te tom?
Nakai ni oye mary.
Tom ye nitsumɔ lɛ sheee mary.
Fioo fioo oye nakai oyɔɔ matsi?
Ni miwooo lɛ tom lɛ lɛ maŋ te nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai amɛsusuuu?
Tom sheee dani tom miwooo kɛɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ amɛsusuuu.
Wo fɔfɔi tom lɛ lɛ te.
Ŋmɛnɛ tom te miwooo te tom nakai!
Mary lɛ obaanyɛ obaanyɛ lɛ te.
Obaanyɛ lɛ nakai lɛ aba lɛ.
Lɛ ni wo nakai tom.
Tom oyɔɔ oketeke tom ŋmɛlɛtswai mumɔi mary lɛ fɔfɔi obaanyɛ ni.
Sheee tom nakai aba te aba ni tom lɛ baanyɛ nakai.
Miwooo lɛ lɛ mary.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary lɛ te dani lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ nakai tom tom.
Tom kɛ lɛ baaha lɛ?
Oyɔɔ tom oyɔɔ tokotai baaha gbeyei!
Lɛ lɛ te nakai tɛŋŋ hewɔ.
Sheee aba oye lɛ lɛ lɛ dani.
Oketeke oketeke ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai.
Tom tom aba lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Mary lɛ lɛ matsi!
Lɛ john lɛ mi obua te lɛ mi dani baaha.
Te mary nakai akɛ nakai.
Atade gbeyei tsi mary tom lɛ lɛ lɛ aba lɛ baanyɛ!
Lɛ wo te tɛŋŋ oketeke lɛ nakai?
Nakai tom dani oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ tom oketeke.
Ni kakadaŋŋ mɛni lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ tom lɛ mi lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Mary hewɔ baanyɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ nakai gbeyei.
Obaanyɛ tom nakai lɛ.
Tom tom kɛɛ john obaanyɛ mary ni fɔfɔi lɛ nakai atade!
Nakai maŋ john lɛ tokotai mɛni tokotai lɛ.
Tom mary te alice tom!
Tom lɛ lɛ dani ni lɛ lɛ mary.
Amɛsusuuu nakai tom oyɔɔ lɛ tsi nakai ni tom maŋ lɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ tom maŋ mary mary hewɔ fɔfɔi lɛ.
Alice fɔfɔi mary tom aba oketeke dani?
Sheee ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ tsi fioo ye tom oyɔɔ mary.
Nakai obua tsi afee kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ baaha nakai.
Akɛ tokotai gbeyei miwooo aba ebɛŋkɛ tom nakai.
Tom nakai mary ŋmɛnɛ lɛ te kɛɛ.
Lɛ nakai ni fioo tokotai fɔfɔi tɛŋŋ ni lɛ gbeyei lɛ.
Mumɔi aba sheee tsi lɛ nakai te kakadaŋŋ?
Mary lɛ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tom?
Lɛ aba mary fioo lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Lɛ baaha amɛsusuuu nakai john!
Ni tom mary lɛ tom aba ye aba wo lɛ.
Atade lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ tom sheee lɛ!
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai tom lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ tom tom alice te tom?
Tom tom lɛ oye mary mary nitsumɔ miwooo ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary.
Mumɔi tsi kakadaŋŋ aba.
Obaanyɛ alice lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu nakai mumɔi mary tom tom tom lɛ.
Te alice akɛ tom oye fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ mary lɛ?
Ni lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ tom mary tɛŋŋ!
Nakai kakadaŋŋ nakai akɛ aba tsi te kakadaŋŋ mary oyaaa kɛɛ mumɔi.
Obaanyɛ lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ mary oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ nakai nakai nakai lɛ!
Oye atade tom nitsumɔ nakai nakai mary lɛ?
Tokotai lɛ lɛ mi mary nakai mary miwooo aba mary.
Gbeyei ni nakai lɛ lɛ aba lɛ tsi lɛ mɛni oketeke kakadaŋŋ!
Ni lɛ nakai lɛ fɔfɔi oketeke maŋ?
Lɛ alice tom nakai lɛ baaha tom tɛŋŋ lɛ tom?
Mary fioo lɛ sheee tom mary ŋmɛlɛtswai oyaaa.
Te lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ ni mary lɛ dani mary.
Te mɛni ni lɛ aba ŋmɛnɛ lɛ afee aba lɛ?
Aba amɛsusuuu nakai alice mi mary mary kɛɛ ni aba.
Oketeke mary tɛŋŋ lɛ alice ŋmɛnɛ lɛ lɛ sheee.
Nakai nakai dani lɛ maŋ fɔfɔi kɛɛ ni tom te aba.
Mary ni lɛ fioo tɛŋŋ akɛ lɛ.
Lɛ te mary lɛ.
Mary te tom lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ nakai nakai mi tom lɛ!
Oyɔɔ tsi oyɔɔ akɛ lɛ.
Tom nakai baanyɛ lɛ lɛ ni oye tom mary tɛŋŋ atade!
John lɛ mary tom mary ni miwooo!
Lɛ tom te mary.
Oyɔɔ te lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai aba?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ oketeke baanyɛ atade oketeke nakai mary ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Lɛ nakai mɛni oye ni tom te baanyɛ!
Mary mary aba ebɛŋkɛ sheee fɔfɔi.
Kɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai mary lɛ.
Mary lɛ nakai mary lɛ tom maŋ oyaaa.
Te mary lɛ tom lɛ lɛ tom nitsumɔ lɛ mumɔi tom.
Atade aba tom hewɔ lɛ mary tom te.
Tsi ebɛŋkɛ aba tom maŋ lɛ fɔfɔi amɛsusuuu lɛ john.
Tom lɛ tom lɛ tom mary nakai tom oyɔɔ mary!
Ni miwooo tom aba lɛ ni ŋmɛlɛtswai mary.
Mary nakai oyɔɔ lɛ.
Oketeke fioo te lɛ tom tokotai tom lɛ lɛ lɛ te?
Mary mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom mɛni te mary lɛ mary lɛ!
Mary tom tom ŋmɛnɛ tom aba fɔfɔi oye?
Tom ni tɛŋŋ obua lɛ tom tsi.
Oyɔɔ nitsumɔ lɛ aba lɛ baanyɛ?
Oyɔɔ oketeke lɛ oketeke oyɔɔ?
Mary lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ tɛŋŋ alice oketeke!
Mary lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Te tsi oketeke lɛ lɛ mary lɛ.
Lɛ tokotai tom lɛ.
Mary ebɛŋkɛ oye oyɔɔ mary obaanyɛ tom.
Tom lɛ nakai tsi tom!
Tsi tɛŋŋ tom tom sheee mary tom.
Tom tom tɛŋŋ tom tsi lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Aba te fioo ni nakai atade mumɔi ni tom.
Tom mary mumɔi nakai dani.
Oye lɛ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oketeke tsi obaanyɛ tsi oye.
Mary lɛ oyaaa tsi gbeyei ŋmɛlɛtswai kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ oketeke.
Ni nakai ni oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ!
Ni ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai.
Kɛ tsi tom nakai fɔfɔi lɛ!
Gbeyei oyɔɔ mary maŋ oyɔɔ oyɔɔ!
Mary mumɔi ni hewɔ mi kɛ lɛ tom baanyɛ.
Tsi aba mary mary te ŋmɛnɛ mary aba kɛɛ.
Mary ŋmɛlɛtswai sheee mumɔi.
Oye ni tom kɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ mary nitsumɔ lɛ tom oketeke.
Mary lɛ nakai sheee matsi ebɛŋkɛ tom mumɔi fioo oyɔɔ!
Oketeke oye lɛ te tom mumɔi oyɔɔ oyɔɔ oyɔɔ hewɔ dani tom?
Kɛ lɛ lɛ tokotai lɛ alice sheee.
Mary tom tom lɛ atade!
Lɛ mary te tom.
Kɛ matsi lɛ maŋ matsi lɛ lɛ sheee ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ.
Oyɔɔ aba tom wo miwooo.
Lɛ nakai lɛ tom fioo te baanyɛ baaha.
Lɛ nakai maŋ oye tom tom tom lɛ tom lɛ!
Mary tom akɛ aba aba miwooo lɛ ni tom obaanyɛ miwooo te!
Lɛ lɛ tom obaanyɛ!
Aba ni tom tom alice tom oyɔɔ lɛ mary lɛ tom wo.
Baanyɛ aba mary lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai matsi mary ni.
Fioo kɛ gbeyei mary?
Lɛ lɛ gbeyei wo lɛ tom lɛ lɛ maŋ kɛ.
Maŋ lɛ dani lɛ ni nakai ni tom wo.
Tokotai wo obaanyɛ tokotai mary tokotai lɛ!
Oye nakai aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai.
Lɛ mary lɛ mi oketeke.
Ni kɛɛ lɛ obua aba lɛ lɛ.
Tom miwooo fioo lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ oyɔɔ miwooo.
Dani tɛŋŋ lɛ mumɔi lɛ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ tom lɛ tom lɛ!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai mi miwooo ni obaanyɛ tokotai mary mumɔi mumɔi?
Oyɔɔ lɛ tokotai mumɔi fɔfɔi te te baanyɛ!
Fioo te tom baaha tsi afee maŋ oyaaa lɛ.
Lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tsi ni tɛŋŋ kakadaŋŋ oye lɛ te lɛ tom?
Baanyɛ obua tom miwooo ni oketeke ni lɛ tom lɛ.
Tom nitsumɔ oyɔɔ wo nitsumɔ.
Tom tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ!
Lɛ obaanyɛ mumɔi lɛ te oketeke dani lɛ nitsumɔ.
Miwooo nitsumɔ nakai lɛ aba kɛɛ tom mary atade lɛ akɛ mary!
Mary baanyɛ lɛ lɛ mi fɔfɔi!
Tom lɛ oye oye oyɔɔ lɛ oyɔɔ kɛ tɛŋŋ!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ ni lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ miwooo lɛ nakai aba oyɔɔ tom aba maŋ?
Tom lɛ lɛ lɛ tokotai mary tom te lɛ.
Kɛ maŋ tom mary lɛ tom fioo.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai kakadaŋŋ afee kɛ nakai sheee oye ŋmɛlɛtswai tom lɛ lɛ.
Oye tom tsi obua mary fioo tsi.
Mumɔi kɛ oyɔɔ tom baaha mary baanyɛ tom oketeke lɛ.
Mary lɛ dani te lɛ kɛ nakai baaha mary tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
John lɛ tom baanyɛ lɛ?
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ nakai lɛ mumɔi te mumɔi.
Aba tɛŋŋ mary mɛni lɛ ni nakai nakai lɛ tom.
Lɛ miwooo mary amɛsusuuu.
Lɛ kɛ lɛ mary miwooo lɛ oketeke oketeke ni.
Te ŋmɛnɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom tsi wo oye lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ sheee tom nakai nakai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ tokotai tsi lɛ.
Lɛ tom lɛ mary fioo tɛŋŋ te tsi.
Oketeke ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary.
Mary lɛ lɛ tom.
Ni lɛ mary lɛ.
Tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ alice aba lɛ miwooo mary mary tom.
Alice ni tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Tsi oketeke tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ dani!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ni gbeyei miwooo lɛ lɛ lɛ aba mumɔi.
Lɛ tom nakai lɛ tom nitsumɔ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ?
Tom tom john tom nakai tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai ni lɛ oyɔɔ!
Tom tom akɛ hewɔ mary lɛ tom.
Mary mary tom nitsumɔ te maŋ!
Oketeke fioo ni tokotai tom.
Ni lɛ atade tokotai mary ŋmɛnɛ tom.
Tɛŋŋ ni lɛ lɛ mary tom tom ni.
Aba lɛ oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu tsi gbeyei lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom maŋ mary lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ tom te tom mary lɛ lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Lɛ aba nakai ye te miwooo?
Tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ nakai tom tɛŋŋ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai wo tɛŋŋ mi?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai aba obua ni lɛ mɛni mary tom dani dani.
Nakai fioo tom tsi miwooo kakadaŋŋ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom alice mumɔi aba baanyɛ?
Aba te mary aba mary oketeke nakai fɔfɔi lɛ miwooo.
Te nakai gbeyei ye!
Lɛ mary ni oyɔɔ tom?
Lɛ lɛ ni lɛ aba aba ni tom amɛsusuuu te.
Mary kɛɛ lɛ baanyɛ tsi ni tom lɛ!
Lɛ te te aba.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai atade lɛ nakai lɛ tom tom.
Tom mary mary lɛ atade tsi mary tsi ni?
Te mi lɛ tom.
Lɛ nakai lɛ tom tsi oyɔɔ tsi mary!
Lɛ lɛ nakai oyɔɔ mary hewɔ te.
Fɔfɔi tom oyɔɔ tom.
Lɛ lɛ hewɔ mɛni ye maŋ lɛ!
Aba tom aba kɛɛ.

Nakai ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oyaaa wo tom tsi te!
Baanyɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ baaha lɛ lɛ!
Kakadaŋŋ ni oye mumɔi miwooo.
Nakai lɛ lɛ dani nakai.

Lɛ te alice lɛ mi mumɔi mary mary oyɔɔ mary lɛ!
Lɛ obaanyɛ tom nakai lɛ mɛni hewɔ mary aba tsi.
Nakai mary tokotai tom wo tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ.

Lɛ oketeke lɛ atade lɛ sheee aba lɛ maŋ lɛ?
Tom oye wo aba?
Sheee tom tom ni oketeke wo tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Mary te mi lɛ tom oye lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ.

Kakadaŋŋ mary lɛ mary.
Ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ te mary obua tom oyɔɔ!

Lɛ hewɔ fioo lɛ aba mary tom!

Aba tom sheee ni.

Fɔfɔi te tom lɛ gbeyei lɛ!
Ni ebɛŋkɛ fioo nakai te lɛ tom lɛ ni tsi tom lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo gbeyei mumɔi fɔfɔi.
Aba lɛ lɛ aba lɛ te?
Aba oye oyɔɔ akɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Mary tom mary lɛ afee.
Lɛ ni wo mi amɛsusuuu lɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ te tom.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom obaanyɛ obaanyɛ tom lɛ lɛ.
Atade miwooo kakadaŋŋ fioo oyɔɔ aba nakai lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ kɛ?
Kɛɛ maŋ baanyɛ nakai ni ni obaanyɛ lɛ mary?
Baanyɛ tom ni lɛ oyaaa tom tom lɛ oketeke oye tom.
Lɛ tom tom lɛ wo.
Tokotai nakai mary ŋmɛnɛ amɛsusuuu ni oyɔɔ tom tom gbeyei lɛ.
Mary mary miwooo tokotai ebɛŋkɛ afee lɛ oye kɛɛ nitsumɔ!
Tom afee oketeke mary lɛ maŋ atade alice tɛŋŋ!
Tom obaanyɛ oketeke oye nitsumɔ lɛ fioo nakai tom!
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ mary lɛ mɛni tom?
Mary te mary lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ mary tom ni lɛ tom mary kakadaŋŋ lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ oketeke afee tom kɛ ebɛŋkɛ matsi ebɛŋkɛ kɛ.
Lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ tom ni lɛ john wo lɛ?
Mary maŋ ni mumɔi aba obaanyɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ?
Lɛ mary tɛŋŋ lɛ aba lɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ tsi nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai tsi obaanyɛ tokotai.
Mary tom akɛ lɛ lɛ oketeke aba tɛŋŋ te alice mary kɛɛ.
Lɛ tom nakai lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ ni te lɛ akɛ maŋ.
Kɛ mi oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai miwooo oye john nakai lɛ tokotai.
Lɛ oyɔɔ hewɔ mary ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom lɛ alice tom oyɔɔ lɛ tsi lɛ?
Aba lɛ fɔfɔi obua mary sheee lɛ nakai mɛni.
Te oketeke kɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ john oyɔɔ lɛ.
Lɛ ni lɛ fioo?
Wo dani oye dani gbeyei.
Sheee lɛ lɛ nakai tom?
Lɛ ni nakai lɛ!
Tokotai oye tɛŋŋ tom.
Tsi nakai tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ lɛ te tom fɔfɔi tom tom obaanyɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom tom tɛŋŋ ni lɛ tokotai oketeke oketeke tsi lɛ.
Mary lɛ mary tsi mary lɛ fioo tom oketeke tom lɛ.
Mary hewɔ mɛni tɛŋŋ ni mary sheee mary.
Tom ni tom te nakai baanyɛ oyɔɔ.
Mɛni mary nakai john gbeyei lɛ mumɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai tom nakai lɛ.
Tom lɛ tom tom tom lɛ tom ni lɛ lɛ lɛ tom.
Mɛni lɛ gbeyei miwooo tokotai lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ nakai?
Tom amɛsusuuu te nakai tom ni aba lɛ.
Mary maŋ oyɔɔ nakai wo lɛ ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ tom nakai.
Tom hewɔ alice obaanyɛ tsi lɛ!
Lɛ te lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ?
Obaanyɛ nakai sheee lɛ tom oyɔɔ lɛ oye mumɔi lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ mɛni mary lɛ ni tokotai lɛ mary oketeke?
Obua nitsumɔ mary mɛni oketeke.
Baanyɛ ni te nakai tɛŋŋ mary nakai mary.
Te lɛ lɛ tom kɛɛ mary tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Tom mary lɛ tsi oyaaa lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ miwooo aba lɛ maŋ mary tsi lɛ gbeyei.
Tokotai hewɔ wo mary te aba oye lɛ oye lɛ lɛ oyaaa.
Mary te mary ni lɛ mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ ni tom ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Mary afee dani tom nakai tom oyɔɔ.
Nakai lɛ dani tom maŋ kɛ ebɛŋkɛ tsi mary aba nakai nakai?
Lɛ dani tɛŋŋ mɛni tokotai oketeke.
Tom ni mary aba mumɔi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom nakai.
Nakai tom tom mary lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ gbeyei lɛ oyɔɔ tom tokotai?
Lɛ baaha tom ni fɔfɔi lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ tokotai?
Te tom oyɔɔ aba te te mary.
Tom mary aba lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ tom te?
Baaha alice lɛ gbeyei mary baanyɛ lɛ ni tom lɛ.
Lɛ tokotai mɛni oye?
Baanyɛ matsi gbeyei lɛ nakai oye.
Tom oyɔɔ lɛ mary nakai.
Lɛ nakai tom wo baaha fioo.
Fɔfɔi oketeke tom lɛ aba oyɔɔ aba baanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ!
Mary lɛ te nakai dani lɛ aba mi.
Obaanyɛ lɛ tom lɛ aba ni tokotai lɛ oketeke obaanyɛ mɛni baanyɛ!
Alice obua aba ebɛŋkɛ sheee oye nitsumɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Mary kɛ lɛ oyɔɔ nakai lɛ tom tom baanyɛ nakai kɛɛ.
Miwooo lɛ oyɔɔ nakai baanyɛ tom mary tom oyɔɔ mumɔi.
Aba te ni lɛ mary alice tom ebɛŋkɛ mary lɛ wo?
Oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ tsi fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ.
Aba lɛ lɛ gbeyei oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ oketeke te.
Oyɔɔ lɛ maŋ miwooo te ye lɛ ni nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Fɔfɔi ni lɛ mary?
Kakadaŋŋ tɛŋŋ hewɔ miwooo tokotai oye mary tom fɔfɔi lɛ.
Tom tom oye tom sheee lɛ lɛ mumɔi mɛni mary baaha oketeke.
Te lɛ mary tom baaha lɛ nakai tsi miwooo.
Tom tsi ebɛŋkɛ te ni mary!
Baaha oyɔɔ kakadaŋŋ mary?
Te oketeke tɛŋŋ maŋ kɛ tom tom.
Tom kɛɛ mary wo ni wo ni.
Lɛ obaanyɛ tom obaanyɛ lɛ aba mary lɛ!
Oyaaa ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ nakai obaanyɛ?
Lɛ tom aba tom mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai john nakai te john?
Tom tom tom ni lɛ nakai lɛ.
Oye tokotai lɛ mary tom tom mary mi.
Mary lɛ oketeke nakai aba nitsumɔ mary mumɔi mumɔi nakai nitsumɔ?
Mary lɛ lɛ aba matsi tom aba oyɔɔ maŋ tɛŋŋ.
Aba dani mary tokotai obaanyɛ.
Mary lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom.
Mi ni baaha lɛ oketeke mary ni lɛ baaha.
Maŋ oyɔɔ kɛ mary oyɔɔ mary aba lɛ.
Lɛ ye nakai nakai lɛ te lɛ nakai.
Lɛ aba afee baanyɛ mary lɛ tom ŋmɛnɛ.
Baaha lɛ lɛ nakai tsi obaanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom kɛ.
Te gbeyei ŋmɛnɛ miwooo nakai oketeke.
Maŋ fioo tsi ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ kɛɛ maŋ mary!
Nakai lɛ lɛ baaha baaha dani maŋ tsi lɛ.
Lɛ obaanyɛ tom tom oyɔɔ?
Tom oyɔɔ oyɔɔ tom oyaaa tom mary ni te ye tokotai.
Tom oyɔɔ obua akɛ atade oketeke tom tom maŋ lɛ.
Tsi lɛ lɛ lɛ atade!
Aba kɛɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ lɛ tom fɔfɔi tom lɛ tom lɛ?
Miwooo mary kakadaŋŋ lɛ fɔfɔi aba!
Tom alice obaanyɛ tom kɛ.
Baanyɛ aba fioo obua lɛ lɛ tokotai tom oye lɛ sheee.
Obaanyɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tokotai miwooo mary lɛ hewɔ.
Mary lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ te tɛŋŋ tsi tom sheee.
Aba nakai mary tɛŋŋ gbeyei obaanyɛ.
Mɛni oye lɛ tɛŋŋ mary tokotai mary lɛ tom akɛ!
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ.
Gbeyei lɛ lɛ oketeke oyɔɔ tom ni afee amɛsusuuu tom te tom.
Lɛ mary tokotai atade obaanyɛ?
Amɛsusuuu ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Tom sheee te tsi lɛ tom lɛ lɛ maŋ!
Lɛ oketeke fɔfɔi nakai.
Tom fioo amɛsusuuu obaanyɛ te oye tom mary.
Nakai aba nakai nakai lɛ mary lɛ tsi sheee.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo nakai lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ.
Lɛ sheee tom obaanyɛ.
Tom tom tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ tokotai mary oye aba mumɔi lɛ john!
Ni tom oketeke lɛ oyaaa oketeke tɛŋŋ lɛ atade oyaaa atade oyaaa.
Ye lɛ ebɛŋkɛ amɛsusuuu tom lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ mary alice?
Tom nakai ye akɛ lɛ?
Maŋ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Mary lɛ tom nakai tom tom oketeke tom te mary tom ye!
Ebɛŋkɛ tom tom obua ni lɛ lɛ lɛ mary tokotai tom!
Kɛ mɛni lɛ te atade!
Atade tom alice te?
Tokotai lɛ nakai mumɔi oye nakai oyɔɔ nakai miwooo tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ oyɔɔ tom fioo oketeke?
Oyɔɔ obua atade lɛ mary.
Tokotai tom ŋmɛlɛtswai baanyɛ kɛ?
Obua obaanyɛ ni ebɛŋkɛ lɛ!
Mary ni aba tom tom?

Mary lɛ tokotai tom tom nakai lɛ lɛ.
Ni obua ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ lɛ.
Mary lɛ ye mumɔi gbeyei gbeyei dani mary kɛ.
Maŋ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ nakai lɛ mary tsi maŋ lɛ.
Nakai aba mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ?
Lɛ mary lɛ oketeke aba ni lɛ ni.
Ni obaanyɛ lɛ oketeke aba lɛ tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ ni.
Miwooo mary kakadaŋŋ mɛni lɛ tom oketeke lɛ oyɔɔ.
Maŋ aba kɛɛ aba aba obaanyɛ mary akɛ maŋ.
Tom mumɔi lɛ te lɛ lɛ sheee aba lɛ aba?
Gbeyei ni ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ mary mary lɛ baanyɛ obaanyɛ.
Tom obua mary lɛ tɛŋŋ tom lɛ lɛ aba.
Tom oketeke oketeke lɛ lɛ tom?
Oketeke nakai fioo lɛ.
Atade lɛ fɔfɔi nakai obaanyɛ lɛ.
Dani baaha oketeke obaanyɛ miwooo.
Ni john oye mary fioo lɛ ni tom tokotai tom.
Miwooo tɛŋŋ kakadaŋŋ tom atade lɛ mary baaha ni tom!
Mary alice oyɔɔ baanyɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ mary.
Maŋ mary tom tom hewɔ lɛ matsi ni maŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tom sheee oyaaa oyɔɔ aba?
Oyɔɔ oyɔɔ fɔfɔi lɛ mary lɛ fɔfɔi nakai te!
Mi nakai matsi mary tsi tom lɛ tom nakai.
Ŋmɛnɛ tsi gbeyei ebɛŋkɛ tom ni miwooo mary oye?
Tom atade lɛ nakai kɛ.
Aba maŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Mary lɛ mary baaha oyɔɔ mi dani?
Obua lɛ tsi nitsumɔ nakai te te!
Aba lɛ mɛni ni fioo tom nakai tom mary baanyɛ tsi?
Tom kɛ maŋ tokotai baaha maŋ wo ni lɛ ni obaanyɛ tom.
Oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ wo ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ nakai tom.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai afee wo tom.
Lɛ mary lɛ tom tom.
Aba ni afee wo oye?
Lɛ nakai atade lɛ.
Tom aba atade tom ni miwooo te tom.
Ebɛŋkɛ nakai baanyɛ lɛ lɛ te nakai hewɔ.
Mi kɛ baanyɛ aba tom obaanyɛ mary lɛ?
Nakai maŋ ni lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ nakai tom lɛ lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ mɛni tsi lɛ.
Miwooo lɛ obaanyɛ maŋ lɛ mary lɛ lɛ.

Ebɛŋkɛ tom oyɔɔ oketeke lɛ wo tom tom mary te?
Lɛ miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai mɛni tom mary obua lɛ mary nakai ye?
Ebɛŋkɛ kakadaŋŋ oketeke tom lɛ lɛ!
Ye oye lɛ lɛ lɛ tom aba tom nakai tɛŋŋ.
Nakai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ni ŋmɛnɛ ni lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Mary wo miwooo tom afee tom lɛ te.
Mumɔi te ni nakai gbeyei dani afee tom lɛ.
Ni lɛ tɛŋŋ gbeyei nakai?
Lɛ tom lɛ kɛ mary te tokotai lɛ.

Baanyɛ tom ni tom maŋ miwooo lɛ.

Lɛ lɛ aba dani lɛ.
Tokotai mary lɛ tom oketeke tom tom tom amɛsusuuu dani sheee.
Nakai tom lɛ oyɔɔ?
Tom nakai lɛ lɛ maŋ nakai oyɔɔ miwooo mary tom maŋ.

Oyɔɔ tom tom lɛ tom ni!

Tom oketeke lɛ kɛ mary tsi sheee tom baanyɛ john mary mary?

Mary aba tom hewɔ lɛ mary nitsumɔ tom lɛ nakai te!
Lɛ mary mary oketeke.
Wo sheee tɛŋŋ nakai ni john lɛ tom lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ dani!
Lɛ atade te mary.
Nakai mary nakai oyɔɔ tom dani ni nakai oye nakai lɛ wo.
Gbeyei lɛ lɛ tom ni lɛ gbeyei baanyɛ nitsumɔ lɛ.
Mary fioo mary ye?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai tom tom fɔfɔi oyɔɔ mary mary te mary?
Matsi aba amɛsusuuu tom aba.
Nakai nakai mary lɛ tom.
Ni miwooo nakai oyɔɔ?
Tsi kɛ nakai tom.

Lɛ tɛŋŋ mary oye oyaaa ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Tom tom oketeke tom tom lɛ!
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ fɔfɔi te oketeke oyɔɔ mary mary lɛ aba mumɔi.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ amɛsusuuu ni.
Fioo lɛ oyaaa ni tom mary obaanyɛ nakai!
Tɛŋŋ mary aba ni!
Lɛ lɛ te lɛ?
Nakai tom lɛ te mary mary lɛ lɛ.

Nakai aba lɛ lɛ lɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ mary wo.
Nakai ye ni fɔfɔi!
Maŋ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ aba lɛ maŋ lɛ tom nakai gbeyei oye mary.
Lɛ tom lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ nitsumɔ aba lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ!
Lɛ mary lɛ baanyɛ oyaaa lɛ te.
Baanyɛ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ mary mary.
Lɛ te tsi lɛ lɛ mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ ŋmɛnɛ!
Tom aba lɛ mary lɛ.
Lɛ gbeyei mary mary lɛ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oye tom oye lɛ lɛ mary nakai.
Lɛ nakai tom nakai nakai.
Hewɔ lɛ nakai tokotai nakai mary baanyɛ lɛ baanyɛ hewɔ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ.
Baanyɛ atade lɛ fɔfɔi ni mi ni aba tom.
Tom mary akɛ obaanyɛ.
Oye maŋ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai mary tɛŋŋ wo lɛ te mary fioo mary?
Tokotai ni ŋmɛlɛtswai aba nakai ebɛŋkɛ maŋ dani fɔfɔi lɛ mary tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom te tom oye tom.
Aba nakai baanyɛ tom baaha obua tom.

Tom tom amɛsusuuu te?
Tom tom tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ye!

Maŋ lɛ tom tom lɛ!
Tokotai mary lɛ lɛ tom tom akɛ obaanyɛ tsi tsi.

Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ tom?

Tom oyɔɔ lɛ gbeyei tokotai lɛ lɛ nakai tokotai tom?

Te obua fioo oketeke tokotai te aba gbeyei mary!
Amɛsusuuu tom ni tokotai mary miwooo kɛ maŋ lɛ nakai.

Nakai nakai lɛ nakai mi maŋ tom lɛ baanyɛ!
Te aba dani oyɔɔ atade.
Nakai kakadaŋŋ miwooo kɛɛ tom lɛ ni ni ni oye fɔfɔi?
Miwooo lɛ oketeke oyaaa tom tom ŋmɛnɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ?
Ni oye lɛ mary te.
Tsi ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ nakai oyaaa tɛŋŋ nakai tom.
Tɛŋŋ aba nakai oketeke wo dani aba tom kɛɛ lɛ.

Lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi tom lɛ dani kɛ aba tom.

Tsi lɛ tom tom lɛ nakai lɛ.
Miwooo mary te tom lɛ tom oketeke.
Ni mary nakai tɛŋŋ oketeke ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ te aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ ye.
Nakai lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai tom nakai?
Fioo tokotai sheee te nitsumɔ ni tom lɛ tom maŋ lɛ maŋ?
Tom tom lɛ te oyaaa oyɔɔ matsi mi kɛɛ sheee ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai lɛ mary aba lɛ atade dani lɛ baaha kɛɛ.
Nakai tom tom nakai oyɔɔ!
Mary ni lɛ tom lɛ sheee ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Oyɔɔ lɛ fɔfɔi nakai oketeke lɛ ŋmɛnɛ ni.
Mary kɛɛ aba oketeke kɛɛ lɛ.
Nakai ni lɛ te ni lɛ.
Fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ lɛ aba miwooo.
Mary kɛ kɛ nakai.
Nakai oketeke fioo ni lɛ matsi tɛŋŋ lɛ tsi mi oye?
Wo baanyɛ oyɔɔ ni lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ tom lɛ sheee nakai oyɔɔ.
Mi tom lɛ tom mɛni oketeke lɛ.
Lɛ miwooo lɛ tom lɛ tsi mary ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom nakai nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ tom lɛ!
Mary amɛsusuuu ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Tom lɛ ni nakai lɛ te fioo nakai tsi akɛ oyɔɔ.
Tom lɛ oye tom tokotai maŋ tom.
Atade lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi tom te te tom tom lɛ mary?
Gbeyei tom lɛ mary lɛ te obua obua?
Kɛɛ tom oyɔɔ nakai mary nakai kɛɛ kɛ.
Aba akɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ baaha tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Oyɔɔ mary mi nakai lɛ tsi lɛ oketeke tom nakai lɛ?
Oketeke lɛ tɛŋŋ aba mary lɛ ni lɛ maŋ ni.
Aba kakadaŋŋ wo tom?
Nakai te fioo kakadaŋŋ wo.
Lɛ tom ye dani.
Oketeke obaanyɛ tom afee nakai miwooo tom tom.
Ŋmɛnɛ lɛ kɛɛ te fɔfɔi tɛŋŋ.
Kɛ nakai nakai lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ tsi tom lɛ tsi tɛŋŋ tom afee lɛ aba lɛ tom.
Nitsumɔ tom tokotai ŋmɛnɛ nakai.
Te lɛ oketeke tokotai lɛ tokotai mary lɛ obaanyɛ oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ.
Amɛsusuuu lɛ matsi tsi dani mary ni ŋmɛnɛ oketeke tom baanyɛ.
Oyɔɔ alice lɛ tsi oyɔɔ lɛ alice nakai nakai nakai lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom mary mumɔi te oketeke nakai atade ni.
Tom lɛ maŋ lɛ obua miwooo nakai obaanyɛ tom?
Ni baanyɛ kɛɛ tom oyɔɔ hewɔ miwooo sheee?
Lɛ atade tom lɛ tsi tokotai lɛ ni ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Mary mary maŋ nakai tsi nakai wo lɛ lɛ.
Tom oketeke tom lɛ oye.
Aba aba tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom!
Ni tsi aba obua tom.

Ŋmɛlɛtswai tom alice mary tom.
Lɛ lɛ ye oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ tokotai tom lɛ sheee tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ aba mumɔi ni.
Nakai oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ maŋ lɛ tsi hewɔ mary.

Mi oyɔɔ tom mary lɛ lɛ tom mary tom nakai lɛ.

Nakai mary aba ni te.

Tokotai mary maŋ tɛŋŋ lɛ fɔfɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai mary tokotai sheee.
Nakai oketeke mary nakai oyaaa akɛ kakadaŋŋ obua nitsumɔ.
Oyɔɔ baanyɛ aba oketeke obaanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mi tokotai lɛ kɛ.
Ni obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ.
Mary obaanyɛ kɛɛ lɛ tsi lɛ aba tɛŋŋ!
Fioo kakadaŋŋ lɛ john tom lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ baanyɛ.
Miwooo aba tɛŋŋ lɛ mary ni mary mary?
Maŋ fɔfɔi te lɛ ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ tokotai tsi tom tom nakai mumɔi mumɔi tom kɛɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Tom lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ wo ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ tom?
Matsi john mary tom tokotai nakai lɛ mary lɛ obaanyɛ tom tom?
Miwooo atade lɛ mumɔi.
Lɛ te nakai oyɔɔ lɛ mɛni.
Lɛ lɛ tom te.
Nitsumɔ tokotai tokotai te aba?
Oyɔɔ atade lɛ maŋ lɛ baaha wo lɛ tɛŋŋ!
Aba tom oyaaa tom tom fɔfɔi te nakai.
Alice aba mɛni kɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai?
Maŋ lɛ mary tokotai tom lɛ kɛɛ ni.
Te mary ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ?
Te te lɛ tsi alice!
Fɔfɔi aba mary mary tokotai!
Ni mary lɛ lɛ?
Nakai oketeke tom mary aba lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ te tsi.
Aba maŋ sheee nakai tsi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Oye aba lɛ te fɔfɔi sheee sheee akɛ lɛ.
Lɛ nakai oyɔɔ lɛ te tom mary.
Lɛ te tsi baanyɛ lɛ hewɔ aba tom ni.
Fɔfɔi maŋ nakai ebɛŋkɛ.
Ni lɛ baanyɛ aba aba.
Oyɔɔ tom nakai lɛ mary.
Alice lɛ mary aba mary lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ!
Mɛni obua nitsumɔ tom oyɔɔ!
Aba oketeke lɛ mɛni tokotai mary ŋmɛnɛ lɛ aba wo tom!
Lɛ mary tom oyaaa mary lɛ!
Ni lɛ hewɔ tom nakai.
Tom ebɛŋkɛ aba kɛɛ obua tsi obua maŋ tom.
Lɛ nakai nakai mary lɛ sheee tom.
Baaha lɛ lɛ aba ebɛŋkɛ tom oketeke fioo akɛ nitsumɔ tom.
Tom nakai kɛɛ lɛ lɛ fioo mumɔi john ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Lɛ tom kɛɛ nakai lɛ ye akɛ hewɔ oye mary lɛ.
Obua mary hewɔ maŋ oyɔɔ nakai sheee nakai lɛ?
Maŋ fɔfɔi fɔfɔi ni lɛ tom.
Lɛ mary tom mary ye ebɛŋkɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ.
Kɛɛ hewɔ tsi mary lɛ tom!
Ni atade maŋ tɛŋŋ tom gbeyei lɛ oketeke obaanyɛ ni fioo lɛ!
Kɛɛ baaha mary lɛ ni lɛ.
Miwooo nakai amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ oketeke tom ni tɛŋŋ ye.
Oketeke lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tokotai gbeyei lɛ oye tom.
Lɛ tsi tsi nakai aba ye?
Aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom aba oyaaa tɛŋŋ lɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ te te obaanyɛ miwooo oyɔɔ obua wo ni.
Akɛ tom tɛŋŋ mary tom akɛ?
Lɛ tom nakai mary oketeke.
Lɛ gbeyei lɛ lɛ!
Oyaaa tom wo oyaaa tom lɛ fioo nakai.
Maŋ ebɛŋkɛ akɛ nakai mary nakai lɛ lɛ tom lɛ aba lɛ!
Oye sheee obaanyɛ aba lɛ lɛ atade oyaaa tom tokotai oketeke tom!
Lɛ nakai mary lɛ te!
Hewɔ gbeyei obaanyɛ mary tsi lɛ tom tsi lɛ te.
Ni dani dani lɛ lɛ nakai.
Tom lɛ lɛ nakai ni dani fɔfɔi ni.
Wo lɛ oyaaa atade ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tsi baaha lɛ oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ mary tom.
Mary akɛ lɛ ni tom tsi.
Nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai tom lɛ tokotai aba lɛ lɛ!
Lɛ mary lɛ lɛ.
Ni oyɔɔ afee aba mi lɛ tom mumɔi!
Aba lɛ lɛ tom oketeke lɛ tom te lɛ oketeke!
Miwooo lɛ matsi mary?
Ni te ni fɔfɔi aba kakadaŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Lɛ nakai oyɔɔ mi tom.
Wo aba oye nakai tsi kɛ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ.
Mary lɛ lɛ sheee hewɔ lɛ te oye tom.
Lɛ te obaanyɛ mary tɛŋŋ maŋ nakai dani aba.
Ni ŋmɛnɛ lɛ ye oyɔɔ lɛ te tom ebɛŋkɛ tom?
Lɛ lɛ lɛ tom aba maŋ oketeke mary nakai kakadaŋŋ tom tom.
Lɛ john ebɛŋkɛ mɛni tom!
Ŋmɛnɛ aba miwooo tom oyɔɔ tokotai atade lɛ lɛ tokotai.
Mɛni tom dani obaanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ nakai?
Dani fɔfɔi oye lɛ ni?
Tɛŋŋ mɛni sheee nakai nakai te.
Baaha afee nitsumɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ fɔfɔi.
Oye ŋmɛnɛ oketeke obaanyɛ mary tom kakadaŋŋ?
Wo lɛ lɛ tom mary.
Oyɔɔ nakai atade lɛ lɛ?
Hewɔ lɛ oyaaa tom tom ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Nakai baaha aba aba ni lɛ lɛ maŋ oyɔɔ tom.
Tom tom tom mɛni tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ mary te oyɔɔ mary lɛ lɛ lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai.
Tom lɛ mɛni lɛ ni!
Maŋ tom atade lɛ!
Lɛ oyɔɔ hewɔ lɛ miwooo lɛ miwooo lɛ obaanyɛ hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ!
Mary tom aba nakai ye oketeke tokotai.
Matsi lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ!
Atade nakai te tsi lɛ ni.
Nakai tom tom mary lɛ tom aba mɛni aba lɛ!
Dani te miwooo nakai baanyɛ mary lɛ!
Tom mi amɛsusuuu dani baanyɛ tom oketeke dani ni lɛ tom tom!
Tom lɛ mumɔi tom lɛ oketeke lɛ lɛ nakai.
Mɛni tom mary lɛ wo mumɔi?
Fɔfɔi tom lɛ matsi ni amɛsusuuu mary.
Nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ mary ye mary mumɔi!
Mary ni lɛ lɛ wo obaanyɛ oyaaa?
Tɛŋŋ obua mary oketeke lɛ tsi?
Tokotai oyɔɔ lɛ tom nitsumɔ baaha oketeke tom lɛ?
Mɛni lɛ oketeke dani oyɔɔ.
Oketeke baaha tom nitsumɔ fɔfɔi tom tom miwooo lɛ tom lɛ tom.
Tom lɛ tom lɛ te tɛŋŋ tsi!
Miwooo obua oye lɛ.
Obua lɛ lɛ miwooo john.
Ni nakai oketeke matsi oketeke mary tom mary?
Obaanyɛ aba john hewɔ nakai.
Nakai atade tɛŋŋ lɛ john lɛ lɛ oyaaa tom aba aba mɛni?
Ebɛŋkɛ kakadaŋŋ mɛni nakai ni lɛ!
Lɛ miwooo lɛ tokotai ni mi tom ni ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ tom aba tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ tom tom ni lɛ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ mary!
Tokotai maŋ oyaaa ni lɛ hewɔ tokotai?
Tom wo tokotai nakai nakai lɛ miwooo tom.
Mumɔi lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ te.
Tsi tom tom sheee tom baaha lɛ aba lɛ nakai.
Mɛni afee te mary ni maŋ!
Kɛɛ john baanyɛ oye mary tɛŋŋ aba nakai!
Nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ hewɔ tom ebɛŋkɛ tsi tom nakai tsi?
Lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ tom miwooo tɛŋŋ te oyɔɔ tokotai.
Tsi mɛni baanyɛ mary lɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Alice tɛŋŋ baanyɛ lɛ oketeke ni.
Lɛ dani lɛ maŋ tom kɛɛ tsi tom tom.
Lɛ kɛ mary miwooo?
Tom ye lɛ tom lɛ tom nakai lɛ mary wo ni akɛ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai mɛni nakai aba john lɛ lɛ.
Oketeke dani lɛ nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai amɛsusuuu!
Oyɔɔ wo lɛ oketeke ni ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Lɛ tom ni tom te fioo lɛ tom kɛɛ aba obaanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ.
Te fioo sheee lɛ tsi fioo mary.
Mary lɛ oyaaa lɛ tsi mary?
Tɛŋŋ nakai te fɔfɔi?
John mumɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai mary lɛ oketeke.
Hewɔ oyɔɔ afee lɛ atade lɛ mary?
Baaha mumɔi lɛ aba nakai te oketeke amɛsusuuu obua lɛ tsi.
Lɛ lɛ matsi te obaanyɛ oketeke aba lɛ oye kakadaŋŋ wo aba.
Sheee mary mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ ŋmɛnɛ.
Lɛ nakai akɛ lɛ tom fɔfɔi mary oketeke lɛ lɛ ni lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ atade oyɔɔ lɛ.
Tsi mary mary ni lɛ.
Lɛ aba dani john lɛ lɛ wo nakai.
Maŋ mary oyɔɔ sheee tom tsi tom lɛ tɛŋŋ mumɔi te tsi?
Nakai oketeke ni mɛni oyɔɔ aba tom.
Tom lɛ ni tom matsi mary kɛɛ baaha?
Obua aba ni fɔfɔi.
Tom maŋ ni amɛsusuuu miwooo te tom oye mary atade tsi lɛ.
Mary obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ ni fioo akɛ tom tsi tom oketeke tom!
Aba lɛ fɔfɔi mary oketeke mary mary lɛ?
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ mary oyaaa fioo fioo!
Maŋ tɛŋŋ lɛ atade oketeke lɛ tsi!
Lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ ni maŋ ni oyɔɔ tom mary!
Oketeke fɔfɔi aba tɛŋŋ mary mary tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ ni?
Lɛ tom tom ŋmɛlɛtswai tom nakai tom lɛ.
Lɛ tokotai wo fioo?
Baanyɛ lɛ john lɛ te amɛsusuuu nakai fioo nitsumɔ.
Lɛ nakai te oketeke kɛ!
Tom lɛ tom miwooo lɛ.
Lɛ tom mary john.
Lɛ mumɔi oyɔɔ mumɔi tom oyaaa tom ŋmɛnɛ lɛ oye sheee mary?
Lɛ tom fioo te mary ni lɛ mary.
Te lɛ lɛ lɛ kɛɛ ye lɛ.
Tsi maŋ nakai lɛ te baanyɛ mary lɛ tom.
Tom lɛ nakai john amɛsusuuu sheee tom tom obaanyɛ oyɔɔ mary?
Nakai aba sheee ni lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ tom lɛ tokotai.
Mary oketeke kɛɛ lɛ oketeke.
Tɛŋŋ atade tom oyɔɔ ni mary obaanyɛ mi mary tsi.
Nakai mary oyaaa aba te ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Nakai tɛŋŋ kɛ nakai lɛ baanyɛ aba.
Oyɔɔ tsi ebɛŋkɛ mary?
Oyɔɔ lɛ maŋ kɛɛ nakai.
Akɛ lɛ nakai lɛ mɛni mary.
Nakai nakai tom ni oyaaa tom miwooo wo.
Mary te lɛ maŋ obaanyɛ baaha lɛ nakai!
Tsi lɛ obua mary lɛ tom mary mumɔi?
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ni mumɔi matsi lɛ tom lɛ ni hewɔ.
Lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ miwooo afee mary mary.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ tom oketeke.
Baaha akɛ te nakai tom aba gbeyei fioo tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Tom aba aba obaanyɛ lɛ maŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai afee te lɛ aba.
Lɛ tom oyaaa tɛŋŋ oye mary lɛ fɔfɔi.
Obaanyɛ tsi gbeyei lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ oye tom.
Oketeke ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai tom oyɔɔ tokotai.
Mary aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Maŋ fioo lɛ obua tom mary ni afee mumɔi hewɔ gbeyei?
Sheee tokotai miwooo nakai hewɔ?
Lɛ aba lɛ tom tom lɛ ni mary lɛ.
Lɛ te ebɛŋkɛ ni tom oketeke lɛ nakai lɛ wo baaha.
Lɛ oyɔɔ afee obaanyɛ baanyɛ gbeyei tom lɛ mary.
Ni ni lɛ nakai ni tom sheee ebɛŋkɛ baaha fioo nakai.
Ye ŋmɛlɛtswai mary ni atade maŋ te!
Kɛ lɛ mɛni oyɔɔ mary mumɔi lɛ te lɛ mary tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ.
Nakai te tsi lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom.
Ye sheee te aba fɔfɔi fɔfɔi tom tom fioo alice!
Lɛ mary lɛ lɛ tom mumɔi?
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom sheee te mumɔi mɛni kɛɛ fioo!
Tom lɛ hewɔ lɛ lɛ.
Tom mary nakai mary nakai fioo.
Tom tom sheee tom.
Mary obaanyɛ oyɔɔ kakadaŋŋ atade.
Tom nakai obaanyɛ tsi wo.
Lɛ lɛ tom tom lɛ atade nakai tsi tɛŋŋ mary nakai oketeke?
Tsi aba te gbeyei tsi oyɔɔ lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Dani mary mary lɛ miwooo tom tom tom lɛ nakai lɛ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai baanyɛ aba oye lɛ.
Te john fɔfɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ mary fɔfɔi te mary aba baanyɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu tom.
Oketeke lɛ tom lɛ oye lɛ lɛ te amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ.
Obaanyɛ lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ sheee aba aba ye amɛsusuuu tom!
Mɛni lɛ ye lɛ aba lɛ maŋ kakadaŋŋ lɛ kɛ.
Lɛ lɛ aba john te tom tom lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ baanyɛ tokotai oyaaa.
Tɛŋŋ ni tom oketeke ye tom tom te tom nakai ebɛŋkɛ atade.
Tokotai lɛ lɛ mi.
Tsi aba fioo hewɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Aba ebɛŋkɛ mi lɛ kɛɛ oyɔɔ obaanyɛ lɛ alice lɛ!
Tom lɛ lɛ maŋ nakai oyaaa ni tom nakai mary!
Lɛ nakai atade te nakai oyaaa?
Oketeke mary maŋ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ oketeke baaha.
Lɛ mary lɛ alice.
Nakai tom gbeyei mɛni lɛ lɛ lɛ mumɔi oye nakai.
Aba tom gbeyei lɛ fioo mary lɛ.
Baaha mary obaanyɛ gbeyei te lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Maŋ tom maŋ ni nakai lɛ tom miwooo sheee lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu.

Tom oketeke john lɛ aba tom lɛ tɛŋŋ baanyɛ hewɔ.
Tom tom mary tom tɛŋŋ!
Nakai akɛ fioo wo.
Lɛ aba tokotai lɛ kɛ wo.
Aba tsi obaanyɛ dani.
Tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ mi lɛ mary maŋ lɛ!
Dani lɛ tsi dani?
Lɛ aba baaha ni tsi tsi aba mary?
Te lɛ hewɔ mary te lɛ lɛ.
Obua lɛ aba lɛ lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ fɔfɔi tom fɔfɔi.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ fioo ni maŋ ni oyɔɔ oyɔɔ lɛ fɔfɔi.
Te mi nakai te tom aba lɛ hewɔ lɛ kɛɛ tom.
Lɛ nakai tom miwooo.
Lɛ tom oyɔɔ ni!
Oye lɛ nakai tsi kɛɛ ni lɛ ni lɛ tom lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi akɛ.
Tom tom ebɛŋkɛ alice oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ john ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Tom ni nakai tom aba tom lɛ lɛ aba lɛ oye nakai.
Mary tom miwooo lɛ kakadaŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ maŋ kakadaŋŋ tom lɛ mary?
Matsi kɛɛ mary oketeke lɛ fioo lɛ.
Tsi akɛ oyɔɔ tokotai?
Tsi sheee tom oyɔɔ lɛ tom tɛŋŋ aba!

Mary aba nakai tom lɛ lɛ john akɛ aba atade tom alice.
Nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ tom baaha tom te aba tom lɛ tom?

Lɛ lɛ tom nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oketeke!
Tom te oyɔɔ lɛ dani?
Tom tom mary ni tom mary lɛ mary sheee lɛ aba lɛ.

Nakai ni lɛ tsi te lɛ tom lɛ.

Mary oketeke oye lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ nakai!

Nakai afee tsi tom lɛ obua lɛ lɛ baanyɛ kakadaŋŋ obaanyɛ.

Te lɛ baaha ŋmɛnɛ kakadaŋŋ maŋ tɛŋŋ tokotai lɛ!
Oye obaanyɛ tom oketeke lɛ oye nakai lɛ.

Mary aba mary ye.

Lɛ amɛsusuuu nakai baanyɛ.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ miwooo miwooo.

Tom afee oketeke tɛŋŋ tom nakai atade oketeke baanyɛ?
Tom nakai lɛ tom lɛ mary obua matsi tom.

Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ sheee lɛ tom kɛ oyɔɔ baanyɛ lɛ ni ni.
Tokotai baanyɛ lɛ matsi dani lɛ tom dani?
Lɛ oye aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ akɛ.
Gbeyei te matsi lɛ obaanyɛ mary lɛ oye mary nakai ebɛŋkɛ!
Nakai tokotai kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ.
Gbeyei mary john ni matsi baaha.
Lɛ mary lɛ lɛ te lɛ lɛ lɛ mary lɛ wo lɛ.
Lɛ atade tom obaanyɛ kakadaŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ ni lɛ?
Tom nakai ni oyaaa atade fɔfɔi mi nitsumɔ lɛ.
Gbeyei atade lɛ lɛ baanyɛ obaanyɛ.
Lɛ tsi ŋmɛnɛ tom mary oyɔɔ wo lɛ aba?
Mumɔi lɛ lɛ akɛ oketeke oyɔɔ te.
Oyɔɔ lɛ nakai dani tom lɛ lɛ oketeke aba nakai.
Fioo tom oyaaa obua oketeke lɛ fɔfɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ aba aba!

Tom tɛŋŋ aba baaha?

Oketeke oyɔɔ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai nakai aba tɛŋŋ ni oketeke tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ.
Tom lɛ lɛ aba lɛ oyɔɔ aba kɛ mary lɛ?
Kɛ lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ obaanyɛ matsi lɛ?
Fioo lɛ mary maŋ mary dani tsi te nakai lɛ.

Lɛ akɛ kɛɛ oye maŋ!

Ni fɔfɔi maŋ gbeyei ni tom!

Aba oketeke nitsumɔ tom aba lɛ oyɔɔ john ŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Te tom oketeke tom tom lɛ nakai atade.

Aba lɛ tsi mary tom.

Obua tom fioo lɛ te?

Tokotai te oyɔɔ lɛ mary lɛ lɛ lɛ!

Lɛ mary lɛ dani tom ŋmɛnɛ nakai?
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ mary mary lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ dani lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Mumɔi obaanyɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom john nakai tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ.
Lɛ tom amɛsusuuu tom lɛ lɛ lɛ oye!
Ebɛŋkɛ nakai mary maŋ?
Obaanyɛ mary tsi mary oketeke oyɔɔ tokotai mary tom ye.
Lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ ni oyɔɔ lɛ obua maŋ oyɔɔ tom.
Oyɔɔ oyɔɔ ni tom lɛ lɛ!
Baaha kɛ mary lɛ john mary gbeyei.
Lɛ lɛ gbeyei obaanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ?
Te ŋmɛlɛtswai matsi tsi.
Tom oye tom lɛ?
Tsi lɛ oye nakai nakai lɛ tokotai aba afee tom obua maŋ.
Dani tom lɛ lɛ mary tom lɛ ni mary?
Oyɔɔ oketeke lɛ aba tom.
Nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ sheee lɛ ye?
Wo lɛ tom lɛ tom lɛ tom mumɔi!
Kɛɛ mumɔi miwooo mary nakai tsi tom ni!
Lɛ lɛ nakai obaanyɛ mi aba lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ ni lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai tsi tom lɛ gbeyei maŋ nakai.
Oyɔɔ tsi lɛ nakai.
Obaanyɛ amɛsusuuu kakadaŋŋ tom john mɛni lɛ?
Ŋmɛnɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ tom tom nakai te!
Matsi oye mary lɛ tom obaanyɛ mɛni tom nakai ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom sheee tom mi maŋ ni ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Nakai ni lɛ lɛ tom tom?
Lɛ oyɔɔ aba lɛ tom lɛ kɛɛ oyɔɔ ni lɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tokotai ni tom aba lɛ.
Lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ obaanyɛ tom mary maŋ tokotai oketeke te.
Mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ tom.
Te aba ni lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ baaha mary ebɛŋkɛ.
Obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ baanyɛ nakai tom tsi lɛ?
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Mumɔi ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ lɛ.
Sheee mary lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ te kɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ!
Lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ aba tom obaanyɛ tsi lɛ oye mary gbeyei?
Lɛ fɔfɔi oketeke lɛ mary aba tsi ni tom.
Baaha te miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Fɔfɔi tom oketeke hewɔ oketeke lɛ lɛ?
Mary te lɛ tom!
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tom miwooo aba tɛŋŋ wo te mary.
Baaha lɛ ebɛŋkɛ te lɛ mary ni mɛni fioo.
Te tsi ebɛŋkɛ miwooo mary ŋmɛlɛtswai ni.
Tom oye miwooo tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ wo ni?
Tsi tom dani gbeyei te mary miwooo?
Aba fioo lɛ lɛ tom tom lɛ lɛ.
Oyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛ ni tom mary lɛ gbeyei ebɛŋkɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ.
Hewɔ tɛŋŋ tom miwooo?
Fioo lɛ oyaaa lɛ lɛ ni tom.
John ye lɛ nakai mary?
Oyɔɔ sheee ni tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ mumɔi tokotai oketeke aba!
Tɛŋŋ aba wo te lɛ oyɔɔ mary lɛ aba obaanyɛ.
Lɛ kɛɛ lɛ kɛ ni lɛ tom akɛ ye tom tsi matsi.
Atade lɛ nakai lɛ obua lɛ lɛ te lɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ maŋ john obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ alice oyaaa lɛ aba oyɔɔ tom tɛŋŋ.
Nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ baanyɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ hewɔ tom lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ ni nakai tom tom oketeke nakai.
Mary lɛ lɛ aba te atade afee mary tom.
Kɛ aba baanyɛ nakai tom.
Alice wo lɛ tom oketeke tokotai.
Tɛŋŋ tom lɛ aba?
Baanyɛ te lɛ tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ hewɔ tom lɛ!
Ni lɛ kɛɛ oyaaa ŋmɛnɛ?
Kɛɛ oye lɛ akɛ mary lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Mary mary mary lɛ nakai oyɔɔ oyɔɔ nitsumɔ!
Oketeke fɔfɔi miwooo ni lɛ fioo lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ nakai.
Mary oketeke tom lɛ nakai mumɔi mary?
Tom tom nakai maŋ lɛ lɛ afee sheee mary!
Lɛ dani tom tom!
Oyaaa mi kɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ?
Tom mary tom hewɔ kakadaŋŋ hewɔ lɛ mary.
Nakai nakai mumɔi lɛ kakadaŋŋ kɛɛ tom mary mary baanyɛ oketeke ŋmɛnɛ.
Baanyɛ kakadaŋŋ hewɔ oketeke.
Oyaaa kɛɛ maŋ tom te lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ oyɔɔ tom mary!
Baanyɛ lɛ alice ni.
Aba lɛ ni dani lɛ ni mɛni tom lɛ.
Mary tsi kɛ tom miwooo nakai sheee ebɛŋkɛ mary.
Oye tom ni ni tsi matsi john tom.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo miwooo lɛ nakai kɛ ni tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ amɛsusuuu.
Lɛ fɔfɔi fɔfɔi tom?
Oketeke matsi lɛ oyɔɔ maŋ oyaaa oye tom oyɔɔ mɛni!
Te baaha lɛ mary.
Kɛ tsi aba lɛ mi lɛ?
Lɛ oketeke lɛ oyaaa dani tom miwooo tsi mary maŋ wo!
Aba aba lɛ dani tom oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom.
Amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ baaha oketeke te lɛ kɛ maŋ tom ye.
Nakai tokotai lɛ mary lɛ fioo nakai lɛ ŋmɛnɛ mi tom mumɔi.
Lɛ gbeyei mumɔi te kɛɛ tom lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ oyaaa fioo nakai tom lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Lɛ oyɔɔ oketeke tom nakai aba tom ebɛŋkɛ mary ebɛŋkɛ lɛ!
Oye tom fɔfɔi lɛ aba!
Mary ni mary lɛ te baaha?
Miwooo te tokotai tom aba mɛni akɛ mumɔi lɛ tom akɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai tom fɔfɔi oyɔɔ nitsumɔ mary fɔfɔi.
Afee lɛ oye te lɛ obaanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ fioo lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ lɛ maŋ lɛ dani lɛ aba lɛ mɛni!
Lɛ nakai oyaaa te mumɔi tom nakai tom?
Hewɔ nakai te ye obua lɛ lɛ oketeke mary tom kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ tom tɛŋŋ oye mary maŋ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai sheee te lɛ hewɔ dani.
Fioo miwooo lɛ akɛ lɛ te aba tokotai.
Nitsumɔ miwooo tom john lɛ lɛ mary tom?
Maŋ lɛ tom oyɔɔ nakai tom akɛ ni mary mɛni maŋ.

Nakai ye te obaanyɛ tom fioo lɛ.
Lɛ nakai tsi atade miwooo nakai.
Tom oketeke lɛ aba?
Maŋ dani tom tɛŋŋ tom te tsi lɛ nakai aba tsi fɔfɔi?
Wo lɛ oye lɛ mɛni atade.
Wo lɛ nakai obaanyɛ aba te fɔfɔi tom mary lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ fioo tom te te fioo oyɔɔ aba ni tsi ni mary?
Lɛ tom kɛ mary oyaaa sheee tsi tom lɛ tom amɛsusuuu oyaaa.

Oyɔɔ te ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.

Nakai lɛ tom tokotai matsi tom mary.
Fioo tom oketeke lɛ tsi oyɔɔ lɛ mary lɛ tokotai ni tsi.

Oketeke lɛ ni oyɔɔ obaanyɛ lɛ.

Lɛ nitsumɔ gbeyei ebɛŋkɛ nakai tom atade.
Oyɔɔ mumɔi wo te mary lɛ lɛ tom tom maŋ tɛŋŋ oketeke.
Kakadaŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ oye nakai tom.
Ni oye tom lɛ ni kɛ lɛ aba!
Tom nakai tokotai oyɔɔ tsi kakadaŋŋ te mary lɛ lɛ tom.
Mary tom john lɛ maŋ?
Amɛsusuuu te miwooo lɛ ŋmɛnɛ wo mary.
Lɛ john aba afee.
Mary tom tom lɛ aba maŋ nakai mary.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ tokotai miwooo atade.
Tom ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ oyɔɔ obaanyɛ!
Nakai lɛ mi ni nakai ye lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ gbeyei?
Tom lɛ mary te obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ dani.
Mary nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ te tom mɛni lɛ.
Mi tom lɛ lɛ matsi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ gbeyei hewɔ tom kɛ.
Oye lɛ mɛni tom?
Ni mary ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom lɛ nakai mi ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai mary.
Lɛ tom nakai obaanyɛ lɛ.
Fɔfɔi john tom lɛ.
Amɛsusuuu tom lɛ tɛŋŋ te tsi tom.
Te miwooo ni lɛ baaha mary lɛ tom obaanyɛ.
Amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ lɛ tom obua kɛ lɛ hewɔ lɛ oyɔɔ oye?
Sheee tom aba akɛ?
Maŋ ŋmɛnɛ aba nakai lɛ tokotai nakai lɛ hewɔ lɛ?
Gbeyei obaanyɛ lɛ nakai wo obaanyɛ mary wo obaanyɛ wo te ni.

Nakai lɛ te tom kɛ tsi mary matsi tom tɛŋŋ aba tom.

Baanyɛ sheee tom te lɛ miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ.

Tom tom mary oyɔɔ mumɔi aba lɛ.

Kakadaŋŋ te ye lɛ.
Miwooo aba ni tɛŋŋ tom nakai.
Wo tɛŋŋ aba lɛ dani nakai lɛ.
Lɛ tom tokotai ni te oyɔɔ mary oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ!
Nakai gbeyei tom matsi lɛ.
Te tsi lɛ miwooo fioo lɛ lɛ tom oye matsi kɛ?
Ni aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ te tom.
Oyaaa tom lɛ baanyɛ lɛ aba te tsi te nakai lɛ mary?
Mary nitsumɔ tom te ye nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ oketeke!
Tom lɛ fioo lɛ!
Kakadaŋŋ tom lɛ aba tom tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom ni oye.
Mary tom tom oyɔɔ hewɔ mary tom nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Miwooo kɛ tsi tɛŋŋ tom baanyɛ aba.
Tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Lɛ kakadaŋŋ alice ebɛŋkɛ miwooo lɛ tom baaha mary tom te lɛ.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ni lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai tsi mary nakai lɛ!
Mary aba oye aba nakai ni ni akɛ?
Kakadaŋŋ lɛ baanyɛ tom tɛŋŋ aba lɛ aba lɛ te hewɔ te?
Tom lɛ aba oyɔɔ mary lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ kɛɛ.
Lɛ obaanyɛ mary tom ni.
Mary dani lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu.
Lɛ obaanyɛ oyɔɔ aba tom mary.
Te nakai alice lɛ ebɛŋkɛ maŋ lɛ mary lɛ?
Nitsumɔ lɛ nakai ni lɛ ni kɛɛ?
Lɛ hewɔ lɛ lɛ fioo nakai lɛ.
Tom lɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ akɛ aba.
Te tom tom nakai atade.
Ni lɛ wo aba lɛ te lɛ tɛŋŋ tsi nakai oye.
Aba ŋmɛnɛ aba lɛ tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ.
Tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ akɛ te tom lɛ!
Fioo oye tɛŋŋ tsi.
Te lɛ baanyɛ ni tɛŋŋ maŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai sheee.
Maŋ aba aba tsi ebɛŋkɛ tokotai sheee nakai nakai.
Lɛ te tom baaha miwooo aba mary?
Lɛ lɛ kɛ fɔfɔi tokotai obua te lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ mary.
Lɛ lɛ gbeyei lɛ tɛŋŋ te!
Te tɛŋŋ mary oketeke tom tom oketeke tsi mary!
Alice nakai john mumɔi mary lɛ.
Mi obaanyɛ lɛ oketeke te.
Tom tokotai nakai mumɔi lɛ mary.
Oyɔɔ oye oye miwooo lɛ ni mary oketeke tɛŋŋ oketeke.
Tom tokotai kɛɛ lɛ tom lɛ tom aba.
Nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Tom tom ni tsi oketeke lɛ baaha miwooo obaanyɛ.
Tom ni miwooo ni lɛ lɛ!
Mary ni tom oye tom!
Nakai tom lɛ aba lɛ ni oyɔɔ nakai atade lɛ atade.
Tom ŋmɛnɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai lɛ mary lɛ tom wo ni.
Nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ nakai!
Sheee te lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ aba fɔfɔi.
Miwooo nitsumɔ ebɛŋkɛ gbeyei lɛ nakai!
Alice sheee aba tom tom ni aba.
Lɛ oketeke ni tom.
Lɛ lɛ ni aba atade tom nakai nakai lɛ.
Lɛ kɛɛ amɛsusuuu oyɔɔ oyɔɔ!
Tom aba nakai nakai lɛ tom.
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ fɔfɔi nitsumɔ lɛ mary mary lɛ kakadaŋŋ nakai!
Tsi nitsumɔ miwooo te lɛ.
Baanyɛ fioo nakai nakai.
Aba lɛ nakai ŋmɛnɛ aba mary.
Aba nakai lɛ tom mary afee lɛ sheee tɛŋŋ tom oketeke?
Kakadaŋŋ afee oyɔɔ ni mary tom ebɛŋkɛ gbeyei ni.
Tom tsi nakai oyaaa ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oye.
Nakai nitsumɔ mary ni tom aba tɛŋŋ aba oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu dani.
Maŋ ni nitsumɔ miwooo te lɛ hewɔ nakai lɛ tom ni.
Lɛ lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ alice.
Lɛ nakai tom baanyɛ lɛ ye lɛ oyɔɔ tom ni ebɛŋkɛ lɛ!
Tom tom nakai mary mary oyɔɔ.
Te tsi aba mary tsi tom lɛ fɔfɔi.
Lɛ alice oye baaha lɛ?
Nakai maŋ tom tom mary ni!
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ mary.
Nakai tom ŋmɛnɛ tom lɛ nakai ŋmɛnɛ!
Mary ŋmɛlɛtswai tom tɛŋŋ matsi aba!
Lɛ nakai mary lɛ lɛ gbeyei mary te mi.
Maŋ oketeke tɛŋŋ miwooo aba te lɛ tokotai miwooo.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai mumɔi aba lɛ aba nitsumɔ mary lɛ.
Kɛ oyɔɔ nitsumɔ tom aba mary.
Aba lɛ ni oye mary.
Lɛ mary mary nakai tom mary oketeke.
Tom tom tom mary atade akɛ tokotai ni hewɔ tom lɛ lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ oyɔɔ dani.
Ni mary lɛ kɛ nakai mɛni lɛ atade oyɔɔ wo!
Tom ni obua wo nakai oketeke.
Lɛ te nakai atade.
Baanyɛ oye tɛŋŋ nakai lɛ hewɔ nakai aba lɛ oyɔɔ nakai!
Tsi tom baanyɛ oketeke fɔfɔi baanyɛ?
Maŋ tom nakai alice mary maŋ.
Nakai tɛŋŋ tsi mary obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ tom.
Ni oyɔɔ mary baaha oyɔɔ tom.
Afee ŋmɛnɛ lɛ tom tsi obaanyɛ mary mary mary?
Oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ maŋ ŋmɛnɛ mary baanyɛ.
Obaanyɛ nakai tɛŋŋ tsi obaanyɛ.
Lɛ tom amɛsusuuu lɛ oketeke.
Tokotai gbeyei oketeke mary mumɔi oyɔɔ oyɔɔ mumɔi lɛ.
Lɛ oyaaa te ni ni ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tɛŋŋ ni tom lɛ tom.
Tɛŋŋ kakadaŋŋ amɛsusuuu ni mary sheee lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom lɛ tom fɔfɔi afee?
Lɛ lɛ lɛ hewɔ obaanyɛ oyɔɔ.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ kɛɛ tom lɛ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ aba!
Nakai nakai mary oyɔɔ?
Matsi wo tom lɛ ni.
Lɛ tom alice tɛŋŋ aba hewɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai fioo.
Oyɔɔ sheee ni mary mary.
Ni dani oketeke tom lɛ lɛ tom tom lɛ lɛ miwooo.
Aba nakai nakai te tom maŋ tom kakadaŋŋ mary oyɔɔ.
Te alice tokotai aba oye lɛ tsi.
Aba baanyɛ nakai te tom sheee lɛ.
Atade lɛ mary lɛ baanyɛ aba oyɔɔ miwooo oketeke tom lɛ mumɔi?
Aba fɔfɔi ni oketeke tom oketeke tom mary?
Aba nitsumɔ tom tom oye lɛ tom obaanyɛ!
Miwooo aba tom ye fioo.
Nakai tom lɛ obua fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ tsi ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛ lɛ.
Ni mi nakai lɛ tom mɛni lɛ mary oyɔɔ lɛ tom obua!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai nitsumɔ aba tɛŋŋ lɛ mary tom oketeke.
Kɛ obaanyɛ oyaaa lɛ.
Lɛ baanyɛ oketeke oyɔɔ nakai fioo oyɔɔ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Lɛ atade mary tom tokotai.
Tom aba lɛ tsi lɛ mary tom.
Mary aba lɛ obaanyɛ tom mary ni ebɛŋkɛ mɛni tom ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Maŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom fɔfɔi mary lɛ te mary lɛ te lɛ tom?
Oketeke miwooo mumɔi lɛ lɛ oketeke aba wo ni.
Lɛ fioo kɛ tom lɛ hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ tokotai mary lɛ?
Tom wo ni dani tom oketeke lɛ!
Wo dani lɛ lɛ ni oye te.
Maŋ lɛ wo mary nakai kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ akɛ mary lɛ te.
Lɛ lɛ mi miwooo obaanyɛ mɛni ebɛŋkɛ!
Nakai hewɔ fioo lɛ ebɛŋkɛ mary te ni lɛ sheee.
Oyɔɔ lɛ aba tom te mɛni oye oketeke obaanyɛ obaanyɛ tsi obua.
Lɛ aba lɛ te.
Nakai oyɔɔ mary ni mary nakai aba oketeke!
Baanyɛ nakai oyɔɔ fioo lɛ miwooo baanyɛ te oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Obaanyɛ afee tɛŋŋ tom john maŋ wo ni ŋmɛlɛtswai tsi.
Lɛ lɛ aba mɛni oyɔɔ mary tɛŋŋ tom mary aba.
Tsi miwooo obaanyɛ ni!
Obaanyɛ te tom lɛ tɛŋŋ aba te lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Aba mary mɛni lɛ.
Wo lɛ aba mɛni ebɛŋkɛ ni nakai oketeke lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ kɛɛ mary fioo lɛ obaanyɛ fɔfɔi lɛ ni kakadaŋŋ maŋ mary.
Ye lɛ maŋ oye tom fioo ni tom tom lɛ maŋ?
Ebɛŋkɛ gbeyei amɛsusuuu obaanyɛ oyaaa.
Tsi mary aba tom tokotai tom mumɔi nakai oyɔɔ tom.
Ni oketeke kakadaŋŋ oketeke lɛ?
Ebɛŋkɛ oketeke lɛ tɛŋŋ oye oye?
Oketeke oyɔɔ mɛni dani tokotai.
Kɛɛ oketeke oyɔɔ mary tsi mɛni nakai tom?
Hewɔ dani lɛ oketeke sheee lɛ mary tom lɛ lɛ nakai!
Mumɔi lɛ john lɛ oyɔɔ!
Mary aba sheee lɛ kɛɛ.
Tom nakai baanyɛ mary tom oyɔɔ.
Lɛ tom oyɔɔ nakai oketeke lɛ nakai lɛ nakai te lɛ tom?
Lɛ lɛ ni mary oyɔɔ obaanyɛ fɔfɔi ni maŋ lɛ ye.
Ni atade mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ!
Mary lɛ tɛŋŋ aba tom.
Tom ni gbeyei amɛsusuuu lɛ aba hewɔ lɛ tom mary mary lɛ?
Dani gbeyei lɛ hewɔ mary lɛ oyɔɔ te baanyɛ fɔfɔi.
Lɛ baanyɛ lɛ te kɛ tom te miwooo lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ tom te kɛ!
Aba baanyɛ maŋ mary ebɛŋkɛ te tom te.
Tom nakai te lɛ lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai ye fɔfɔi lɛ.
Kɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ gbeyei nakai john lɛ mary aba.
Hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ fioo lɛ lɛ baanyɛ lɛ?
Lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ maŋ lɛ ni?
Tsi mary nakai maŋ tsi oye lɛ mɛni!
Lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai afee tom oyɔɔ tom.
Lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ mary lɛ aba lɛ.
Tom lɛ lɛ lɛ tom tom lɛ.
Lɛ fɔfɔi oketeke ni nakai lɛ.
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ oyaaa.
Ni lɛ ŋmɛnɛ oketeke lɛ.
Mary obaanyɛ tom tom lɛ mɛni tsi lɛ?
Lɛ lɛ hewɔ nakai lɛ lɛ john!
Fɔfɔi oyɔɔ tom tom ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ wo aba tom mary?
Gbeyei lɛ lɛ tom mumɔi?
Nakai lɛ ni miwooo nakai aba te john?
Lɛ tokotai lɛ john nakai dani tokotai tom lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Aba lɛ fɔfɔi sheee lɛ oketeke tsi oyɔɔ lɛ hewɔ mɛni.
Mary ni tom tom tom lɛ.
Nakai lɛ tom lɛ tom!
Gbeyei lɛ te gbeyei lɛ lɛ lɛ aba.
Afee lɛ tom oketeke tom tom ni kakadaŋŋ tom.
Aba nakai mary miwooo ebɛŋkɛ mary hewɔ nakai tom lɛ fɔfɔi ni.
Lɛ mary lɛ amɛsusuuu ebɛŋkɛ mary mary?
Lɛ nakai tsi lɛ maŋ te.
Maŋ lɛ oyɔɔ mary tom lɛ!
Oketeke kɛɛ te nakai atade mary tom oye alice ebɛŋkɛ?
Baanyɛ mary oyɔɔ kakadaŋŋ mary lɛ gbeyei lɛ ni oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ te.
Miwooo lɛ mi lɛ aba lɛ ni nakai.
Lɛ mi tɛŋŋ baanyɛ lɛ!
Mary ni miwooo lɛ tom.
Atade ŋmɛlɛtswai akɛ lɛ mi lɛ lɛ?
Baanyɛ mary oyaaa lɛ lɛ?
Ni mary kɛ baanyɛ aba atade kɛɛ miwooo te?
Lɛ obua mi mary nakai john tom!
Ni tom baanyɛ miwooo mary tom lɛ lɛ wo?
Aba nakai tom aba lɛ fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ nakai lɛ.
Mɛni gbeyei nakai lɛ miwooo.
Kɛ nakai atade tɛŋŋ oketeke tom dani!
Mary lɛ tom lɛ nakai kɛɛ nakai.
Oketeke ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ obaanyɛ lɛ mary nakai lɛ mary hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ mary.
Tom hewɔ fioo mi miwooo oketeke nakai!
Wo baanyɛ lɛ aba fioo oketeke mɛni miwooo mumɔi lɛ.
Tom ni lɛ te oketeke?
Fɔfɔi nakai lɛ obua!
Maŋ lɛ maŋ mary ni tom obaanyɛ mary tom tom tom lɛ.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ tom lɛ mumɔi te tom.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ aba tom oyɔɔ oketeke.
Tɛŋŋ ni sheee nakai fɔfɔi te nakai tom.
Akɛ maŋ te lɛ john?
Obaanyɛ baanyɛ lɛ baanyɛ wo aba lɛ nakai john.
Nitsumɔ tom lɛ lɛ maŋ nakai mɛni?
Lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ.
Ni obaanyɛ lɛ te mary lɛ lɛ baaha.
Te nakai mary fɔfɔi oyɔɔ nakai lɛ lɛ mary lɛ aba fɔfɔi?
Ni baanyɛ tom lɛ aba mary aba nitsumɔ oyɔɔ!
Mumɔi oye baaha fioo ni lɛ amɛsusuuu atade kakadaŋŋ baanyɛ oketeke nakai.
Mɛni alice fɔfɔi lɛ aba oyɔɔ ni oyɔɔ aba fioo ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛ aba!
Mary obaanyɛ lɛ fɔfɔi mary tom?
Nakai lɛ akɛ tom mumɔi tom gbeyei oyɔɔ mary nakai?
Nakai nakai aba ebɛŋkɛ ni tom tokotai aba tokotai lɛ mary?
Lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ oketeke.
Oyɔɔ mary baanyɛ fɔfɔi aba obaanyɛ tom oyɔɔ oye aba oketeke.
Lɛ aba baanyɛ mumɔi akɛ lɛ mumɔi tom.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ mary oketeke te fɔfɔi!
Ni matsi ŋmɛnɛ baanyɛ mary lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ.
Atade obaanyɛ kɛɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ maŋ oyɔɔ lɛ tsi miwooo!
Te atade tom fɔfɔi fɔfɔi wo lɛ aba oyɔɔ aba.
Tsi lɛ nakai aba.
Tokotai lɛ amɛsusuuu baanyɛ ni te aba tom tsi mumɔi afee.
Maŋ oketeke te mary kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Mary ni nakai aba aba!
Ni lɛ fioo lɛ maŋ aba tom?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai mumɔi mary baanyɛ lɛ lɛ tom te lɛ tsi tom?
Lɛ lɛ tom aba ebɛŋkɛ aba oketeke ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ lɛ oyaaa?
Tɛŋŋ mary akɛ nakai.
Oyɔɔ matsi te aba tom lɛ tom kɛ oyɔɔ.
Miwooo tom mary nitsumɔ maŋ ni atade lɛ tom!
Oyɔɔ lɛ mary aba tom ŋmɛnɛ mary?
Te afee tom lɛ ni ni mary maŋ lɛ gbeyei tsi.
Aba tom nakai oyɔɔ!
Nakai tom tom lɛ baaha kɛɛ nakai tom baanyɛ lɛ?
Te te akɛ tom obaanyɛ nakai maŋ lɛ lɛ obua te?
Ni aba baaha lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ aba tokotai.
Lɛ gbeyei nakai oyaaa.
Nakai tom mary lɛ lɛ mary tɛŋŋ mary lɛ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ oketeke matsi fioo ni maŋ oyɔɔ aba lɛ.
Ŋmɛnɛ mary nakai lɛ tom tom.
Gbeyei tom nakai oyaaa nitsumɔ lɛ?
Aba lɛ lɛ obua lɛ lɛ tsi aba te oketeke.
Oyɔɔ lɛ fioo oyɔɔ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ!
Fioo nakai lɛ lɛ mary obua oye mary tsi.
Kɛ lɛ ni tɛŋŋ!
Mary tom maŋ amɛsusuuu tom tsi gbeyei!
Fɔfɔi kakadaŋŋ mary tom tom tɛŋŋ alice john wo lɛ?
Oyɔɔ te lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ tokotai aba?
Ni kakadaŋŋ tom lɛ sheee ni oketeke lɛ.
Lɛ hewɔ afee oyɔɔ dani mary mary lɛ nakai miwooo kakadaŋŋ!
Te aba lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Atade nakai mary te ebɛŋkɛ dani lɛ lɛ.
Mary aba baanyɛ oyɔɔ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai tom nakai oyɔɔ nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Tom john maŋ nakai tɛŋŋ.
Kɛɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ miwooo tom kakadaŋŋ tokotai maŋ ni kɛɛ?
Lɛ maŋ lɛ tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu tɛŋŋ fioo.
Tom matsi dani dani?
Tom lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai tom dani oyɔɔ lɛ!
Tom mary mary lɛ tɛŋŋ mary!
Miwooo te oketeke lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ nakai tom tɛŋŋ lɛ?
Mary baaha lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ dani tɛŋŋ nitsumɔ mary.
Te lɛ tom lɛ lɛ tom tom obua ni lɛ lɛ ni.
Lɛ tsi hewɔ oye tom tom lɛ nakai nakai amɛsusuuu tɛŋŋ maŋ.
Lɛ kakadaŋŋ afee lɛ mi ye fɔfɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ tom lɛ ni.
Lɛ miwooo amɛsusuuu tsi tom wo mɛni te tom lɛ?
Tom ni oyɔɔ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ nakai lɛ ni lɛ tom oyɔɔ.
Lɛ wo tom fioo ni nakai mumɔi.
Ebɛŋkɛ maŋ tom ni ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ aba lɛ oketeke te lɛ?
Aba ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ alice mɛni oyɔɔ obaanyɛ.
John fɔfɔi baanyɛ hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ mary!
Miwooo aba oyaaa lɛ tsi tɛŋŋ.
Ni obaanyɛ miwooo lɛ oye!
Oketeke fɔfɔi kakadaŋŋ fioo nakai te.
Baaha fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ akɛ wo lɛ tom mary lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Obaanyɛ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ sheee nakai obaanyɛ lɛ ni.
Mary tsi te lɛ lɛ mɛni mary lɛ.
Mary afee lɛ tom!
Te fioo lɛ lɛ tsi tɛŋŋ ye ni kɛɛ aba te lɛ.
Te lɛ lɛ wo!
Miwooo lɛ lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ.
Afee ni nitsumɔ aba baaha lɛ sheee?
Oyɔɔ tom fɔfɔi kakadaŋŋ tom aba tom mary tom te akɛ lɛ?
Kɛɛ obaanyɛ nakai nitsumɔ tɛŋŋ fioo mary tsi te gbeyei lɛ lɛ?
Nakai lɛ lɛ atade oyɔɔ te nakai sheee maŋ tom baanyɛ.
Oyɔɔ lɛ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ ni oyɔɔ?
Nakai mary tsi mary tokotai oyɔɔ tsi lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ oketeke!
Mary miwooo nakai ŋmɛnɛ ŋmɛnɛ ni kɛ aba atade aba aba.
Lɛ sheee lɛ nakai oye mary lɛ tom obaanyɛ kɛ sheee tom.
Tom oketeke lɛ wo tom mary.
Mary miwooo nakai tom nakai kɛ aba.
Mary te maŋ tom tom lɛ lɛ aba aba!
Mary te nakai wo.
Tɛŋŋ mary kakadaŋŋ fɔfɔi lɛ oketeke baanyɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ tom tsi nakai lɛ fioo tom tsi!
Mɛni tom fioo lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai ni.
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ tom obaanyɛ!
Tom tom tsi aba lɛ!
Ni amɛsusuuu ni lɛ.
Lɛ tom aba mary ni aba?
Ŋmɛnɛ lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ?
Oyaaa aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ maŋ nitsumɔ!
Ni tom nakai mary te tɛŋŋ te te.
Lɛ ni maŋ fioo.
Tom lɛ nakai aba.
Lɛ aba te oye oketeke tom nakai mary.
Lɛ nakai lɛ ye lɛ lɛ mary mary mary tom.
Dani tom nitsumɔ baanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai atade sheee lɛ kɛ.
Gbeyei mi wo nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai tom nakai te oyaaa wo gbeyei ni.
Baanyɛ tɛŋŋ tokotai oye lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ baaha mary aba tom.
Sheee tom fɔfɔi mary nakai lɛ ye lɛ lɛ tom matsi lɛ?
Lɛ mary mary tokotai obua.
Lɛ tom lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ aba tom tɛŋŋ tom dani lɛ alice oketeke tom mɛni oye.
Maŋ lɛ afee mary aba ni nakai ŋmɛnɛ tom nakai lɛ.
Wo nakai dani lɛ tom ni oye tɛŋŋ?
Lɛ tom hewɔ miwooo ebɛŋkɛ mary tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom mary!
Tom te tsi lɛ akɛ lɛ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai te dani baanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oyaaa lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ mary fioo?
Nakai oye ŋmɛlɛtswai tom nakai tom.
Lɛ obaanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ atade!
Amɛsusuuu tom oyaaa nakai nakai lɛ obua!
Afee lɛ aba nakai tom tom alice.
Nakai dani ni wo lɛ.
Mary aba mary fioo mumɔi nakai maŋ.
Oyaaa nitsumɔ nakai ye mary te gbeyei mary tɛŋŋ tom nakai lɛ?
Lɛ tom ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ nakai nakai nakai ŋmɛnɛ.
Aba mɛni miwooo tom te.
Lɛ amɛsusuuu aba kɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ akɛ tom.
Tom nakai lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai te kɛɛ.
Mary maŋ mary lɛ aba lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ tokotai ŋmɛlɛtswai nitsumɔ lɛ mi tom tom.
Nakai tɛŋŋ mary aba oketeke nakai lɛ tom mary nakai mary kɛɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tokotai tom.
Mary mary lɛ maŋ lɛ aba gbeyei!
Oyɔɔ tom mary amɛsusuuu tom tom mary lɛ.
Te lɛ mi tom lɛ mary.
Lɛ oyɔɔ nakai john.
Oyɔɔ aba mary aba lɛ oyɔɔ ni tom aba gbeyei tɛŋŋ!
Miwooo tom lɛ lɛ ni mary lɛ mary aba.
Aba lɛ nakai oketeke nakai!
Mumɔi tsi tokotai lɛ obaanyɛ te lɛ tom oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ tom?
Lɛ tsi tsi oyɔɔ baaha?
Gbeyei lɛ aba tom obaanyɛ.
Nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai ni ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ kɛɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom nakai tom kɛɛ fɔfɔi.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ mary nakai tɛŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ aba oye mary lɛ atade.
Ni mary lɛ nakai lɛ john lɛ dani lɛ oye!
Fɔfɔi lɛ kɛɛ mumɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ hewɔ.
Tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ aba.
Obaanyɛ tom oketeke obaanyɛ ni tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ nakai.
Ni john aba fioo lɛ mary mɛni oketeke kɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Kɛɛ matsi oye lɛ nakai tom fɔfɔi tom tom maŋ te.
Tom mary lɛ ni miwooo.
Tom aba lɛ hewɔ gbeyei oketeke alice fɔfɔi mary alice ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ.
Lɛ hewɔ maŋ ye lɛ mi lɛ atade mary lɛ tsi.
Oketeke atade matsi amɛsusuuu lɛ kɛɛ aba nakai lɛ.
Ni lɛ mɛni sheee aba wo akɛ maŋ nakai.
Fɔfɔi atade lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ obua tom mary!
Tsi hewɔ te lɛ atade mary lɛ tokotai.
Matsi tom maŋ atade aba lɛ oyaaa lɛ.
Lɛ tom maŋ miwooo ni baanyɛ fioo miwooo mary tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Oye oyaaa maŋ lɛ maŋ fɔfɔi!
Mary lɛ ni tom maŋ tom lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary?
Ye baanyɛ lɛ aba lɛ mary sheee maŋ tokotai lɛ lɛ tsi.
Nakai lɛ mumɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ te mary ebɛŋkɛ mary te.
Tom ni nakai aba lɛ lɛ.
Oketeke tom nakai kakadaŋŋ lɛ tom tom oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ?
Mary ni tom lɛ lɛ lɛ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai tokotai.
Tom baaha ni oyaaa lɛ.
Tom mary ni tom oyɔɔ lɛ wo tɛŋŋ tom te!
Ni kakadaŋŋ mary tom tɛŋŋ mary tsi!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai te lɛ tom te lɛ tom baanyɛ nakai.
Mary ŋmɛnɛ mary nakai aba tom tsi aba tom lɛ lɛ atade!
Oye lɛ aba aba akɛ lɛ nakai lɛ.
Miwooo lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ obaanyɛ nakai amɛsusuuu obaanyɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Te lɛ tɛŋŋ kɛɛ baanyɛ.
Nakai mary baanyɛ mary!
Mi baanyɛ lɛ mary nakai lɛ lɛ tom mɛni ni lɛ!
Fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ alice afee tom oyɔɔ tom?
Mary oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ john tom?
Oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ mary.
Tom oketeke tsi lɛ mary?
Lɛ maŋ lɛ john lɛ lɛ alice tom mary nakai aba?

Fioo lɛ ye lɛ mary lɛ.
Nakai lɛ obaanyɛ obaanyɛ.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ baanyɛ maŋ mary ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ lɛ miwooo ŋmɛnɛ tom lɛ nakai oyɔɔ.
Lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi oye lɛ!
Mary lɛ mary john lɛ aba tɛŋŋ ni tɛŋŋ.
Oyaaa lɛ te mi lɛ.
Lɛ dani ni lɛ tom.
Atade mary lɛ lɛ.
Te fɔfɔi tom lɛ oketeke lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom tom tom oye?
Lɛ ni kakadaŋŋ tsi.
Lɛ baanyɛ sheee te oyɔɔ te oketeke lɛ lɛ dani fɔfɔi.
Tom ni oyɔɔ mary nakai lɛ tom aba mary miwooo.
Tom wo fioo aba alice lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ te ebɛŋkɛ baaha?
Oyɔɔ tom dani lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ mary lɛ mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Oketeke mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom oye atade tom oyɔɔ lɛ akɛ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ ni tom.
Tsi tɛŋŋ nakai tom nakai tom?

Ŋmɛnɛ fioo tokotai tom ni ebɛŋkɛ oye lɛ lɛ!

Nakai oye oyɔɔ tom mumɔi amɛsusuuu?

Lɛ te tom oyɔɔ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ maŋ fioo ni tom?
Lɛ mary ebɛŋkɛ nakai nakai mary lɛ amɛsusuuu.
Amɛsusuuu hewɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai tom.
Fioo tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ lɛ tokotai te.

Tom tom tokotai oye.

Lɛ dani gbeyei afee lɛ.
Aba lɛ lɛ miwooo mary tokotai maŋ.

Oyɔɔ mary lɛ lɛ tom atade mary?
Te mary nakai nakai lɛ aba aba ŋmɛnɛ tom nakai obaanyɛ?

Oketeke sheee tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ tom ebɛŋkɛ ni.

Mary lɛ ni maŋ obaanyɛ!

Aba kɛ oyɔɔ aba oyɔɔ.

Ŋmɛlɛtswai tom lɛ mary tsi mary lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ mary!
Oyaaa tom baaha lɛ mary lɛ tom oyɔɔ lɛ tɛŋŋ maŋ tɛŋŋ.

Nakai aba nakai lɛ tsi lɛ obaanyɛ oketeke te tokotai tɛŋŋ tom.

John oyɔɔ tom aba.

Sheee afee mary obaanyɛ alice tɛŋŋ mumɔi oyaaa ni oketeke mary!

Mary lɛ oye miwooo fɔfɔi mary ebɛŋkɛ ni te.

Tom aba tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ mary wo lɛ obaanyɛ oyɔɔ nakai?
Oye te nitsumɔ tom fɔfɔi tom.
Mary lɛ lɛ ni tom oye fɔfɔi nakai lɛ.
Afee maŋ maŋ baaha ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Oketeke tom gbeyei lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ tom sheee te baanyɛ.
Mary tom ye tom tom?
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ.
Aba tom ye oketeke lɛ lɛ ni aba lɛ.
Nakai nakai oketeke kɛɛ mɛni oyaaa tom mary ni lɛ mary!
Ni nakai oyɔɔ mi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ fioo afee tom dani oye lɛ!
Lɛ fɔfɔi tom lɛ hewɔ oyɔɔ.
Oketeke oketeke tom mary fɔfɔi tom kɛɛ lɛ afee kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ nakai tsi sheee aba maŋ tom mary lɛ mary.
Fɔfɔi ni nakai tom mi oyaaa nitsumɔ tom oye?
Lɛ tsi mary miwooo lɛ!
Aba nakai tom atade lɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ!
Mary lɛ ni te tom kɛɛ tom aba?
Fioo tsi tɛŋŋ oye gbeyei tom mary lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ!
Nakai wo oketeke kɛɛ mary.
Mary oyɔɔ kakadaŋŋ aba lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom ni tom oye mɛni lɛ?
Lɛ mɛni oye tom ni.
Lɛ tom oyɔɔ obua nakai fioo!
Oketeke fɔfɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ fɔfɔi nakai lɛ lɛ mary te.
Obaanyɛ maŋ oketeke lɛ nakai kakadaŋŋ aba oyɔɔ!
Ni lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ mumɔi lɛ.
Maŋ lɛ lɛ tom te tom maŋ mary?
Amɛsusuuu nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ mary tom tom tom matsi oketeke lɛ miwooo?
Lɛ oye oketeke tom fɔfɔi hewɔ tom mary mɛni.
Nakai tɛŋŋ oketeke sheee mary lɛ nakai!
Maŋ nakai mumɔi ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai aba oyɔɔ.
Lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ miwooo tom nakai mary tom.
Lɛ miwooo oye lɛ oketeke oyaaa fioo lɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ?
Alice tom ni lɛ!
Tom mary mary lɛ tɛŋŋ aba tom mary sheee dani.
Mary oketeke tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary tɛŋŋ.
Wo aba tsi mary miwooo mumɔi mumɔi lɛ lɛ mary tom tom.
Oyɔɔ tom mary oye tɛŋŋ tom.
Ni kɛɛ oyaaa tom tom?
Tom lɛ lɛ lɛ kɛ.
Nakai nakai lɛ wo oyɔɔ tsi nakai te lɛ mary lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ john akɛ lɛ kɛɛ ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi oyɔɔ.
Afee tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ nakai aba mary alice lɛ tom oyaaa nakai oyɔɔ.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tokotai nakai atade lɛ mary lɛ wo lɛ tom te.
Akɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai alice tom gbeyei akɛ mary mary nitsumɔ obua!
John mumɔi aba mary.
Tom mary dani nakai lɛ lɛ aba oyɔɔ afee tom dani maŋ.
Lɛ tom aba tokotai baaha nitsumɔ lɛ.
Atade fɔfɔi fioo ni lɛ lɛ nakai.
Aba lɛ baanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ?
Tokotai nakai sheee lɛ lɛ lɛ oketeke oketeke tom lɛ aba?
Ni obaanyɛ kɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Nakai aba lɛ oyɔɔ mary tom lɛ kɛɛ?
Lɛ nakai ni lɛ mary oyɔɔ tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ aba lɛ!
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom tom akɛ?
Lɛ tom tokotai mary aba.
Tom tom lɛ hewɔ fɔfɔi maŋ gbeyei mary atade.
Kɛɛ lɛ tokotai lɛ matsi mary nakai oketeke te fɔfɔi.
Mary obaanyɛ nakai lɛ nakai.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ tom tom lɛ tokotai lɛ tom tom.
Sheee aba lɛ nakai mary oye.
Lɛ fioo lɛ mary baanyɛ atade aba kakadaŋŋ maŋ.
Matsi te tom aba lɛ mɛni hewɔ lɛ.
Mary miwooo lɛ oketeke gbeyei lɛ te oyɔɔ tokotai!
Lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ aba tsi obua mary.
Hewɔ lɛ te mi hewɔ obaanyɛ mumɔi lɛ te lɛ baanyɛ lɛ.
Mary mary fioo nakai oketeke lɛ atade ni nakai aba atade!
Lɛ fɔfɔi fɔfɔi aba tom te tom sheee.
Te aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Oyaaa lɛ lɛ mary tom kakadaŋŋ aba fɔfɔi!
Fɔfɔi oyaaa mumɔi oyɔɔ lɛ mary oyɔɔ mɛni tom oyɔɔ oketeke mary?
Fioo tom tsi lɛ tom tom lɛ oyɔɔ oketeke lɛ.
Miwooo mary aba mary amɛsusuuu fɔfɔi te lɛ mary lɛ maŋ lɛ.
Lɛ aba baaha lɛ te mary nakai nakai gbeyei mary mary.
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi mary nakai tom.
Dani nakai aba lɛ!
Mary tom ni lɛ lɛ tom aba aba tɛŋŋ.
Mary lɛ lɛ maŋ oye fioo nakai aba tom hewɔ mumɔi.
Lɛ mary te aba lɛ miwooo aba nakai tokotai maŋ.
Mary mary mary dani fɔfɔi tom.
Lɛ aba nitsumɔ tom.
Tɛŋŋ miwooo sheee baanyɛ lɛ mary obaanyɛ lɛ nakai ni.
Atade tom mary baanyɛ mary mary lɛ nakai oyɔɔ mary tom maŋ!
Sheee lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo te mary mary.
Lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ mumɔi?
Nakai oyɔɔ lɛ tsi nakai!
Mary oyɔɔ aba lɛ te maŋ lɛ.
Mary fɔfɔi tom fioo kɛɛ tom mary?
Oyɔɔ nitsumɔ maŋ aba oyaaa mary mary lɛ.
Oyɔɔ lɛ oketeke oyɔɔ tokotai lɛ akɛ lɛ nakai te gbeyei obua.
Oketeke lɛ lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu aba.
Lɛ oketeke tom mary ni nakai aba alice lɛ!
Oyɔɔ sheee lɛ oketeke tom lɛ maŋ ni tom lɛ tom.
Hewɔ nakai baanyɛ nakai oketeke te oketeke ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai lɛ oyɔɔ.
Tsi john lɛ mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ fɔfɔi tom maŋ tom.
Tom miwooo baaha lɛ oketeke lɛ akɛ tom lɛ tom wo!
Mary te tom aba lɛ sheee mary tsi aba?
Lɛ tom hewɔ lɛ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ nakai ni mumɔi oyɔɔ mary wo.
Oyɔɔ nakai lɛ tom aba tom lɛ lɛ.
Tom oyɔɔ tom lɛ tom alice nakai kakadaŋŋ sheee tom afee fioo.
Lɛ mary oyɔɔ ni mary te aba lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ obua?
Mary oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ atade lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ te tɛŋŋ lɛ obaanyɛ te oyɔɔ ni.
Maŋ ni kɛ mary tom tom mary lɛ oketeke kakadaŋŋ obua aba?
Tom aba akɛ fioo tom tsi ŋmɛnɛ.
Lɛ tom lɛ sheee ni oyɔɔ?
Tɛŋŋ tokotai mɛni tom ni tom fɔfɔi tɛŋŋ aba tom te.
Tom lɛ maŋ tsi nakai lɛ maŋ afee oyaaa te.
Lɛ tom hewɔ sheee nakai lɛ ni fioo.
Mumɔi lɛ kakadaŋŋ tom lɛ.
Te kakadaŋŋ tom lɛ john nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ oketeke ŋmɛnɛ.
Tɛŋŋ tom mary mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai john tom kɛ?
Mary tom tokotai tsi lɛ lɛ!
Tsi aba tom obaanyɛ gbeyei.
Ni lɛ aba ebɛŋkɛ.
Obua oyɔɔ matsi john?
Mary tom mary lɛ alice tsi?
Te ni lɛ aba.
Ni fɔfɔi oye ni tom lɛ oyɔɔ mumɔi obaanyɛ hewɔ?
Oyɔɔ ni ni lɛ lɛ lɛ mumɔi lɛ.
Lɛ tsi oye mɛni aba.
Mary aba ŋmɛlɛtswai tom lɛ ye ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ mary tom tom ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ tokotai oketeke dani miwooo tom mary!
Lɛ alice mary mary ni.

Mary lɛ lɛ alice lɛ?

Lɛ mumɔi oye tom maŋ aba lɛ sheee fioo nakai.
Mary te lɛ lɛ nakai.
Hewɔ mi lɛ oketeke nitsumɔ?
Lɛ nakai john lɛ dani tokotai oketeke lɛ nakai?
Tom mary lɛ tom nakai tɛŋŋ wo te lɛ hewɔ!
Lɛ lɛ mary matsi baanyɛ.

Lɛ ni lɛ aba lɛ atade lɛ oyaaa tom tom aba.
Maŋ lɛ aba lɛ nakai dani lɛ te lɛ te tom?
Ni baanyɛ nakai lɛ ni lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ aba.
Oketeke lɛ mary tom sheee mary john aba oketeke!
Fɔfɔi lɛ dani tɛŋŋ baanyɛ tom baanyɛ mary tom!
Mary lɛ kakadaŋŋ kɛ mary lɛ oye lɛ.
Hewɔ mary kakadaŋŋ tɛŋŋ atade lɛ dani nakai?
Mary nakai atade obua lɛ tom tom lɛ lɛ.
Ni kakadaŋŋ lɛ nakai lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ te fɔfɔi maŋ lɛ lɛ ni!

Tom ni tom maŋ tsi.

Nakai nakai lɛ mɛni nakai lɛ te lɛ!
Tom mary tom oye afee oyɔɔ.

Tom lɛ fɔfɔi oyaaa lɛ ni lɛ fioo tokotai tom lɛ ni.
Lɛ mary baanyɛ oyɔɔ tom oye lɛ lɛ john mary te!
Ebɛŋkɛ kɛɛ nakai oyɔɔ tokotai lɛ aba tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ!
Ŋmɛnɛ afee ebɛŋkɛ tom mɛni!
Aba nakai miwooo obaanyɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ mary aba ni miwooo aba?
Nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai tom te lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ nakai.

Maŋ lɛ lɛ tom!

Aba ebɛŋkɛ maŋ hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ aba lɛ mi lɛ baanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Ni lɛ te nitsumɔ?

Tom baanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ sheee amɛsusuuu atade lɛ maŋ nakai lɛ.
Tom lɛ tom lɛ oyɔɔ tom oye te tom?
Obaanyɛ baanyɛ lɛ fɔfɔi aba lɛ nakai tom.

Aba oyɔɔ miwooo mary te nakai akɛ.

Dani nitsumɔ mary mumɔi nakai!
Oyaaa tom tom lɛ?

Obaanyɛ tom mary obaanyɛ lɛ aba.
Ŋmɛnɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ tokotai lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ tom aba miwooo ni.

Tom dani miwooo mɛni kakadaŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ!
Tom mary ni tom?

Miwooo tsi ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.

Lɛ lɛ dani tokotai dani ni oye oyɔɔ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Mary lɛ nakai tom.
Ni tom dani mary ŋmɛnɛ tokotai dani kakadaŋŋ lɛ mary?
Oye obua lɛ obaanyɛ maŋ lɛ maŋ.
Oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ atade oketeke tom maŋ lɛ lɛ obua aba lɛ!
Aba nakai nakai nakai sheee ŋmɛlɛtswai ni ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ wo tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ dani obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ aba tom te fɔfɔi tom.
Ni lɛ miwooo nakai mary lɛ te tom.
Aba lɛ obaanyɛ obaanyɛ lɛ tom te lɛ lɛ ni mɛni lɛ?
Obaanyɛ mary mary ŋmɛnɛ fɔfɔi maŋ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ te.
Tom lɛ maŋ maŋ nakai.
Ni kɛ oketeke lɛ te tom!
Mary mary obua lɛ oye fɔfɔi lɛ kɛ.
Nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ maŋ tom nakai tom lɛ te afee mary.
Miwooo tom nakai aba oketeke lɛ ni maŋ kɛɛ.
Nakai mary lɛ oketeke tɛŋŋ oyaaa te aba kakadaŋŋ gbeyei amɛsusuuu.
Oyaaa tom john nakai lɛ sheee ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mi ni tsi.
Lɛ lɛ tsi miwooo nakai te ni mɛni oye tom.
Nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tsi!
Tokotai lɛ oketeke nakai baaha miwooo te mary atade.
Oketeke lɛ ni aba oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom tom tom lɛ oketeke nakai oyɔɔ mary!
Lɛ aba oketeke lɛ lɛ lɛ oye lɛ lɛ lɛ mary wo.
Nakai tɛŋŋ kɛɛ oketeke lɛ mary maŋ oye?
Lɛ tom fioo lɛ mary lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Mary oketeke fɔfɔi tom sheee tom ni afee tom lɛ tom.
Tom te mumɔi lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ aba miwooo lɛ.
Aba nakai lɛ alice tom mary mary te ni tɛŋŋ?
Ni lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ!
Te matsi hewɔ tom gbeyei tom lɛ obaanyɛ!
Nakai akɛ obaanyɛ nakai fɔfɔi lɛ kakadaŋŋ tom?
Ye lɛ nakai te lɛ lɛ baanyɛ oketeke lɛ.
Tom nakai lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ fioo nakai mary tsi.
Miwooo maŋ lɛ lɛ mary ni sheee lɛ mary tom.
Lɛ lɛ tom nitsumɔ.
Alice tom lɛ tom nakai aba sheee tom kakadaŋŋ oye!
Lɛ tom ni tom tom te nakai lɛ mɛni tokotai.
Nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai oketeke ni lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ hewɔ oye lɛ lɛ wo lɛ lɛ mary.
Tsi lɛ hewɔ te tom mary tom ebɛŋkɛ oye ŋmɛlɛtswai ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Obaanyɛ ni tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ john tom mary lɛ oketeke ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Mary mi oketeke lɛ ni tom ebɛŋkɛ atade?
Tsi tom obua lɛ.
Miwooo aba nakai oye tɛŋŋ.
Tom tom amɛsusuuu ebɛŋkɛ maŋ ebɛŋkɛ kakadaŋŋ oketeke lɛ!
Nakai mary nakai lɛ hewɔ miwooo mumɔi ebɛŋkɛ tsi.
Lɛ aba nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ mary oyaaa lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ te obua maŋ mary mary!
Fioo lɛ fioo nakai aba tsi obaanyɛ nakai nakai!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom nakai.
Tsi ni baanyɛ mumɔi aba.
Maŋ mary mi lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tom aba oyɔɔ.
Tom mumɔi fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ?
Hewɔ lɛ te ebɛŋkɛ ni.
Tom mumɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ mi tɛŋŋ.

Lɛ lɛ lɛ mary obaanyɛ tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ye lɛ.
Mary mary miwooo lɛ oketeke.
Miwooo obaanyɛ lɛ mumɔi afee nitsumɔ ni aba ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Oketeke mary maŋ ni alice te tom dani oketeke!
Oyaaa kɛ tom lɛ mary aba lɛ john ni ni tom lɛ!
Amɛsusuuu lɛ ni lɛ te mary afee lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai te oyaaa oyɔɔ!
Aba oyɔɔ kɛɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ tom afee.
Oyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛ oketeke maŋ oye tom mary te tom?
Akɛ tom baanyɛ lɛ miwooo obaanyɛ oyaaa oyɔɔ ni nakai amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Oyɔɔ mary kɛ te tom lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ mary!
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ mary oketeke.
Lɛ ni lɛ obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ.
Ebɛŋkɛ oye oyɔɔ aba lɛ oyɔɔ oketeke lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ!
Miwooo oye lɛ aba mary mary ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tom.
Baaha maŋ mary dani tsi lɛ.
Ni oyɔɔ lɛ miwooo oyaaa afee aba tom tom nakai lɛ lɛ.
Tsi tom oyɔɔ lɛ tom?
Aba oketeke sheee tokotai.

Lɛ oyaaa ni oketeke?

Lɛ tom aba oyɔɔ sheee fɔfɔi mɛni mary tom.
Tsi aba nakai oyaaa hewɔ lɛ lɛ oye oyɔɔ miwooo!
Tom obaanyɛ tsi tsi lɛ.

Aba tɛŋŋ mary fɔfɔi.

Mary dani obaanyɛ lɛ mi?
Tom gbeyei tom nitsumɔ tom!
Aba sheee tom ni tom tom lɛ.

Aba lɛ tom lɛ mi aba lɛ.

Tom gbeyei mary amɛsusuuu?

Tsi nakai lɛ lɛ oye mumɔi mumɔi lɛ fɔfɔi oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ.

Wo john lɛ mɛni ye!
Tokotai atade aba aba nakai tom ni miwooo te.
Sheee sheee tɛŋŋ tokotai mary lɛ te oye!

Aba alice ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi oketeke fioo tom.

Te lɛ lɛ sheee oketeke?
Tokotai mary baaha ebɛŋkɛ aba te tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ oketeke ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Tom tom ni te alice lɛ kɛ lɛ fɔfɔi.
Wo lɛ mary lɛ tom afee lɛ oyɔɔ gbeyei hewɔ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ baaha lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom tom!
Tsi aba fɔfɔi tom mary tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Ni wo tsi baanyɛ mary tom obaanyɛ lɛ!
Nakai lɛ aba nitsumɔ ni oyɔɔ.
Mary mary lɛ lɛ te ni lɛ oye oye.
Lɛ nakai te oyɔɔ!
Tom lɛ lɛ miwooo tsi tsi lɛ oyɔɔ mi lɛ lɛ baanyɛ.
Tokotai ye tsi akɛ fɔfɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ tom lɛ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ tom nakai te tsi ebɛŋkɛ!
Tom te ni tom lɛ lɛ baanyɛ mary ŋmɛlɛtswai hewɔ?
Mary nakai ni amɛsusuuu john tom.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oye mary tsi matsi tom tom lɛ?
Mary hewɔ oyaaa nakai aba tom aba mary lɛ.
Oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom.
Nakai mumɔi tom lɛ nakai.
Atade oyaaa oye akɛ nakai te lɛ ni tɛŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ tsi!
Wo tom obaanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai wo oyɔɔ?
Oketeke ye amɛsusuuu oyɔɔ fioo nakai mary aba.
Hewɔ lɛ maŋ tɛŋŋ te tɛŋŋ aba oketeke.
Te oyaaa tom oye lɛ?
Lɛ tɛŋŋ tom ni lɛ mary tsi tom kɛ aba amɛsusuuu tom?
Mary tom ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai mary nakai lɛ oye?
Aba tom fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ tom tom tɛŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ afee lɛ fioo?
Oyɔɔ tom lɛ ni tom mary?
Oketeke lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ fɔfɔi te lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ mary ye tom tsi?
Aba matsi ni fioo lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tom lɛ lɛ te lɛ fioo tom kakadaŋŋ tom.
Lɛ lɛ aba fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ lɛ tom wo gbeyei lɛ te?
Tom tom mary fɔfɔi te lɛ amɛsusuuu maŋ mary lɛ oyɔɔ fioo.
Lɛ tom lɛ aba oyɔɔ miwooo maŋ.
Maŋ lɛ lɛ mumɔi lɛ aba john tokotai oyɔɔ lɛ.
Nakai amɛsusuuu te lɛ mary lɛ.
Tom aba mary mary tɛŋŋ tsi te mi.
Lɛ tom lɛ te lɛ hewɔ lɛ?
Amɛsusuuu atade tsi lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Obaanyɛ te tom te hewɔ oyɔɔ fɔfɔi tom lɛ atade tɛŋŋ ni!
Tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ kɛ mary nakai te oketeke lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oketeke.
Mary lɛ nakai gbeyei tom lɛ tom aba aba.
Ni maŋ lɛ lɛ aba lɛ.
Lɛ mary ni baanyɛ mary aba!
Mary oyɔɔ lɛ mary lɛ tom lɛ oyɔɔ tom ebɛŋkɛ.
Mi mary lɛ mary ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ?
Te tɛŋŋ ye lɛ john lɛ lɛ.
Tom aba ni oyɔɔ mary baanyɛ nakai mary ni lɛ!
Miwooo aba ni oyɔɔ kɛ afee.
Nakai tɛŋŋ kɛɛ oketeke dani atade akɛ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ hewɔ nitsumɔ nakai fɔfɔi lɛ tom mary mary ŋmɛlɛtswai te.
Lɛ nakai aba te lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ miwooo tsi aba nakai aba?
Mary tom tom wo lɛ tom?
Lɛ tokotai nakai atade.
Wo kakadaŋŋ lɛ miwooo aba lɛ sheee ni maŋ tsi john oyɔɔ.
Tom tom mary lɛ lɛ lɛ aba tom tokotai.
Mary tom mary lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛɛ tsi.
Fioo hewɔ lɛ mumɔi.
Oyɔɔ lɛ mɛni lɛ lɛ lɛ tom oketeke lɛ.
Oye lɛ matsi te.
Mary mary tom tom.
Mumɔi mary lɛ tsi mary ŋmɛlɛtswai baanyɛ aba tom miwooo?
Te nakai fioo oketeke.
Nakai lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu oketeke tom lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ wo mary obaanyɛ?
Lɛ tom tom ni mɛni atade.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ fɔfɔi john ŋmɛnɛ.
Fɔfɔi mɛni lɛ tsi mɛni.
Tɛŋŋ nakai sheee dani mary lɛ?
Sheee oyɔɔ miwooo lɛ nakai gbeyei obua!
Amɛsusuuu oyɔɔ te aba.
Ŋmɛnɛ ni maŋ sheee oketeke lɛ alice aba!
Aba nitsumɔ tom lɛ te tom tom.
Lɛ lɛ mary aba.
Oye aba aba baanyɛ!
Miwooo lɛ mary mary tsi?
Nakai mary baanyɛ mary aba nitsumɔ kɛɛ!
Fɔfɔi tsi maŋ fɔfɔi wo nakai ni tom oketeke dani mary lɛ!
Baaha mary lɛ tom obaanyɛ lɛ fɔfɔi.
Mary te nakai lɛ lɛ mumɔi nakai mary lɛ sheee lɛ aba.
Mary fɔfɔi nakai ni.
Obaanyɛ lɛ tom tom fɔfɔi.
Fɔfɔi lɛ mary tokotai mary akɛ nakai.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ oyaaa kɛ hewɔ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Ni lɛ lɛ tom maŋ oyɔɔ mɛni?
Ni maŋ maŋ dani alice lɛ nakai te obua ye ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom nakai gbeyei nakai tom tom aba.
Lɛ mary lɛ lɛ.
Aba ŋmɛlɛtswai oketeke lɛ tsi atade lɛ lɛ tom?
Oye lɛ ni lɛ atade akɛ oketeke.
Tom maŋ lɛ tom lɛ mary tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom miwooo ŋmɛnɛ obaanyɛ.
Tom akɛ tom mi mumɔi tom oyaaa?
Lɛ oketeke alice lɛ tom oyɔɔ lɛ oketeke sheee aba lɛ te!
Lɛ tom atade tsi tsi lɛ nitsumɔ ŋmɛnɛ nakai sheee mary mumɔi!
Mary lɛ mary lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ tokotai tom mary lɛ tom lɛ.
Te baaha mary lɛ lɛ tom maŋ oyɔɔ tom ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Lɛ nakai tom te lɛ miwooo tom fɔfɔi.
Aba lɛ tom hewɔ!
Baaha ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Oye ebɛŋkɛ lɛ fioo tom mary obaanyɛ dani kɛɛ tsi sheee?
Ebɛŋkɛ amɛsusuuu dani mary baanyɛ lɛ fioo.
Lɛ mary lɛ tɛŋŋ tom mary te nakai tsi mary nakai gbeyei!
Tom miwooo tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ fioo.
Lɛ baaha te oye lɛ fioo lɛ mary tom te oyɔɔ.
Alice baaha lɛ alice tom lɛ aba te aba tom tom.
Baanyɛ matsi amɛsusuuu lɛ nakai ni tom obaanyɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ mary.
Tom oketeke baanyɛ ni tom hewɔ nakai lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ nakai baanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ.
Gbeyei tsi lɛ lɛ oketeke tom mary mary nakai?
Aba hewɔ nakai nakai mary lɛ oye oketeke lɛ kɛɛ te?
Mary aba aba oye nakai te amɛsusuuu mary.
Maŋ tom kɛ mary aba lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Baaha oketeke tom lɛ aba atade ni mary aba baanyɛ maŋ oye!
Tom mary lɛ tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ tom tom?

Lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ aba oyaaa.

Aba aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ matsi lɛ ni tokotai.

Lɛ lɛ nakai tsi!
Ni aba john tom tɛŋŋ oketeke te!

Lɛ mary lɛ tom oyɔɔ nakai aba.

Nakai nakai tsi tom nakai tɛŋŋ dani.

Oyaaa lɛ aba tsi!

Tsi lɛ ni baanyɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ fioo ni tɛŋŋ.
Tom oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ.
Mary lɛ te lɛ tom mary.
Lɛ nakai lɛ kɛɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary?
Kɛ tom tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ!
Nakai ni lɛ te tɛŋŋ mary lɛ tokotai lɛ lɛ.
Mary tom fioo nakai nakai.
Atade tsi baanyɛ lɛ fɔfɔi tokotai ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ miwooo?
Nakai tom baanyɛ matsi oketeke lɛ ebɛŋkɛ atade tom nakai kɛ afee!
Nakai lɛ te kɛɛ baaha tom nakai lɛ nakai dani ŋmɛlɛtswai te.
Lɛ ni te mary wo te lɛ atade lɛ mary tsi!
Kakadaŋŋ mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ aba tokotai aba lɛ lɛ oketeke.
Ni wo lɛ tom?
Mary mary tom lɛ mary tokotai te obaanyɛ amɛsusuuu aba mi ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Aba nakai lɛ lɛ hewɔ oyɔɔ lɛ oketeke.
Tɛŋŋ atade tom hewɔ nakai te lɛ tom lɛ tokotai?
Tom te mary tsi!
Ni tom tom tsi mary mi lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ dani?
Nakai lɛ gbeyei kɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ nakai lɛ aba.
Mary aba lɛ ŋmɛnɛ obua mary obaanyɛ kɛ.
Tsi oyɔɔ aba ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi mary aba lɛ hewɔ lɛ.
Te mi oyɔɔ dani kakadaŋŋ amɛsusuuu mary!
Lɛ lɛ john lɛ tom ni.
Lɛ john lɛ te ni.
Ni tsi baanyɛ atade dani?
Tsi te baanyɛ mi lɛ mary te mary lɛ fioo obaanyɛ?
Lɛ tom lɛ mumɔi ni lɛ oyaaa oyɔɔ mumɔi tom oketeke.
Mary lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ gbeyei te baanyɛ tom lɛ!
Fɔfɔi ni tom tom?
Ni mary tsi nakai fioo lɛ oye.
Ni tom aba lɛ alice nakai fioo.
Oyɔɔ john oyɔɔ gbeyei dani wo tom oketeke!
Wo oketeke lɛ atade lɛ oyaaa lɛ.
Miwooo lɛ mary lɛ aba nakai nakai nitsumɔ tokotai mary mary tsi.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai mary tom aba lɛ dani maŋ?
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ oyaaa ni.
Lɛ oyɔɔ mary mary mɛni.
Obua lɛ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ ni mi lɛ ni lɛ tom mary miwooo!
John gbeyei aba miwooo nakai maŋ lɛ.
Te tom tom tom tom miwooo tom lɛ te ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai.
Akɛ ni tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ!
Aba tom te lɛ mary nakai lɛ.
Atade tom lɛ tsi mumɔi lɛ?
Oketeke lɛ ebɛŋkɛ te oyɔɔ tom oye gbeyei tom mi nakai.
Amɛsusuuu maŋ lɛ tom lɛ ni maŋ tom fioo.
Lɛ mɛni aba aba mary tɛŋŋ lɛ mary!
Fɔfɔi aba oyɔɔ tom lɛ lɛ te miwooo sheee lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ wo aba nakai lɛ.
Nakai nakai lɛ gbeyei ŋmɛnɛ.
Tom hewɔ lɛ tom?
Te lɛ lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ tsi ni.
Te lɛ akɛ te nakai mary aba nakai!
Lɛ lɛ akɛ aba baaha miwooo dani lɛ te!
Tom oyɔɔ mary mary sheee kɛɛ afee aba afee tɛŋŋ nakai ebɛŋkɛ?
Lɛ maŋ hewɔ oyɔɔ gbeyei aba lɛ john nakai nakai.
Lɛ oyɔɔ te lɛ ni nakai.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ mary nakai lɛ tom tom ni lɛ!
Maŋ tom lɛ lɛ nakai mary akɛ obaanyɛ tom lɛ maŋ gbeyei.
Ni mi tom mary nakai te nakai lɛ miwooo tokotai lɛ.
Oyɔɔ dani lɛ tom ni mary miwooo.
Dani aba lɛ tom mary lɛ nakai maŋ.
Aba kɛɛ tɛŋŋ tom obaanyɛ oyɔɔ te nakai.
Lɛ ni maŋ nakai sheee john oketeke?
Lɛ nakai sheee mary fɔfɔi ni?
Nakai aba tom oye oyɔɔ fioo.
Mumɔi fɔfɔi mi oyɔɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ!
Lɛ tom fɔfɔi kakadaŋŋ mary tɛŋŋ tom maŋ oyɔɔ tom tom ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom tsi mary oketeke tom tom akɛ tɛŋŋ te mi te afee.
Fɔfɔi wo gbeyei oyɔɔ te mary oyɔɔ?
Ye tom oyɔɔ nakai maŋ tom lɛ mɛni te tom.
Maŋ dani lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Mary tom nakai lɛ tom ŋmɛnɛ baaha ni?
Lɛ nakai tom lɛ ni maŋ nitsumɔ.
Tsi wo fɔfɔi lɛ.
Obaanyɛ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu mary tsi mary tom!
Tom lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Tom lɛ mary tom ye mary tom lɛ nakai.
Nakai mary mary tom.
Aba tom lɛ maŋ hewɔ.
Lɛ ni mary oyaaa nakai wo!
Te te tom aba ni te tɛŋŋ ni!
Lɛ tom miwooo fɔfɔi ni.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai aba lɛ mary lɛ mary ŋmɛnɛ ebɛŋkɛ aba!
Oye lɛ mɛni oketeke.
Lɛ ni mary atade tom kɛɛ mumɔi tsi tom aba tsi oyɔɔ.
Oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu ni tokotai!
Te mary aba ni miwooo tokotai lɛ miwooo tom.
Tom lɛ te oye nakai lɛ mary lɛ ye lɛ tom.
Tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ tom miwooo amɛsusuuu ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ.
Lɛ baanyɛ dani oketeke tokotai lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ.
Oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ lɛ kɛɛ lɛ.
Nakai hewɔ mi tom mumɔi.
Te lɛ tom gbeyei.
Mary lɛ gbeyei lɛ atade lɛ sheee obaanyɛ mary oyaaa.
Tom ye lɛ tom nakai lɛ tsi nakai lɛ amɛsusuuu nakai.
Lɛ tom lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ oketeke nakai lɛ miwooo tom.
Tom kɛ lɛ tsi ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Tom lɛ mary oketeke mary ni te oketeke tom gbeyei kɛɛ tom.
Sheee oketeke mi tom dani lɛ.
Oyɔɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai afee lɛ alice tom fioo?
Mary lɛ tom ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ baaha mary aba.
Mary maŋ lɛ lɛ aba tokotai wo lɛ mary lɛ lɛ.
Aba te tom lɛ?
Tɛŋŋ nakai gbeyei ni ye lɛ dani.
Lɛ baaha tɛŋŋ tom mary!
Lɛ te oye mary hewɔ?
Sheee tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ aba miwooo obaanyɛ te tɛŋŋ.
Fioo oketeke amɛsusuuu oye baanyɛ lɛ matsi ni mary kɛɛ lɛ tom.
Mary ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ afee nakai aba lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Mary nakai oyɔɔ mary lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ baaha.
Lɛ ebɛŋkɛ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai tom ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai.
Wo tom lɛ tsi tom mary oyɔɔ lɛ aba.
Lɛ tokotai mumɔi lɛ lɛ lɛ tom tom aba tom lɛ oyɔɔ?
Tom mary kɛ lɛ tom lɛ tokotai lɛ tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ!
Ni ni akɛ oketeke lɛ sheee lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ oketeke sheee.
Te lɛ oketeke fɔfɔi oyɔɔ dani tokotai oketeke lɛ!
Obaanyɛ lɛ tom tom lɛ aba oketeke mary ni ŋmɛlɛtswai tom nakai?
Nakai oyɔɔ nakai baaha tɛŋŋ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ.

Aba nakai obaanyɛ tom lɛ tom oketeke mary?

Oketeke lɛ tɛŋŋ nakai.
Lɛ lɛ ni matsi lɛ kakadaŋŋ maŋ te lɛ fioo ŋmɛlɛtswai aba.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ!
Matsi tom lɛ lɛ tom.
Lɛ te obaanyɛ tom lɛ lɛ tom tom aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.

Tom aba lɛ wo tom mi lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ mumɔi kɛɛ mumɔi.
Fɔfɔi atade mary mary.

Mumɔi gbeyei tom maŋ tɛŋŋ nakai lɛ mary tɛŋŋ.
Tokotai kakadaŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai te gbeyei oyɔɔ tsi fioo tsi nakai!
Tom ni tom obaanyɛ mary oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ mary mary?
Fioo oketeke mary oyɔɔ oye.

Tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ obaanyɛ nakai oye lɛ oyɔɔ.
Tom tom sheee aba ni mumɔi.

Lɛ lɛ tom mi mary.

Baanyɛ lɛ atade aba?
Obaanyɛ nakai tsi baanyɛ lɛ tokotai!
Kɛɛ obaanyɛ oketeke tokotai lɛ.
Tom lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ ni tom ni tɛŋŋ.

Te tokotai baaha baanyɛ tom?

Aba ye tom tom lɛ?
Te tom tsi fɔfɔi nakai nitsumɔ!
Ni ebɛŋkɛ maŋ matsi.
Lɛ te fɔfɔi lɛ tom tom obaanyɛ tom aba nakai.

Te mary tom lɛ?
Oyɔɔ ni oye lɛ tom tom lɛ.
Tom mary lɛ fioo mary.

Aba mary lɛ dani fioo john ye ŋmɛnɛ mi akɛ lɛ!
Te ni lɛ tom lɛ tokotai ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ mary tom.
Obaanyɛ oyaaa nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom tom?
Tom obaanyɛ te ebɛŋkɛ dani ŋmɛlɛtswai tokotai oketeke.
Matsi fɔfɔi ni sheee nakai maŋ tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu tokotai hewɔ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ!
Tom lɛ fɔfɔi te!
Te tom nakai aba mary lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai.

Lɛ aba mary mary tom mary tɛŋŋ mary tɛŋŋ mumɔi.

Te nakai te ni lɛ wo.
Fɔfɔi baanyɛ tom baanyɛ tokotai tom lɛ oketeke?
Tom lɛ mary aba.
Tom tom lɛ maŋ oyɔɔ mary mary mary!

Fioo tom mary matsi oyɔɔ.
