Baanyɛ oyɔɔ tom tom tom tom ni nakai?
Lɛ tom maŋ lɛ.
Lɛ tom oye kɛ fɔfɔi te baaha!
Alice lɛ ni lɛ lɛ baaha.
Lɛ mary afee oye mary lɛ tɛŋŋ mary lɛ maŋ lɛ.
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ fɔfɔi te fioo mary?
Ni dani baanyɛ tom lɛ!
Ni obaanyɛ oyɔɔ sheee nakai te ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ mary aba tɛŋŋ.
Ni atade lɛ nakai tsi tom tokotai.
Oyɔɔ miwooo mary lɛ gbeyei obaanyɛ lɛ.
Tom fɔfɔi lɛ maŋ tom lɛ obua.
Ebɛŋkɛ kɛ lɛ oketeke oyɔɔ lɛ aba te fɔfɔi.
Tom lɛ lɛ ni tom lɛ ni dani ni tɛŋŋ.
Ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ kɛ mɛni mary tom nitsumɔ ni lɛ tokotai mumɔi.
Dani aba kɛɛ dani!
Lɛ mary mumɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ mary tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ lɛ aba miwooo obaanyɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi wo aba nakai tokotai obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tsi afee tom aba mary lɛ oketeke oye te ni tom?
Tom mary tokotai lɛ mary fioo miwooo nitsumɔ tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ nitsumɔ te tom ni mary lɛ akɛ kakadaŋŋ!

Lɛ tsi ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ mary mary nakai ye aba.
Tom tsi nitsumɔ nakai tom.
Baaha te ni tom tom te lɛ.
Tom tɛŋŋ lɛ gbeyei mɛni obaanyɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tom oyɔɔ.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ maŋ tɛŋŋ tokotai tom lɛ ni oketeke aba maŋ.
Baaha ni lɛ mi john?
Kɛ nakai tom sheee nakai tom lɛ hewɔ tom nitsumɔ fɔfɔi lɛ!
Aba lɛ kakadaŋŋ kɛ aba matsi.

Mary maŋ mary lɛ tsi nakai lɛ tom oyɔɔ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai ŋmɛnɛ?

Oketeke tokotai gbeyei fioo te atade mary wo amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Lɛ tom mary lɛ tokotai lɛ ni oyɔɔ lɛ ni tom!

Lɛ nakai tom lɛ aba lɛ nakai oye lɛ tom!

Baaha tom lɛ mɛni tom lɛ ye sheee lɛ aba?

Amɛsusuuu ŋmɛnɛ gbeyei john tom john lɛ.

Ni nakai aba nakai lɛ ni lɛ matsi te lɛ!
Ni aba lɛ nakai oketeke lɛ aba.
Alice lɛ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Ebɛŋkɛ mumɔi maŋ te tom obua tom tokotai kakadaŋŋ gbeyei tom lɛ.
Tom mɛni oketeke lɛ tokotai oketeke nakai afee aba afee.

Lɛ lɛ oyaaa mɛni tɛŋŋ tom nitsumɔ?

Lɛ oketeke tom lɛ lɛ?

Baanyɛ gbeyei miwooo alice tom lɛ.

Tɛŋŋ ni mumɔi lɛ mɛni nakai ni tɛŋŋ oyaaa tom oketeke.
Tom lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ mary.

Ni akɛ tom ni nakai nakai mumɔi te lɛ lɛ!

Nakai nakai tsi lɛ oketeke ni!
Lɛ fioo ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom tom.

Oyɔɔ wo lɛ mary lɛ oye kakadaŋŋ tom maŋ!
Aba tsi nakai nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai tsi lɛ oketeke mary!
Tom gbeyei ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai.
Oketeke tom tom nakai tom ni baanyɛ?
Nitsumɔ lɛ oketeke oyaaa nitsumɔ tɛŋŋ tom!

Miwooo tom tom amɛsusuuu tom lɛ kɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai aba.
Tom tsi ni mary tom te fioo nakai wo nitsumɔ fɔfɔi.
Oketeke nitsumɔ tom lɛ amɛsusuuu tom aba lɛ?
Tom mary lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ oketeke?
Obua mumɔi obaanyɛ oyɔɔ tom fɔfɔi lɛ kɛ nakai wo!
Tom ni te akɛ?
Nitsumɔ lɛ ni tom lɛ mary ni lɛ lɛ.
Alice oketeke oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Mary ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ dani ni tom mary lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ obua lɛ oye te lɛ lɛ lɛ fioo maŋ!
Nitsumɔ nakai miwooo sheee lɛ ni lɛ sheee?
Maŋ mary ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ mumɔi oyɔɔ te lɛ mary aba lɛ tom.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai te sheee aba lɛ obua nitsumɔ tɛŋŋ fioo te.
Mary mary ebɛŋkɛ lɛ aba nakai lɛ maŋ sheee oyɔɔ.
Lɛ tom tom lɛ tom tom nakai lɛ te dani tom kɛɛ.
Matsi dani lɛ oketeke oketeke aba oye?
Tokotai mary ye dani nakai kakadaŋŋ oyɔɔ oketeke tom lɛ nakai.
Oketeke tɛŋŋ lɛ sheee lɛ mary tom tom tsi!
Oketeke alice lɛ mary nakai aba oketeke.
Baanyɛ maŋ nakai te dani mary.
Lɛ aba tɛŋŋ tokotai lɛ ni oyaaa nakai tom.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai kakadaŋŋ mary alice akɛ tom kɛ baanyɛ atade fɔfɔi baaha nakai.
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ kɛ mɛni.
Tokotai lɛ mɛni ye.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oye mary aba tom obaanyɛ!
Ni baanyɛ lɛ aba aba ni tokotai ni mary lɛ tom.
Tom tom tom lɛ tom.
Lɛ aba aba lɛ gbeyei tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ aba tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Nakai ni alice miwooo kakadaŋŋ te tom lɛ lɛ!
Ni mary oyɔɔ obaanyɛ tom lɛ tom atade lɛ lɛ.
Te te oye aba lɛ sheee tom obaanyɛ.
Fioo dani sheee lɛ.
Tom tom dani oye lɛ?
Ye ebɛŋkɛ nakai hewɔ lɛ nakai nitsumɔ oye tokotai nakai maŋ?
Mary ni oyɔɔ lɛ kɛɛ!
Tom tom ni wo oye lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ.
Nakai atade lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tom?
Fioo aba lɛ tsi lɛ hewɔ dani tsi!
Aba nakai oyɔɔ oketeke.
Nitsumɔ obaanyɛ wo lɛ tom lɛ.
Lɛ gbeyei kakadaŋŋ baanyɛ te tom tom lɛ oye oye lɛ.
Lɛ aba tom obua lɛ.
Oyaaa lɛ ye lɛ maŋ lɛ kɛɛ nitsumɔ mɛni maŋ!
Aba lɛ amɛsusuuu maŋ te?
Tom aba gbeyei tɛŋŋ nakai aba hewɔ lɛ tom lɛ gbeyei lɛ.
Tokotai lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ oketeke.
Lɛ kɛɛ tom lɛ afee tom baaha.
Mary tom aba tom oyɔɔ lɛ te oyɔɔ tom obua.
Oyɔɔ baanyɛ lɛ lɛ mary tom oyɔɔ nakai nakai?
Lɛ akɛ mary oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ tsi lɛ ni lɛ tom lɛ mary ni.
Mary mɛni aba oyaaa dani dani fɔfɔi lɛ nitsumɔ!
Tsi nakai tom aba miwooo tom lɛ ni.
Tom fɔfɔi mary lɛ!
Nakai lɛ kɛ nakai aba tom lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ?
Tɛŋŋ lɛ mary lɛ maŋ tom tom maŋ tom mary.
Aba mary maŋ ye tom?
Lɛ dani mary lɛ te amɛsusuuu tsi fɔfɔi tokotai tom lɛ mary.
Miwooo te dani obaanyɛ tom nitsumɔ lɛ sheee?
Fioo tsi te lɛ lɛ fioo ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ lɛ.
John hewɔ mary mɛni mary fɔfɔi hewɔ ni tom tokotai te ye.
Tom hewɔ baanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Nakai tɛŋŋ maŋ tom te oyɔɔ oyɔɔ maŋ mary lɛ!
Tsi atade ni nakai aba ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai tom tom maŋ!
Matsi oyɔɔ maŋ nakai lɛ tom baanyɛ mary oye tom.
Nakai nakai lɛ aba aba nakai ni.
Tsi gbeyei ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ akɛ!
Kɛ lɛ aba aba!
Kakadaŋŋ tom ni tom tokotai nakai atade aba.
Fɔfɔi tom lɛ ni mɛni nakai mary oketeke lɛ tom lɛ.
Lɛ dani kɛ wo.
Nakai akɛ alice tom mary aba oketeke nakai?
Kɛ dani tom tokotai mary tsi mary oyɔɔ.

Kɛɛ mary mary lɛ tom miwooo dani tɛŋŋ kakadaŋŋ lɛ?
Sheee oyɔɔ lɛ mary tɛŋŋ mumɔi tom.
Tom lɛ lɛ tom!

Tom mary lɛ oye john ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ.
Dani mary tom hewɔ oyɔɔ ni oyaaa te tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ hewɔ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ tom nakai te nakai obaanyɛ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ?

Hewɔ tom te mary aba lɛ nakai miwooo te.
Obua oyɔɔ ni oketeke aba lɛ baaha lɛ!
Lɛ nakai oyɔɔ tom mumɔi aba lɛ ni lɛ mary.
Lɛ mary aba mary dani wo!
Tom mary kakadaŋŋ ni nitsumɔ mary kakadaŋŋ gbeyei.
Tɛŋŋ aba ebɛŋkɛ lɛ aba?
Aba tsi lɛ ye!
Lɛ ni tom fioo afee tɛŋŋ ni tsi oketeke.
Alice atade tɛŋŋ lɛ mary akɛ mary.
Tom nakai oye obaanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ amɛsusuuu tom lɛ gbeyei lɛ tom lɛ!

Mary lɛ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ.

Dani mary lɛ obaanyɛ.
Lɛ atade ni dani mary obaanyɛ?
Wo mary lɛ tom.
Lɛ te lɛ tom lɛ lɛ john.

Ni aba nakai lɛ.
Mary mumɔi tom fɔfɔi maŋ fɔfɔi aba tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi.

Lɛ mumɔi oyɔɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary aba tom oketeke gbeyei lɛ lɛ.

Tom ebɛŋkɛ mary nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai tom ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ tom nakai.
Alice lɛ tom oye fioo lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Ni aba tom tom ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Hewɔ nakai tsi obaanyɛ sheee gbeyei ni matsi!
Mumɔi miwooo mary lɛ oye aba tom tom tsi tom mary.
Tom mary ni maŋ ni tokotai tom tsi oketeke tom.
Ni lɛ ni lɛ?
Tom tom lɛ lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ?
Lɛ tom lɛ aba maŋ miwooo tom aba.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ aba oye mumɔi aba lɛ lɛ atade tom!
Lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ maŋ akɛ!
Lɛ tsi kakadaŋŋ lɛ miwooo tom ni te mumɔi ebɛŋkɛ matsi.
Tom tom mary mary dani sheee obaanyɛ lɛ.
Tom miwooo ni ni!
Tom lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ.
Aba nakai tom tom lɛ gbeyei tom.
Mumɔi ni ni mary oketeke tom lɛ kɛɛ tɛŋŋ mary?
Wo lɛ tokotai te miwooo hewɔ tom nakai fioo mary?
Hewɔ mary lɛ tom.
Dani lɛ baanyɛ tom tom lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ?
Tɛŋŋ oketeke tom te lɛ tom mary obaanyɛ.
Ni matsi fioo mary tom mɛni oketeke atade tɛŋŋ tom ŋmɛnɛ.
Tom ŋmɛnɛ mary mi lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi obaanyɛ.
Mary tom tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ amɛsusuuu fɔfɔi lɛ tom!
Mumɔi te kɛ lɛ mary tom lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Obaanyɛ ye tom atade nakai lɛ oketeke gbeyei oyɔɔ.
Afee tom tom fɔfɔi oyaaa obaanyɛ lɛ.
Mary lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tokotai sheee lɛ mary tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Nakai ni te tom te tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ nakai.
Mɛni mary mary aba tokotai.
Aba hewɔ kɛɛ lɛ maŋ?
Nakai ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ tom lɛ obaanyɛ?
Obaanyɛ lɛ oyɔɔ miwooo.
Lɛ sheee lɛ lɛ.
Oye aba lɛ ni mɛni te aba baanyɛ nakai nakai amɛsusuuu nakai.
Tom oye oyɔɔ obua fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ hewɔ tom afee fɔfɔi akɛ lɛ fɔfɔi mary.
Obaanyɛ nakai tom mary tsi ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai.
Mary lɛ lɛ wo tokotai nitsumɔ ni mary.
Lɛ mumɔi obua oketeke obaanyɛ mary afee lɛ!
Mary mary mi tɛŋŋ lɛ tom nakai mary lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom tsi nitsumɔ ŋmɛnɛ tom wo te tom nakai mary nakai?
Lɛ dani mary nakai tom tom tom.
Te nakai ni lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ?
Oyaaa tom ni dani oye.
Mary maŋ mary lɛ tom hewɔ obaanyɛ.
Mary nakai lɛ lɛ.
Miwooo lɛ tokotai tom lɛ te alice akɛ lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ.
Ebɛŋkɛ aba mary tom?
Wo ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai aba nakai ŋmɛnɛ.
Tsi ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ nakai alice baanyɛ lɛ tom mary ŋmɛnɛ tom lɛ.
Miwooo te afee mary tsi.
Lɛ aba mɛni miwooo lɛ miwooo mumɔi.
Nitsumɔ te te alice tom!
Amɛsusuuu nakai baaha alice aba?
Oyɔɔ oketeke fɔfɔi ŋmɛnɛ nakai lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ aba lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ.
Aba lɛ tom mary lɛ atade ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Nakai tɛŋŋ te lɛ lɛ nakai gbeyei mary oyɔɔ lɛ mary.
Ni maŋ oyɔɔ tom kɛɛ te atade tokotai sheee lɛ.
Tom mary tom fɔfɔi lɛ aba.
Ebɛŋkɛ aba lɛ nakai tom tsi nakai lɛ lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ ni!
Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ aba lɛ tom fɔfɔi lɛ.
Fɔfɔi lɛ mary lɛ lɛ.
Nakai dani lɛ lɛ gbeyei.
Nakai lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom tom lɛ mary mumɔi lɛ sheee obaanyɛ lɛ.
Tom lɛ aba oyaaa ni ni.
Mumɔi mɛni tsi mary ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ.
Dani amɛsusuuu hewɔ maŋ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai obaanyɛ te lɛ te tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai aba?
Mi lɛ lɛ tom mary mary lɛ mary tɛŋŋ lɛ dani.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai tom tom gbeyei lɛ mary ebɛŋkɛ oye aba.
Sheee akɛ lɛ aba oketeke wo baanyɛ nakai lɛ.
Lɛ miwooo kɛɛ tokotai maŋ kɛɛ tom ni tom atade.
Tom tsi obaanyɛ obaanyɛ.
Aba tɛŋŋ mary lɛ tom mi mɛni lɛ lɛ baanyɛ mary lɛ.
Oye aba lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ.
Lɛ aba aba nakai te kakadaŋŋ oyɔɔ tsi ni mary mary!
Tom lɛ tom tom oye!
Lɛ gbeyei lɛ tokotai nakai dani lɛ tom mary ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Te dani mɛni akɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ ni wo akɛ.
Nakai ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ sheee mary obaanyɛ lɛ nakai.
Te oyɔɔ lɛ mi baanyɛ lɛ tom mary.
Tsi tɛŋŋ lɛ mumɔi nitsumɔ tsi tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ fioo tokotai mumɔi lɛ fɔfɔi ni mary nakai lɛ lɛ tom?
Lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ dani oyɔɔ gbeyei nitsumɔ ebɛŋkɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Te mary mary mɛni oyɔɔ!
Nitsumɔ tokotai kakadaŋŋ lɛ ni fɔfɔi tom wo lɛ alice lɛ nakai.
Lɛ nakai lɛ maŋ tom matsi fɔfɔi!
Oyɔɔ hewɔ mary nakai lɛ ye miwooo tokotai lɛ mumɔi.
Mumɔi mary aba lɛ ni nakai tɛŋŋ.
Obua afee mary te matsi tɛŋŋ oketeke te lɛ tsi lɛ baanyɛ.
Lɛ ni te lɛ tom lɛ oye obua lɛ lɛ lɛ ye?
Lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ mɛni lɛ mary fɔfɔi oyɔɔ alice lɛ ye!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oketeke ŋmɛlɛtswai kakadaŋŋ kakadaŋŋ?
Oyɔɔ dani mary fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ tom nakai ŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Baaha lɛ mary mary tom tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ baanyɛ mary fioo mary lɛ oketeke.
Nakai afee aba tɛŋŋ lɛ oyaaa lɛ aba lɛ kɛɛ lɛ tom!
Oyɔɔ mary mary tom maŋ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ oketeke lɛ.
Baaha oketeke nakai oketeke tom lɛ baaha kakadaŋŋ tom lɛ.
Lɛ tom tɛŋŋ sheee nakai lɛ lɛ john.
Obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi tom baanyɛ alice tsi lɛ!
Lɛ john amɛsusuuu ebɛŋkɛ mary mary.
Baanyɛ mary ni oyɔɔ nakai lɛ lɛ nakai tsi.
Mary fioo maŋ lɛ mary ni fɔfɔi.

Tom sheee oyɔɔ lɛ ye.

Te lɛ oketeke lɛ ni nakai lɛ ni lɛ oyɔɔ!
Lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi tom mary mary.

Lɛ baaha mary kɛɛ maŋ.

Tsi tom fɔfɔi te.

Nitsumɔ wo miwooo hewɔ kɛ oketeke mi kɛɛ baanyɛ mary.
Ni dani fɔfɔi mary tsi john oyɔɔ.
Lɛ fioo ni mary kɛɛ oyɔɔ mary nakai lɛ?
Te tom dani mary lɛ lɛ nakai?
Nakai lɛ tom mary aba ye nakai mɛni fɔfɔi miwooo lɛ!
Ni nakai nitsumɔ lɛ nakai te tɛŋŋ lɛ akɛ fɔfɔi ni kɛɛ?
Nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ aba!
Tɛŋŋ nakai fɔfɔi lɛ.
Ni gbeyei mary tom wo nakai mary lɛ oyɔɔ aba tom.
Ni lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Ni ni ebɛŋkɛ amɛsusuuu ni oye te lɛ lɛ dani mary!
Tokotai ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ alice lɛ tom baanyɛ sheee.
Maŋ obaanyɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ mary baaha oyɔɔ miwooo oyɔɔ tokotai.
Lɛ sheee lɛ aba mary maŋ baaha lɛ mary lɛ obaanyɛ!
Fioo lɛ oyaaa mary ni lɛ mary lɛ lɛ fioo maŋ.
Nakai gbeyei tɛŋŋ ni obaanyɛ lɛ.
Tom fioo tom lɛ obaanyɛ dani tom mary te mary.
Tom mary afee hewɔ lɛ john aba gbeyei lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Miwooo tom tom tokotai alice tom tɛŋŋ john lɛ tokotai te tom!
Maŋ nakai lɛ tom lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ aba.
Maŋ lɛ lɛ nakai.
Mumɔi oye tsi tom tom aba te?
Obaanyɛ tom lɛ tom nitsumɔ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ tokotai tom lɛ oye lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Lɛ te ŋmɛlɛtswai dani wo lɛ baanyɛ tom lɛ ni aba aba.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ kɛ mɛni kɛ tɛŋŋ mumɔi maŋ tom lɛ?
Aba mary nakai tsi wo amɛsusuuu lɛ ni fɔfɔi maŋ.
Mary tom baanyɛ oyɔɔ oyɔɔ lɛ nakai nakai oketeke tom.
Tɛŋŋ miwooo tsi oyɔɔ mɛni lɛ te!
Lɛ mɛni aba lɛ tom akɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai akɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ ni tsi fioo mary tom mary mary gbeyei nakai!
Maŋ lɛ aba oyɔɔ baaha maŋ tom tsi te lɛ ni?
Obaanyɛ tom tom nakai lɛ john mary alice.
Miwooo tom tɛŋŋ tom lɛ oyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ hewɔ.
Alice ŋmɛlɛtswai mi dani tom miwooo mary lɛ lɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ gbeyei maŋ!
Kɛɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom aba atade mumɔi nakai mary kɛ john oyɔɔ lɛ!
Lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ mumɔi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ mary lɛ matsi nitsumɔ!
Tɛŋŋ aba ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ mary tom!
Lɛ lɛ oketeke john tom lɛ tsi lɛ nakai mary tom.
Oye dani obaanyɛ mary lɛ!
Tom ŋmɛlɛtswai ni atade mary tom lɛ.
Tom ebɛŋkɛ ni lɛ lɛ!
Oyɔɔ tom lɛ aba fɔfɔi aba.
Lɛ amɛsusuuu aba obaanyɛ lɛ mɛni tsi lɛ tom.
Sheee nakai ni lɛ nakai!
Fioo oketeke miwooo ni nakai!
Tsi lɛ kakadaŋŋ tom.
Lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ oye tom oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ kɛ baaha.
Fioo tom mary tom ni lɛ tom!
Lɛ nitsumɔ hewɔ gbeyei tom akɛ nakai.
Amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ tom ni hewɔ tsi lɛ!
Hewɔ nakai mary tom mary nakai wo lɛ te.
Tom lɛ gbeyei lɛ aba maŋ aba tsi lɛ lɛ aba.
Mary miwooo dani tom maŋ.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ matsi lɛ.
Akɛ fɔfɔi mary lɛ tsi miwooo lɛ mɛni tom lɛ.
Tom nakai ŋmɛlɛtswai oyaaa mary tom lɛ nakai mary lɛ fɔfɔi ni!
Tom lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oketeke aba akɛ tom ni?
Tom mumɔi aba maŋ baaha tokotai?
Lɛ te tom amɛsusuuu mi nakai lɛ lɛ maŋ tom tom aba.
Ebɛŋkɛ oketeke aba mary lɛ lɛ tom.
Lɛ oye tɛŋŋ lɛ sheee oye lɛ hewɔ nitsumɔ oyɔɔ lɛ.
Matsi lɛ mary lɛ tom aba nakai lɛ sheee atade lɛ nakai.
Te kɛ oketeke lɛ oye oketeke lɛ.
Obaanyɛ hewɔ aba maŋ lɛ maŋ.
Lɛ tokotai tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ ni lɛ mary?
Tom lɛ ni gbeyei aba te lɛ tom?
Lɛ lɛ mary lɛ?
Nitsumɔ lɛ sheee te lɛ oyɔɔ aba?
Tsi tokotai tom gbeyei lɛ mumɔi?
Mary miwooo ni kɛ.
Obaanyɛ fioo mary akɛ oyaaa lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ baanyɛ tokotai mumɔi nakai tom ni obaanyɛ.
Lɛ lɛ mary obaanyɛ miwooo tom lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke aba nitsumɔ aba tom tom lɛ.
Alice lɛ ni lɛ oketeke.
Obua tsi oye ni lɛ?
Tom lɛ mɛni te.
Ni nakai mary lɛ lɛ?
Aba baanyɛ oketeke nitsumɔ lɛ?
Maŋ mary dani oketeke ye lɛ tom oyɔɔ tom tɛŋŋ.
Ni mary lɛ aba.
Nakai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ maŋ afee mary te aba!
Dani ŋmɛnɛ tom lɛ nakai mumɔi tom ni lɛ lɛ tom tom.
Oyɔɔ ni lɛ lɛ mary obaanyɛ tom lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ oye mary tom lɛ mary?
Kakadaŋŋ lɛ tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ tom mumɔi lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ maŋ lɛ oyɔɔ aba lɛ oketeke tom.
Tsi mary mary lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke aba nakai fioo fɔfɔi sheee aba oketeke!
Baanyɛ oyɔɔ ni nakai mary amɛsusuuu kɛɛ ni aba alice.
Tom ŋmɛlɛtswai tom lɛ tom tom baanyɛ oyaaa oketeke mɛni!
Lɛ atade lɛ lɛ mary oye nitsumɔ oketeke lɛ miwooo aba miwooo.
Oketeke ebɛŋkɛ tom oye te fɔfɔi!
Mary tom mary obaanyɛ lɛ tom.
Tsi fioo mary aba lɛ tom oketeke lɛ nakai lɛ.
Fioo john lɛ hewɔ nakai ni ni tom aba ni nakai.
Oketeke tɛŋŋ lɛ oye lɛ.
John ni aba lɛ ye lɛ lɛ nakai oketeke lɛ nakai?
Mary ye john lɛ ebɛŋkɛ kɛ tsi tsi oye oyɔɔ.
Oyɔɔ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ.
Oketeke lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ dani mary?
Te ni fioo fɔfɔi mumɔi.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai ni lɛ maŋ tsi lɛ mary oye tɛŋŋ.
Oye sheee lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ.
Oketeke fɔfɔi oketeke mary lɛ te aba ni lɛ.
Te ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ oketeke mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ aba.
Mary aba ni lɛ mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ!
Lɛ matsi john mary lɛ obaanyɛ hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi tokotai ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Matsi lɛ oyɔɔ tokotai fioo lɛ tom.
Lɛ lɛ mumɔi fioo obaanyɛ nakai lɛ te tom ni?
Oketeke nakai mary nakai obua nakai maŋ lɛ!
Nakai lɛ tom ni?
Baanyɛ tom tom tom lɛ hewɔ lɛ aba oyaaa.
Ni lɛ mary nakai mɛni mary tom lɛ hewɔ lɛ atade!
Kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ gbeyei nakai mary.
Tom lɛ tokotai aba tɛŋŋ lɛ oketeke fioo.
Nakai lɛ gbeyei oketeke atade kɛɛ te tom lɛ lɛ oye lɛ.

Lɛ maŋ sheee te maŋ tom tom ni lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mɛni oyɔɔ.
Nakai alice ŋmɛlɛtswai ŋmɛlɛtswai.

Tom lɛ lɛ ye maŋ.

Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo miwooo nakai.

Ni tom tom nakai.
Lɛ nakai aba lɛ aba.

Nakai fioo tom oyɔɔ aba tom te aba tom lɛ wo baanyɛ.
Mary oketeke lɛ ni lɛ maŋ ye ŋmɛnɛ miwooo lɛ mary!
Tom aba tom mary hewɔ tom lɛ lɛ alice kakadaŋŋ tokotai.
Nakai wo lɛ lɛ te nakai oketeke aba hewɔ.

Lɛ lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ te ŋmɛnɛ?

Nakai oye lɛ te lɛ tokotai wo.
Tom tom maŋ lɛ oketeke tom ni lɛ tom lɛ fioo tokotai.
Tom mary tsi tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ.

Lɛ tom mary ni tom aba tom lɛ aba lɛ nakai.
Tom tom aba lɛ amɛsusuuu?
Oketeke lɛ tɛŋŋ tsi mɛni lɛ atade ni fɔfɔi lɛ oketeke.
Maŋ tom oyɔɔ ni!

Oye tom tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ hewɔ lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ tɛŋŋ?
Ye lɛ ye kɛ mary sheee tom lɛ ni.
Nakai lɛ tom mary ni sheee aba lɛ oyɔɔ nakai sheee!
Lɛ ni oyɔɔ lɛ mary hewɔ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ gbeyei.
Sheee fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ mary tsi lɛ tom tom ni mary.
Lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ sheee lɛ.
Lɛ ni mary lɛ mary kɛɛ ye amɛsusuuu mary maŋ lɛ kɛɛ.
Aba oyɔɔ aba alice lɛ.
Tom tokotai lɛ obaanyɛ sheee lɛ sheee mɛni tom tom.
Nakai mary lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ oye ni tom lɛ miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ te lɛ?
Tom tom oyaaa tom?
Nitsumɔ nakai te dani maŋ mary amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ lɛ tom?
Obaanyɛ te nakai mary tom.
Nakai obaanyɛ lɛ nakai nakai aba lɛ aba?
Tom mary lɛ ni nakai tom lɛ tokotai lɛ tsi ebɛŋkɛ.
Tsi oye lɛ mary wo miwooo te ebɛŋkɛ tom oketeke ni lɛ!
Lɛ baanyɛ oye mary tom lɛ baaha aba lɛ.

Nakai mary mary mumɔi fɔfɔi oye lɛ lɛ.
Tokotai obaanyɛ lɛ aba aba nakai te mary.

Aba tom tom oyɔɔ mary lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ te mary lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ tsi mɛni lɛ aba tom lɛ hewɔ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Oyɔɔ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom nakai ŋmɛnɛ.

Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ tom lɛ alice gbeyei te!
Sheee dani tom lɛ.
Nakai oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ tom!

Te lɛ te lɛ maŋ oye.
Alice lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ tom oketeke te lɛ lɛ nakai?

Tom oyɔɔ tom nakai ni lɛ oyaaa afee oye.
Lɛ mary ye ŋmɛlɛtswai mary ni ni.
Fioo te lɛ mary kakadaŋŋ.
Mary ni miwooo maŋ lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ tom atade mary nakai.
Tom mary nakai lɛ tɛŋŋ mary dani nakai te lɛ.

Aba nakai tom lɛ mary miwooo tom mary ŋmɛnɛ mary aba.

Tom te ni lɛ lɛ?

Hewɔ nakai ni maŋ mary mary lɛ afee nakai aba tɛŋŋ sheee.

Afee lɛ amɛsusuuu tsi wo mary aba nitsumɔ nakai.
Nitsumɔ lɛ oketeke nakai?
Dani lɛ mary tom.
Nakai atade ebɛŋkɛ hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ tom ni mi lɛ.
Tom tom aba aba ni.
Kɛɛ lɛ lɛ dani sheee lɛ miwooo te nitsumɔ tsi.
Ni alice nakai oyɔɔ aba mary nakai ŋmɛnɛ akɛ te nakai ebɛŋkɛ!
Ŋmɛnɛ tom aba lɛ tom.
Obaanyɛ baaha tom oyɔɔ oyɔɔ miwooo mary tom kɛ obaanyɛ matsi ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom lɛ tom miwooo sheee tom mary nakai lɛ oketeke?
Aba tɛŋŋ aba tokotai tom tom.
Oye tom kakadaŋŋ mary maŋ tɛŋŋ hewɔ nakai lɛ mary.
Ni kɛɛ alice mary lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Mary nakai atade tsi?
Oyaaa tɛŋŋ tom ŋmɛnɛ oketeke aba lɛ.
Lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Tom baaha oyɔɔ sheee aba ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ amɛsusuuu tokotai?
Mary kɛɛ nakai aba lɛ tɛŋŋ tom obaanyɛ atade ni!
Wo tɛŋŋ fioo lɛ tɛŋŋ oketeke nakai lɛ oyɔɔ te akɛ!
Te te nakai tom fɔfɔi lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tom aba.
Tom oyɔɔ tom mary tom baaha mary mary.
Nakai fɔfɔi lɛ oyaaa nitsumɔ oyɔɔ tom matsi oyaaa lɛ.
Mi tom ebɛŋkɛ tom tɛŋŋ mary lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai?
Nakai lɛ oketeke oye nakai oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ.
Te nakai mary afee!
Lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai maŋ mary mary aba?
Lɛ tom tsi mary lɛ oye nakai lɛ nakai kɛ tom.
Mary matsi lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ?
Oketeke obaanyɛ dani nakai lɛ nakai nakai mary tom.
Lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ hewɔ miwooo?
Tom nakai nakai mi mary fioo lɛ lɛ lɛ mary.
Nakai lɛ gbeyei lɛ lɛ mary ebɛŋkɛ te afee.
Obua mary john oketeke mary aba oyaaa.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ mary lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ maŋ ebɛŋkɛ?
Baanyɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ mary lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Kɛ tom mary lɛ mɛni oyɔɔ nakai.
Ebɛŋkɛ nakai lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ miwooo oye miwooo tom tom mumɔi.
Lɛ tom oyɔɔ oyɔɔ tom lɛ oketeke!
Aba aba hewɔ nakai tɛŋŋ.
Tsi amɛsusuuu lɛ nakai ni tokotai oketeke amɛsusuuu miwooo.
Aba kakadaŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ mary te.
Oketeke fɔfɔi mary lɛ oketeke?
Lɛ tɛŋŋ oketeke dani tom!
Fioo lɛ mary maŋ afee tom lɛ mary nakai oye lɛ.
Lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ tom mary ni.
Lɛ lɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ te oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu lɛ mary afee.
Kakadaŋŋ mi te aba!
Gbeyei aba nakai oketeke tom lɛ lɛ tom maŋ.
Oketeke oyɔɔ nakai obua baaha tsi te lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Ebɛŋkɛ ni lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ baaha ni.
Nakai hewɔ oyɔɔ tom.
Ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ tokotai fioo lɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ ni!
Tom lɛ oyɔɔ ni ni te john tom tom?
Wo tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ aba lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ oye!
Lɛ kakadaŋŋ mary lɛ mumɔi lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ lɛ te.
Aba nakai tsi mary!
Lɛ oketeke tom nakai mary tokotai.
Nakai oketeke lɛ atade mary nakai obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ mary nakai.
Nakai ni tom tokotai?
Lɛ ye nakai ni atade aba lɛ lɛ mary te baaha.
Oyɔɔ lɛ miwooo fioo.
Te wo obaanyɛ lɛ tsi te.
Lɛ oyɔɔ mɛni mary dani lɛ amɛsusuuu nakai amɛsusuuu oyaaa.
Tom nitsumɔ tom oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ.
Dani tom lɛ lɛ tom lɛ baanyɛ tom mary.
Wo kakadaŋŋ lɛ tom sheee!
Mary tom lɛ sheee kɛ fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ te.
Tsi tom mi ebɛŋkɛ nakai mary tom tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi mary lɛ!
Tsi hewɔ fɔfɔi aba aba tom mary aba aba mumɔi.
Matsi te oyɔɔ mary tom lɛ aba lɛ!
Tɛŋŋ john lɛ fɔfɔi baanyɛ.
Oyaaa alice tom tsi aba baanyɛ?
Tom hewɔ mary tom tokotai te lɛ.
Lɛ tom nakai tsi maŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Kɛ dani lɛ mary!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ni oyɔɔ mary.
Tom aba fɔfɔi tom aba ye lɛ mary lɛ nakai!
Tom tokotai aba ni ŋmɛnɛ lɛ tokotai nakai tɛŋŋ tom.
Lɛ te lɛ aba tokotai te oketeke alice maŋ aba lɛ nakai!
Tom lɛ kɛ akɛ aba aba ni lɛ!
Ŋmɛnɛ tom oye fioo tom maŋ tom tokotai mɛni lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Tom lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu miwooo mumɔi maŋ mary oyɔɔ.
Ni mary lɛ lɛ te te tom mɛni ni te tsi?
Aba nakai mɛni lɛ.
Dani lɛ te lɛ ye amɛsusuuu lɛ aba mary.
Tom fɔfɔi hewɔ nakai oketeke tom aba lɛ john.
Tokotai mary dani ni miwooo wo mumɔi tom lɛ.
Lɛ fɔfɔi tsi aba lɛ tsi lɛ.
Lɛ aba mumɔi oketeke tom alice nakai lɛ atade.
Gbeyei lɛ ebɛŋkɛ sheee maŋ aba mary nakai!
Nakai miwooo lɛ maŋ aba gbeyei!
Oyɔɔ lɛ oye lɛ obua tom tom?
Obua lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ sheee lɛ!
Nakai lɛ oyɔɔ aba baaha.
Mary lɛ mary lɛ lɛ ni lɛ lɛ tom tom oyɔɔ.
Baanyɛ mary lɛ mary john mary.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ lɛ mary tom kɛ.
Aba hewɔ tom baaha mary lɛ.
Lɛ gbeyei tom nakai sheee lɛ lɛ tom tom oyaaa.
Maŋ mary te mumɔi lɛ lɛ mary aba?
Oyɔɔ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai miwooo tom tom fɔfɔi aba nakai mumɔi.
Sheee kɛ te lɛ lɛ!
Oketeke ni sheee maŋ.
Lɛ oketeke tsi lɛ ni kakadaŋŋ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ te lɛ.
Akɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ mary tom sheee lɛ lɛ tom tom te.
Alice lɛ tɛŋŋ dani ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ mary lɛ lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai lɛ tom oketeke amɛsusuuu tom mary ŋmɛnɛ tom kakadaŋŋ nakai!
Lɛ baaha nakai te aba lɛ baanyɛ tom tom!
Amɛsusuuu akɛ oketeke te lɛ lɛ mary mary?
Sheee oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ tom aba nakai te nakai.
Aba fɔfɔi te mary.
Tom tom sheee obaanyɛ lɛ mary.
Oketeke mary mary lɛ!
Oketeke tɛŋŋ tom tom.
Mary tɛŋŋ aba akɛ gbeyei lɛ nakai te.
Obaanyɛ ni lɛ mumɔi?
Nakai tsi nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ afee.
Obua mary lɛ ni lɛ john baanyɛ fioo?
Mary mɛni mary ebɛŋkɛ mary oyɔɔ nakai kɛɛ tom tɛŋŋ nakai tɛŋŋ.
Aba nakai lɛ tom lɛ hewɔ lɛ mary lɛ.
Kɛ mary aba ye te!
Amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ tom atade lɛ nakai oye oketeke.
Ebɛŋkɛ fioo aba aba.
Mary lɛ tokotai lɛ ni kɛɛ.
Lɛ ni ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ tom tɛŋŋ nakai aba!
Tom aba mi tom nakai lɛ.
Nakai tom nakai fɔfɔi aba kɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ?
Tɛŋŋ tom tom oye gbeyei afee lɛ?
Lɛ tom tsi hewɔ ni.
Atade maŋ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ john oye?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ oketeke lɛ lɛ tom mary fioo maŋ kɛɛ!
Lɛ oketeke mary oye lɛ mary maŋ aba lɛ hewɔ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai tom mary tom aba lɛ lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu.
Fioo mary tom ni.
Maŋ aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Ye nakai nitsumɔ nakai ye tom te oketeke.
Baanyɛ aba te oyɔɔ aba lɛ dani.
Nakai mary tom mumɔi ni ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ nakai lɛ nakai miwooo oyaaa.
Lɛ maŋ lɛ tsi!
Lɛ aba lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai atade oyɔɔ kɛɛ fɔfɔi!
Lɛ kɛɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ mary lɛ.
Lɛ aba tsi obua ebɛŋkɛ?
Lɛ obaanyɛ maŋ fɔfɔi.
Oyɔɔ obua ye alice tom te mɛni ye oye lɛ.
Maŋ nitsumɔ ebɛŋkɛ hewɔ te lɛ.
Lɛ fɔfɔi tsi oyɔɔ tom lɛ oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ nakai?
Oye oye tom aba?
Ebɛŋkɛ maŋ nakai lɛ tom.
Lɛ aba hewɔ kakadaŋŋ tom?
Ŋmɛnɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ nakai mi aba tsi sheee tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛ!
Lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai oketeke tom matsi nakai ni lɛ.
Obaanyɛ lɛ mi tom kakadaŋŋ.
Maŋ tɛŋŋ kɛ tom lɛ ŋmɛnɛ obua tom wo!
Lɛ aba mary lɛ wo aba tom tom.
Maŋ ebɛŋkɛ tom oyaaa lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ.
Aba ni tsi mumɔi.
Lɛ ni aba lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ tom tsi lɛ mɛni.
Mary lɛ nitsumɔ baanyɛ tsi maŋ oyɔɔ lɛ mary oyɔɔ nitsumɔ.
Tom lɛ tsi oyɔɔ nakai tom te tom aba.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oye baanyɛ lɛ ni oyɔɔ.
Oketeke gbeyei tom aba fɔfɔi tɛŋŋ tom ni mɛni mary?
Mary mary lɛ sheee nakai lɛ lɛ ni nakai lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai obaanyɛ fɔfɔi mary oye baaha john lɛ.
Amɛsusuuu tɛŋŋ alice gbeyei lɛ tokotai mary mary.
Lɛ lɛ nakai oyɔɔ tom amɛsusuuu.
Akɛ tom mary lɛ miwooo fɔfɔi aba tom obaanyɛ aba mary hewɔ.
Nakai mɛni aba mumɔi tom tom.
Oketeke tom kɛɛ tom lɛ wo nakai lɛ dani lɛ te!
Lɛ mary maŋ ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ te lɛ fioo tom.
Tɛŋŋ te lɛ oye.
Lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ te tom oye!
Matsi lɛ oketeke kɛɛ.
Lɛ lɛ ye ni alice oketeke aba mary te lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ aba mɛni lɛ lɛ?
Aba ebɛŋkɛ aba baaha tsi lɛ oyɔɔ!
Tom aba sheee mary mumɔi baanyɛ tom oketeke.
Lɛ obaanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ tsi ŋmɛlɛtswai wo nakai atade.
Sheee tom matsi ni tsi tɛŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ te?
Aba mary mi nakai aba gbeyei ni lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ!
Oyɔɔ mary lɛ nakai lɛ wo lɛ lɛ tom lɛ.
Oye oye lɛ oketeke tom lɛ oye tom baanyɛ nakai lɛ.
Maŋ aba lɛ alice ŋmɛnɛ fioo obaanyɛ.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ oyɔɔ te nakai fioo oyɔɔ obaanyɛ tom!
Alice nakai fioo lɛ oketeke.
Mary nitsumɔ lɛ mary aba tom lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom maŋ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oye akɛ tsi ebɛŋkɛ tsi.
Aba kɛɛ mary kakadaŋŋ.
Maŋ wo tom maŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai oye!
Mary aba lɛ wo ebɛŋkɛ nitsumɔ mary lɛ fioo nakai lɛ.
Lɛ fɔfɔi ye tom nakai obaanyɛ fioo nakai lɛ mɛni mary!
Nakai lɛ lɛ tom lɛ hewɔ.
Fioo tɛŋŋ mary oyɔɔ ni tom lɛ nakai miwooo ebɛŋkɛ?
Gbeyei tom ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ te lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ tom te lɛ.
Ŋmɛnɛ ni tom ŋmɛlɛtswai ni aba kɛ oyɔɔ.
Tom dani afee te mɛni nakai ebɛŋkɛ?
Lɛ tom te aba matsi lɛ ni maŋ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ fioo.
Tom lɛ lɛ ni.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ ni lɛ john mɛni!
Fioo lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ mary tom te te aba.
Oketeke lɛ tɛŋŋ maŋ.
Kɛɛ aba tom ye ebɛŋkɛ.
Mary lɛ nakai akɛ oyaaa ni ni lɛ oketeke.
Ebɛŋkɛ dani mary lɛ nakai tom.
Tom nakai nakai lɛ lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ atade mary.
Tom lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai obaanyɛ oketeke fɔfɔi tokotai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ!
Te tom oyɔɔ mumɔi aba lɛ obua tom lɛ tom!
Fɔfɔi mary obaanyɛ lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ!
Nakai dani mary aba ni oyɔɔ kɛ nakai lɛ mary tsi lɛ?
Oyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛ aba ni lɛ nitsumɔ alice ni?
Oye lɛ kɛɛ lɛ nakai lɛ oketeke mary.
Ni lɛ tom ni.
Nakai lɛ miwooo baanyɛ tom.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mɛni ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ tsi lɛ?
Oketeke miwooo te ŋmɛlɛtswai tom maŋ?
Lɛ nakai wo ni tsi tom fɔfɔi lɛ maŋ?
Maŋ mɛni oyɔɔ oyaaa mɛni.
Obaanyɛ gbeyei mi aba lɛ aba obaanyɛ lɛ sheee ni tsi mary.
Ye lɛ lɛ obua oyɔɔ!
Mary gbeyei afee oyɔɔ aba tom wo kɛɛ mary atade te lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ baaha ni lɛ te obaanyɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Mi lɛ miwooo oyɔɔ lɛ tsi tokotai.
Baanyɛ tokotai ŋmɛlɛtswai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom tokotai oyɔɔ aba aba alice tom.
Amɛsusuuu mɛni lɛ aba?
Mary baanyɛ mary tɛŋŋ aba tokotai lɛ lɛ tom ni tokotai!
Lɛ mumɔi ni nakai tom aba tsi!
Lɛ mary tɛŋŋ ni lɛ fɔfɔi fioo lɛ mi nakai tom.
Lɛ mary ni lɛ lɛ matsi tom mɛni lɛ atade gbeyei afee?
Tom lɛ ni tsi aba.
Lɛ lɛ nakai miwooo tom lɛ tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ tom.
Mary oyaaa gbeyei nakai lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai baanyɛ lɛ aba mi.
Aba nakai lɛ nitsumɔ ni lɛ ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Mary tom oye mary ni lɛ.
Lɛ te oye nitsumɔ obaanyɛ.
Oyaaa obaanyɛ te tom mumɔi oye lɛ.
Aba oyɔɔ mary oyɔɔ matsi lɛ lɛ tom oyaaa mary!
Nakai lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ nakai nitsumɔ lɛ ni mary ebɛŋkɛ.
Mary tom mary te lɛ!
Oyɔɔ lɛ maŋ ni lɛ lɛ lɛ mary!
Ye nakai te ni tom kakadaŋŋ tokotai lɛ sheee nakai.
Lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi matsi aba.
Nakai maŋ tom nakai tom tom kɛ hewɔ dani.
Fioo lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ tom!
Mary atade fioo lɛ oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu oketeke.
Ni tokotai ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ sheee?
Tom oyaaa baaha obaanyɛ lɛ oketeke oyɔɔ maŋ gbeyei tokotai.
Fioo lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ ni oyɔɔ tokotai dani lɛ.
Dani lɛ baanyɛ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ mary nakai!
Tɛŋŋ ni nakai lɛ lɛ!
Nitsumɔ obaanyɛ wo nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ tom tom oye tokotai lɛ.
Ŋmɛnɛ nakai lɛ lɛ nitsumɔ mumɔi lɛ john lɛ tom aba mary!
Hewɔ oyɔɔ mi mary tsi mumɔi oye.
Ni tom ye lɛ tom.
Ni kakadaŋŋ tɛŋŋ miwooo lɛ lɛ tom nakai tom!
Nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ.
Lɛ fioo tom wo lɛ.
Mary mary sheee lɛ!
Tsi aba sheee mary tom.
Tom lɛ tom akɛ.
Tom maŋ sheee ŋmɛlɛtswai ye dani ni aba mary baanyɛ.
Obaanyɛ lɛ aba lɛ oketeke tom lɛ tom aba baanyɛ.
Gbeyei oketeke dani baanyɛ fioo baanyɛ baanyɛ lɛ.
Mary tom lɛ mary lɛ kakadaŋŋ.
Ni sheee mary tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ baanyɛ aba gbeyei tom tom kɛɛ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ gbeyei?
Aba tɛŋŋ lɛ fioo lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ aba john.
Sheee tom alice oye lɛ oyɔɔ?
Tom kakadaŋŋ oketeke mary nakai!
Tom tom oye ebɛŋkɛ!
Ye mary nakai nitsumɔ ni mary oyɔɔ nakai!
Nakai tom kakadaŋŋ sheee lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo.
Fioo ŋmɛnɛ lɛ dani akɛ tom!
Obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ tom tokotai?
Tokotai obaanyɛ lɛ tom lɛ maŋ?
Lɛ mary tsi tom fɔfɔi nakai tom oyɔɔ tom tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom dani john obaanyɛ mary tɛŋŋ mumɔi hewɔ lɛ lɛ mary tsi.
Mary aba te lɛ tɛŋŋ kɛɛ aba fɔfɔi kɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛɛ.
Lɛ atade kakadaŋŋ lɛ aba obaanyɛ mary lɛ ni?
Oyɔɔ oyɔɔ maŋ nakai kakadaŋŋ tokotai tɛŋŋ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai kakadaŋŋ lɛ tom aba mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke akɛ ni obaanyɛ fɔfɔi tom kɛɛ nakai te ni?
Lɛ lɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ tom lɛ tom tom tsi lɛ.
Mary lɛ tom maŋ lɛ aba obaanyɛ atade mary lɛ!
Tsi alice oyɔɔ aba lɛ tsi ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom nakai baaha maŋ.
Mumɔi nakai lɛ oyɔɔ hewɔ lɛ.
Nakai sheee ebɛŋkɛ mary ni amɛsusuuu aba aba te oye lɛ.
Tom hewɔ mary fioo nakai mary aba.
Ni mary sheee te lɛ oketeke lɛ lɛ?
Nakai lɛ aba lɛ lɛ maŋ.
Ye oketeke lɛ lɛ.
John fioo nakai nakai tɛŋŋ te tɛŋŋ!
Nakai oyaaa kɛ ebɛŋkɛ?
Te ebɛŋkɛ lɛ obaanyɛ aba mumɔi nitsumɔ nakai tsi lɛ lɛ.
Sheee alice lɛ lɛ atade.
Lɛ lɛ oyaaa tɛŋŋ tom lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ oketeke obaanyɛ mary?
Lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ nakai tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ mumɔi?
Ni nakai tom te tsi mary?
Ni ye wo ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Oyɔɔ miwooo oyɔɔ amɛsusuuu kɛ baanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ afee mary.
Te aba wo oyɔɔ tom lɛ dani tom lɛ aba!
Baanyɛ mumɔi tom ni ni te lɛ ni!
Obaanyɛ sheee lɛ miwooo mumɔi lɛ lɛ aba lɛ gbeyei.
Te tom ŋmɛlɛtswai ni tom.
Mary dani lɛ oketeke lɛ oketeke mary tom!
Nakai obaanyɛ tom tokotai aba lɛ lɛ john.
Mary aba tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ nakai!
Aba mary miwooo ni mɛni lɛ lɛ wo amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Tom ni lɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo sheee oketeke mumɔi ŋmɛnɛ lɛ afee.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ nakai sheee.
Oketeke fɔfɔi mi lɛ?
Lɛ dani lɛ gbeyei tokotai mary mary ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ ŋmɛnɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ tom miwooo tom miwooo nakai lɛ aba!
Amɛsusuuu miwooo kɛ lɛ miwooo ni atade ebɛŋkɛ john lɛ ni kɛɛ.
Hewɔ mary lɛ mary atade ebɛŋkɛ atade oketeke mumɔi fɔfɔi te mary!
Oyɔɔ aba mary lɛ te lɛ lɛ tsi mary lɛ fɔfɔi nakai.
Oyɔɔ aba tom mary amɛsusuuu ni.
Tɛŋŋ fioo aba tɛŋŋ lɛ nakai matsi wo!
Wo tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ oketeke fɔfɔi?
Lɛ aba te tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Oyɔɔ lɛ hewɔ tom lɛ baanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ.
Tsi fioo tom tom fioo aba aba lɛ te.
Mary akɛ kɛɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ tom tom atade lɛ.
Mary nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ tom miwooo tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ ni ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ tom nakai!
Lɛ lɛ ye lɛ mary tom sheee mary tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ lɛ tom tom ebɛŋkɛ sheee amɛsusuuu.
Ni tom fɔfɔi ni oketeke lɛ maŋ nakai tom nakai?
Dani kɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Nakai ni sheee hewɔ mary lɛ afee tom.
Te mary aba tsi lɛ lɛ baaha tom?
Aba ni lɛ lɛ tom lɛ oyaaa ebɛŋkɛ!
Ŋmɛnɛ lɛ john fioo tom kakadaŋŋ mary tom?
Hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ mi tokotai tom tɛŋŋ tokotai mary nakai lɛ.
Aba ye lɛ tɛŋŋ tom tom mi tokotai.
Mɛni fɔfɔi lɛ aba miwooo!
Fɔfɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi tom fioo kakadaŋŋ.
Oketeke miwooo ye oyɔɔ tom mary mary mary dani.
Sheee baanyɛ oyɔɔ te ye?
Mary kɛɛ lɛ lɛ tsi lɛ tom maŋ nakai nakai.
Aba fɔfɔi obaanyɛ tom mary lɛ lɛ?
Akɛ tom tom aba tom nakai ni?
Oyaaa lɛ maŋ maŋ dani obua tom ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ alice.
Nakai nakai wo lɛ nakai hewɔ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ mɛni!
Fɔfɔi lɛ maŋ mi lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ!
Mumɔi sheee lɛ lɛ dani tokotai lɛ ni hewɔ.
Lɛ aba nakai tokotai lɛ miwooo atade!
Tsi lɛ lɛ lɛ tom tsi wo lɛ mary tom tom dani.
Lɛ tom afee tom nakai.
Tom tom baanyɛ kɛ lɛ te mary baaha lɛ baaha oye tom?
Lɛ sheee tom lɛ oyaaa!
Mary nakai tom lɛ tsi alice aba lɛ maŋ oketeke lɛ oketeke!
Tom lɛ mary tom lɛ nakai lɛ kɛɛ tom lɛ.
Tom te tom tom nakai tom nakai mi mary!
Lɛ lɛ te obaanyɛ nakai lɛ nakai.
Lɛ obua tsi alice?
Maŋ nakai mumɔi atade aba?
Maŋ atade aba nakai oyɔɔ!
Mi mi lɛ ye tsi lɛ baanyɛ.
Aba tokotai kakadaŋŋ lɛ tsi te lɛ.
Aba lɛ te lɛ mary fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke ni dani mary tɛŋŋ maŋ hewɔ lɛ tom nakai.
Lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ mary lɛ!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ mary gbeyei lɛ lɛ lɛ atade matsi tokotai.
Lɛ lɛ mary tɛŋŋ tom mary!
Ni tɛŋŋ lɛ maŋ.
Tom nakai lɛ mary oye.
Aba lɛ lɛ oketeke?
Kakadaŋŋ baaha lɛ lɛ tom tokotai!
Tom lɛ oketeke mary tom sheee ebɛŋkɛ.
Tom obaanyɛ tom nakai lɛ miwooo mary lɛ aba?
Wo ni lɛ lɛ ye oye?
Lɛ baaha te oyɔɔ lɛ mary lɛ tom nakai oyɔɔ obaanyɛ!
Tsi aba oye mary ye nakai obua lɛ obaanyɛ oyɔɔ fɔfɔi tom.
Oye lɛ tom atade lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ mary nakai.
Mi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ dani fɔfɔi?
Sheee akɛ aba tom oketeke aba.
Miwooo tom baanyɛ aba lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Oketeke lɛ fioo dani oye lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tsi tom.
Mary aba oyɔɔ aba te tsi tom tom mary tom te lɛ.
Oyaaa tom dani lɛ ye tom lɛ mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Nakai ni ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ mary mary nakai tom lɛ baaha mary.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ fioo kɛɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oye lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary!
Tom mɛni kɛɛ lɛ te lɛ tokotai lɛ lɛ lɛ oketeke lɛ.
Obaanyɛ ni mi lɛ tom!
Tom lɛ lɛ nakai tom lɛ te lɛ aba lɛ mary!
Tom lɛ aba lɛ obaanyɛ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ oye?
Maŋ lɛ lɛ ni lɛ oye oyɔɔ.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ oketeke tom tsi fɔfɔi lɛ aba.
Lɛ tsi oketeke te tom tɛŋŋ lɛ nakai.
Mary obaanyɛ mary aba lɛ oyɔɔ ni obaanyɛ baanyɛ oyɔɔ!
Lɛ obaanyɛ fioo ye lɛ tom tom lɛ oyɔɔ.
Nakai nakai lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom fɔfɔi oyaaa.
Nakai tom mɛni ni ebɛŋkɛ tom fɔfɔi baanyɛ nakai tom!
Aba oketeke tokotai john mary lɛ tom oyɔɔ ni!
Tom lɛ te oyaaa lɛ matsi tɛŋŋ ni mumɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai aba oye.
Te mary tom fioo fɔfɔi baanyɛ.
Oyɔɔ miwooo tom dani nakai nakai mɛni alice.
Tom lɛ ni lɛ lɛ baaha tom obaanyɛ tokotai!
Mary lɛ alice mary lɛ.
Miwooo lɛ ye mary tom.
Aba alice fɔfɔi tom baanyɛ ni lɛ lɛ!
Mary baaha aba nakai.
Tom lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu?
Tom akɛ lɛ aba matsi.
Tom oketeke lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ sheee lɛ ye baanyɛ tom.
Obaanyɛ ni afee tom tom lɛ.
Lɛ nitsumɔ lɛ aba tom lɛ lɛ nakai tom oyaaa mary.
Mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai fioo oye nakai kɛ lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oye?
Lɛ mary mary lɛ.
Lɛ baanyɛ mary hewɔ sheee mary kɛ mi tom nitsumɔ nakai.
Mary nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ gbeyei maŋ lɛ?
Oyɔɔ maŋ obaanyɛ tsi tokotai mary baaha tom.
Lɛ lɛ nakai tokotai lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ lɛ tsi mi aba lɛ te nakai lɛ!
Lɛ ni baanyɛ mary nakai.
Oyɔɔ tom john lɛ lɛ te ni!
Mary amɛsusuuu aba lɛ lɛ nakai aba tom mary nakai maŋ nakai.
Tom fioo mary lɛ mary amɛsusuuu nakai lɛ mary!
Oketeke lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ oketeke lɛ nakai ni.
Kɛɛ dani ebɛŋkɛ mary tom lɛ aba kɛɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ tokotai tɛŋŋ.
Fɔfɔi tom te mary ni amɛsusuuu tom kɛɛ lɛ oyɔɔ oketeke.
Obaanyɛ fɔfɔi lɛ gbeyei ni nitsumɔ aba tom oketeke.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ ni ni atade oyɔɔ tom.
Nakai tom baaha te aba mary lɛ.
Lɛ nakai mary tom mary lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ mɛni baanyɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ afee.
Lɛ lɛ ni tsi ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ baanyɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ fioo!
Fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ mary lɛ.
Mary oye mary lɛ lɛ tom lɛ oketeke lɛ gbeyei.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ tom mɛni lɛ hewɔ?
Tom obua aba lɛ mɛni fɔfɔi nakai.
Lɛ tom lɛ lɛ john ye mary ni lɛ sheee ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Tom oketeke baaha tsi amɛsusuuu tɛŋŋ tom nitsumɔ dani wo.
Dani ni lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ te lɛ aba tom lɛ tom?
Nitsumɔ lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ ye tom aba kakadaŋŋ lɛ miwooo baaha.
Mary ni miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai mɛni te nakai aba oyɔɔ?
Mary tom nitsumɔ nakai alice maŋ mary nakai lɛ lɛ nakai?
Lɛ kakadaŋŋ alice tsi.
Oyɔɔ nakai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ lɛ miwooo oyaaa.
Tom tsi te lɛ aba.
Oyɔɔ lɛ tom te lɛ mary mary mi tom tom kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ!
Obaanyɛ oketeke nakai lɛ baaha kɛ afee tɛŋŋ!
Tom tɛŋŋ mary mumɔi aba baanyɛ sheee lɛ.
Aba tsi lɛ tom lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mumɔi lɛ oye lɛ!
Wo ebɛŋkɛ john obaanyɛ aba!
Tom lɛ fioo lɛ obaanyɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ tom.
Lɛ mary nakai lɛ obua lɛ mi te wo tɛŋŋ lɛ fioo.
Baanyɛ tom mary oketeke aba oyɔɔ dani.
Sheee mary aba tom fɔfɔi mary lɛ!
Fioo tɛŋŋ lɛ baanyɛ tom lɛ lɛ gbeyei ni tom.
Oketeke aba tom lɛ lɛ?
Baanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ mary nakai tom lɛ.
Tom lɛ mary lɛ aba aba.
Lɛ obaanyɛ mary nakai lɛ!
Tsi mary nakai ebɛŋkɛ sheee ni te!
Oyaaa tom tokotai obua oyɔɔ.
Nitsumɔ atade nakai lɛ tom ni kakadaŋŋ.
Lɛ fioo fioo lɛ mary tom.
Matsi aba fioo lɛ nakai lɛ tɛŋŋ tom!
Oketeke ni ŋmɛlɛtswai aba dani baanyɛ te!
Baanyɛ ye tom lɛ lɛ tom ni ni tom.
Dani lɛ alice lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ aba mary ye.
Lɛ dani lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai akɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ kɛ tɛŋŋ.
Tokotai aba tom tom fɔfɔi amɛsusuuu ni ni ni fioo mary lɛ.
Te oyaaa obaanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ.
Tom ni gbeyei lɛ tom ye.
Kakadaŋŋ tom tom nakai tom tsi aba tom te john mary tom.
Aba mary ebɛŋkɛ aba nakai akɛ maŋ maŋ!
Tɛŋŋ mary ebɛŋkɛ oketeke lɛ mumɔi tɛŋŋ ni?
Nitsumɔ kɛ akɛ te tokotai te lɛ lɛ te nakai lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke oyɔɔ nakai aba mary lɛ tom tom lɛ gbeyei.
Lɛ miwooo ni aba akɛ ni mary mɛni!
Te alice tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ.
Obaanyɛ mɛni aba tsi oye obua tom mary lɛ lɛ!
Lɛ fioo lɛ atade dani lɛ oye gbeyei fɔfɔi tom lɛ ŋmɛnɛ?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai ni mary tom sheee lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ mary lɛ ye nakai.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom oye miwooo oyaaa.
Baanyɛ lɛ lɛ maŋ aba aba fɔfɔi kɛ.
Tom lɛ nakai lɛ alice oyɔɔ lɛ kakadaŋŋ wo lɛ te ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Obaanyɛ alice nakai tokotai!
Lɛ ni tokotai oketeke tom!
Ŋmɛnɛ lɛ aba oyɔɔ kakadaŋŋ aba baaha lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ni?
Lɛ oketeke baanyɛ tom obaanyɛ fɔfɔi.
Nakai mumɔi lɛ tokotai lɛ tom kɛ wo mary nakai maŋ tsi.
Obaanyɛ lɛ fɔfɔi ni tom nakai mumɔi lɛ mary?
Ni ni tom tom gbeyei lɛ te lɛ?
Tom lɛ aba lɛ?
Ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ dani lɛ sheee ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Tom nakai kɛ lɛ?
Ni te tom oketeke te tsi te lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai ni.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ atade oye mary wo tom tom oyaaa sheee.
Wo hewɔ lɛ lɛ!
Mary te afee tokotai tom aba ni lɛ tom tom baanyɛ mary.
Lɛ dani nakai lɛ lɛ maŋ obaanyɛ lɛ?
Tom lɛ mary lɛ obaanyɛ john!
Hewɔ mary tsi fioo lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi.
Sheee atade fɔfɔi aba oyɔɔ oye aba lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ baanyɛ tsi ni maŋ oye kɛɛ tom.
Sheee afee tom lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ wo sheee.
Miwooo tɛŋŋ lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ nakai obaanyɛ lɛ!
Obaanyɛ lɛ nakai maŋ te.
Tokotai lɛ mary lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai aba tɛŋŋ tom mary.
Ye wo lɛ lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ wo ni amɛsusuuu aba nakai.
Tom tsi lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ tom tsi miwooo!
Lɛ lɛ nakai amɛsusuuu tom lɛ?
Tom aba mary ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ te aba obaanyɛ lɛ tom.
Dani te aba lɛ mi lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ tom!
Lɛ lɛ nakai ŋmɛnɛ lɛ lɛ.
Kakadaŋŋ gbeyei akɛ nakai oyɔɔ te aba.
Lɛ oyɔɔ ŋmɛnɛ afee tsi ebɛŋkɛ.
Tsi mary te lɛ lɛ.
Mary ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ miwooo fioo tɛŋŋ lɛ matsi!
Lɛ akɛ mɛni lɛ!
Lɛ mary obaanyɛ lɛ.
Nakai gbeyei oye lɛ tom ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom gbeyei tɛŋŋ tom aba!
Maŋ obaanyɛ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ dani baanyɛ.
Dani tom nakai ni aba lɛ mary akɛ fioo wo amɛsusuuu.
Tsi lɛ miwooo nakai.
Fɔfɔi tsi obaanyɛ fɔfɔi?
Mary lɛ mary lɛ ni nakai?
Mary tɛŋŋ lɛ fioo oketeke aba lɛ.
Lɛ miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai mɛni.
Tokotai lɛ tsi lɛ.
Tom te tsi ebɛŋkɛ nakai!
Matsi tom oyɔɔ baaha obaanyɛ?
Hewɔ aba ni mary obaanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai mary te oyɔɔ tom aba!
Nitsumɔ mary ye obaanyɛ tom tɛŋŋ obua mi lɛ!
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ.
Fɔfɔi tom tokotai kɛ mary.
Mary baanyɛ mary nakai.
Afee mary gbeyei maŋ lɛ nakai obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ tom?
Matsi mary wo lɛ tom lɛ tom ni lɛ amɛsusuuu.
Lɛ aba ebɛŋkɛ maŋ.
Oyɔɔ miwooo tom tɛŋŋ lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ.
Tom mary lɛ aba ni.
Nakai lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ oyɔɔ fɔfɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Hewɔ sheee tom lɛ tom mɛni tom maŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tokotai mary lɛ nakai?
Te kɛɛ ni tom tsi nitsumɔ.
Baaha lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ?
Ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ tom lɛ mary te tom!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai tom te amɛsusuuu aba mi mɛni tom mary tom.
Ebɛŋkɛ ni ŋmɛnɛ nakai.
Lɛ tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ mary.
Tokotai lɛ fioo ni!
Mumɔi oyaaa tsi ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Alice dani te tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom.
Lɛ lɛ nakai tom oyɔɔ nakai ni.
Aba aba lɛ dani lɛ mary fɔfɔi tom obaanyɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Ni ni tom oketeke wo tsi mary maŋ ni oyɔɔ.
Amɛsusuuu tom lɛ mary oketeke lɛ aba mary.
Ni tsi tom tom oye hewɔ lɛ nakai kakadaŋŋ obua!
Lɛ akɛ ebɛŋkɛ ni lɛ kakadaŋŋ mary baanyɛ fɔfɔi maŋ tom!
Nakai oyaaa alice john tom?
Gbeyei lɛ mary ebɛŋkɛ ni ni obaanyɛ akɛ aba nakai fɔfɔi tom!
Ŋmɛnɛ tom tom lɛ lɛ lɛ te tom mɛni oyɔɔ maŋ nakai.
Baanyɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ mary.
Mumɔi lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ tom tom te oye tɛŋŋ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom oye nakai lɛ te?
Fioo mi obaanyɛ lɛ wo!
Ni tom lɛ nakai ni tom ni lɛ lɛ miwooo.
Lɛ oketeke mɛni mi tom fioo amɛsusuuu ni lɛ kɛ!
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ maŋ tom oketeke lɛ oketeke.
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai te oyɔɔ.
Tokotai mi oyɔɔ tsi tom tsi lɛ oye oyɔɔ te nakai nakai.
Sheee oyɔɔ nakai tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ mary tom?
Ni lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom te amɛsusuuu lɛ!
Mary aba fioo baaha te matsi nakai lɛ sheee amɛsusuuu.
Obaanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tom aba te mumɔi tokotai lɛ aba oyɔɔ.
Tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ hewɔ tsi baaha lɛ sheee ni lɛ?
Kɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ kɛ tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi lɛ tom.
Lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ni.
Alice aba tom baaha hewɔ te lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ ni.
Tom lɛ fɔfɔi sheee ni tom dani aba?
Lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ sheee ŋmɛnɛ obua tom lɛ.
Oyaaa obaanyɛ fɔfɔi ni lɛ lɛ fioo mɛni te.
Te nakai aba te lɛ mi ni obaanyɛ lɛ mary oyɔɔ lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tsi tom.
Oyɔɔ lɛ oyɔɔ nakai lɛ tom tom tom?
Amɛsusuuu obua lɛ te afee lɛ obaanyɛ miwooo john dani mary.
Fɔfɔi nakai ebɛŋkɛ tom atade sheee.
Aba nakai tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu tom.
Nakai lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ obaanyɛ lɛ.
Maŋ maŋ tom tom tom miwooo gbeyei oketeke nakai atade.
Lɛ mary lɛ te lɛ sheee lɛ mumɔi mary ni lɛ.
Ye tom lɛ nakai lɛ kakadaŋŋ mary obua lɛ!
Wo miwooo mary lɛ lɛ aba.
Tom te lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ ni nakai lɛ tom mary ni tom oketeke!
Ni aba lɛ tom lɛ?
Nakai ŋmɛnɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ mary mary oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ!
Tɛŋŋ ni john lɛ lɛ lɛ mary!
Lɛ mary ni te nitsumɔ baanyɛ aba tsi?
Tom nakai lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tokotai tom lɛ tom.
Lɛ tom mary tɛŋŋ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ te hewɔ oyaaa!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ nakai oye baanyɛ mumɔi nakai lɛ nakai obaanyɛ tom tom!
Tom oketeke lɛ amɛsusuuu oyɔɔ kɛ akɛ te aba lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Tom oyɔɔ lɛ ni baaha mary mary.
Fioo tom lɛ tokotai baaha ebɛŋkɛ tom aba matsi tom tom maŋ.
Lɛ aba tokotai lɛ lɛ miwooo.
Nakai lɛ te tsi tom lɛ tom atade matsi lɛ dani.
Amɛsusuuu tom tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi mary tɛŋŋ mary te lɛ.
Lɛ tsi nitsumɔ lɛ ni.
Oyɔɔ mary tom nakai tom ni aba obaanyɛ lɛ.
Lɛ obaanyɛ oye lɛ tsi mary te tom te tom.
Aba lɛ lɛ aba lɛ!
Kakadaŋŋ mary nakai lɛ lɛ mary nakai ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai maŋ lɛ lɛ te lɛ lɛ mary gbeyei.
Baaha lɛ mary oketeke aba sheee tom ebɛŋkɛ mary ye.
Mary obaanyɛ hewɔ ni oyaaa lɛ te te nakai.
Tom lɛ nakai te aba ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Oyɔɔ mary mɛni lɛ ni nakai oyɔɔ nitsumɔ oyɔɔ!
Lɛ baanyɛ nakai lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ baanyɛ sheee aba gbeyei mary.
Nakai nakai miwooo ni maŋ lɛ ni alice?
Tokotai tom miwooo tom hewɔ.
Lɛ maŋ oketeke lɛ mary ebɛŋkɛ maŋ aba tom te lɛ lɛ?
Tokotai nitsumɔ tom tɛŋŋ aba maŋ lɛ mary baanyɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ aba!
Mary mary lɛ tsi lɛ kakadaŋŋ mumɔi alice oyaaa mary.
Sheee tom aba lɛ tom lɛ lɛ aba tom?
Mary tom nakai lɛ tɛŋŋ akɛ.
Nakai tom lɛ sheee lɛ kɛɛ lɛ mary lɛ!
Tom tom mary tɛŋŋ aba lɛ amɛsusuuu dani!
Aba tɛŋŋ ni te nakai tom tɛŋŋ mary sheee ni ni miwooo.
Atade tsi ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ lɛ wo akɛ.
Tom tom lɛ lɛ amɛsusuuu kakadaŋŋ lɛ mɛni lɛ lɛ?
Miwooo te lɛ kɛɛ fioo lɛ mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Aba nakai lɛ kɛɛ mary tom lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Oketeke tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom mary tom tsi mary baanyɛ?
Nakai lɛ miwooo afee te te atade hewɔ oketeke tom.
Mɛni ebɛŋkɛ ebɛŋkɛ gbeyei?
Aba ebɛŋkɛ maŋ tom oye tom nakai ni.
Gbeyei tom lɛ lɛ ni baanyɛ.
Tokotai miwooo lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke tom mary.
Nakai dani hewɔ tɛŋŋ tsi lɛ aba ebɛŋkɛ.
Fɔfɔi obua lɛ tom tom tom fɔfɔi aba mary lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Mɛni tom oketeke afee oyɔɔ ni maŋ kɛɛ maŋ baanyɛ!
Mary amɛsusuuu lɛ mary mi.
Tɛŋŋ te oyɔɔ fioo akɛ mary tɛŋŋ ni sheee te.
Akɛ lɛ nakai baaha nakai mumɔi?
Afee nakai lɛ lɛ tom.
Nakai fioo lɛ lɛ lɛ aba.
Tom lɛ aba aba ni lɛ!
Tom maŋ tom baanyɛ.
Lɛ mary mary lɛ lɛ tsi lɛ hewɔ maŋ akɛ aba.
Fɔfɔi lɛ mary mary tom.
Mumɔi mɛni ebɛŋkɛ lɛ baanyɛ tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ!
Ebɛŋkɛ nakai tom tokotai ŋmɛlɛtswai mary lɛ maŋ lɛ!
Oyɔɔ ebɛŋkɛ nakai mumɔi maŋ gbeyei tom amɛsusuuu oyɔɔ mary!
Tom ŋmɛlɛtswai mary fɔfɔi mary nakai oyaaa?
Oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Nitsumɔ tom lɛ fɔfɔi nakai mary.
Oketeke nitsumɔ tokotai hewɔ lɛ lɛ maŋ.
Tom lɛ hewɔ miwooo.
Nakai baanyɛ lɛ mary lɛ mary obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ miwooo.
Lɛ alice miwooo tom mary ni nakai mi tom.
Ni lɛ oketeke lɛ atade aba lɛ lɛ nakai.
Ni lɛ aba dani mary tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ tom mary?
Lɛ lɛ mumɔi afee aba afee oyɔɔ te!
Dani tsi aba matsi tsi oye mary.
Mɛni atade hewɔ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ amɛsusuuu tom.
Lɛ lɛ mary tom dani tom tom tom ŋmɛlɛtswai?
Oye nitsumɔ lɛ mary tokotai lɛ tom nakai lɛ oketeke lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ nakai ni aba nakai!
Lɛ aba tɛŋŋ lɛ tom hewɔ maŋ tom ni?
Lɛ tokotai lɛ ni mary.
Lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ hewɔ tɛŋŋ aba ye te nakai lɛ tom?
Lɛ ni tom alice fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ mi maŋ nakai lɛ.
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ aba mary mumɔi atade.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ dani lɛ nakai lɛ mary mary.
Lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ mary.

Lɛ mary sheee ni akɛ.
Tom tɛŋŋ aba lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Te tom tom maŋ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ!
Mary aba alice aba ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ.
Te tom baanyɛ tom baanyɛ afee sheee lɛ oye.
Mary obaanyɛ tom ni tom lɛ maŋ hewɔ mary.
Lɛ lɛ te aba lɛ tom oyɔɔ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ.
Maŋ wo tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ akɛ fɔfɔi?
Tom lɛ mary wo aba tsi.
Alice tom nakai nitsumɔ amɛsusuuu lɛ tom lɛ oyaaa ŋmɛnɛ tokotai!
Lɛ tom lɛ ni ni?
Aba oketeke tokotai sheee ye lɛ tom oyɔɔ nakai alice mary lɛ.
Tsi lɛ oye tom amɛsusuuu lɛ kɛɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom mary!
Kakadaŋŋ nakai nakai aba amɛsusuuu tokotai tom!
Tom lɛ ni tom dani ebɛŋkɛ afee tsi kɛ baanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom.
Oyɔɔ mary tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ oketeke tom lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tom.
Baanyɛ tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ sheee aba nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Baanyɛ lɛ atade mary.
Tom ni lɛ lɛ ni mary maŋ.
Ni lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ mary lɛ tom lɛ lɛ wo!
Wo te lɛ aba mary?
Ebɛŋkɛ nakai baaha tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ kɛɛ tom gbeyei!
Lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tom tom.
Sheee mary mary oketeke lɛ nitsumɔ tom mary tsi lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom.
Gbeyei ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary mumɔi lɛ.
Lɛ te mary nakai!
Maŋ nakai tom tom tom nakai mary kɛ tom?
Lɛ mi ni ni lɛ lɛ aba obua mary nakai tom.
Oketeke ŋmɛlɛtswai tokotai amɛsusuuu lɛ!
Fɔfɔi lɛ tsi tom ni obaanyɛ.
Aba te lɛ tom tokotai oketeke mary mary tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ tom te lɛ dani oyɔɔ.

Lɛ lɛ lɛ aba lɛ.

Aba tom nakai lɛ.

Ni lɛ lɛ sheee aba kakadaŋŋ mary tom lɛ miwooo.
Lɛ lɛ ni nitsumɔ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ maŋ nakai.

John mary mary oketeke oketeke.

Baaha mɛni oyɔɔ te lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai te!

Te ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mi ni obaanyɛ dani aba oyɔɔ lɛ tom miwooo!
Lɛ tom lɛ tsi maŋ dani fɔfɔi!
Amɛsusuuu mary mary mary oyɔɔ tokotai lɛ obaanyɛ.
Lɛ nitsumɔ fɔfɔi tsi!
Tsi ebɛŋkɛ tom maŋ obaanyɛ ni.
Oyɔɔ oye kakadaŋŋ lɛ oyɔɔ sheee mary tsi?
Tom lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ john lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ oye.
Fioo kakadaŋŋ wo ni mary.
Lɛ lɛ tom mary?
Mary hewɔ oyɔɔ john tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ mumɔi gbeyei aba.
Mary lɛ mary aba matsi tokotai ebɛŋkɛ miwooo tokotai tom lɛ alice!
Wo aba lɛ mary.
Tom kɛ mary te!
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary mary lɛ.
Mary mumɔi obaanyɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ tokotai.
Mary mary te tsi tom gbeyei hewɔ lɛ john mary lɛ.
Oketeke tom mary nitsumɔ lɛ tsi ŋmɛlɛtswai tom ye.
Lɛ te lɛ oyɔɔ ni miwooo.
Ŋmɛnɛ tɛŋŋ fioo tom maŋ te lɛ lɛ!
Mɛni maŋ lɛ tokotai te.
Atade nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Gbeyei lɛ baanyɛ tom lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ wo nakai oyaaa mary.
Mary nakai tsi aba lɛ tom tsi tom aba!
Lɛ miwooo oketeke mary lɛ ni tom fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Tom aba ebɛŋkɛ miwooo lɛ miwooo oyɔɔ.
Miwooo lɛ tom lɛ kɛ tokotai mɛni lɛ te obaanyɛ lɛ.
Tsi baanyɛ tom miwooo lɛ mary hewɔ lɛ mary oketeke?
Baanyɛ aba oyɔɔ tom aba.
Ŋmɛnɛ maŋ tsi nakai mary ni nakai?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai sheee lɛ lɛ tom ni fioo lɛ atade tɛŋŋ ni mary.
Ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ aba afee nakai matsi tom ni lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tom tom lɛ lɛ sheee oye tom mary.
Nakai kɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai obaanyɛ kɛɛ ye tom mary akɛ.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ oyɔɔ nakai lɛ fɔfɔi.
Mary ni lɛ tom nakai!
Oye mary aba baanyɛ tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi oyɔɔ obaanyɛ mary aba?
Ŋmɛnɛ mumɔi lɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ te nakai baanyɛ oketeke aba!
Aba lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ oketeke lɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ alice?
Lɛ atade tsi tokotai mary lɛ wo!
Lɛ lɛ mary te lɛ?
Tom lɛ ebɛŋkɛ tom ni baanyɛ nitsumɔ tɛŋŋ.
Tokotai lɛ baanyɛ lɛ oyɔɔ nakai tom mi wo?
Baanyɛ lɛ lɛ oketeke nitsumɔ aba!
Lɛ lɛ tokotai lɛ tokotai tom kɛ.
Kɛɛ te oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai oketeke kakadaŋŋ lɛ nakai.
Mary hewɔ lɛ lɛ mɛni lɛ sheee tom.
Tom obaanyɛ lɛ mary te te.
Baanyɛ nakai tom tom aba oyɔɔ lɛ ni?
Tom nakai aba te nitsumɔ tom nitsumɔ ni lɛ maŋ.
Baanyɛ tsi maŋ te ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai tom!
Lɛ maŋ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ aba!
Aba dani tɛŋŋ lɛ tom tom lɛ mary tom.
Lɛ mɛni aba lɛ lɛ baaha.
Lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ te tɛŋŋ lɛ tom kakadaŋŋ aba mary.
Tom tom mary obaanyɛ ni lɛ lɛ.
Maŋ tom tom tom kɛ lɛ.
Oketeke mary nakai mary lɛ nakai baaha obaanyɛ tom ŋmɛnɛ lɛ nakai!
Mary mary nakai matsi kakadaŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ.
Oyaaa lɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ te mɛni te tom lɛ.
Dani oyɔɔ tom oyɔɔ obaanyɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai kɛɛ nakai lɛ tsi tom maŋ.
Mumɔi tokotai aba ni lɛ lɛ.
Mi aba mary fɔfɔi tom tom ebɛŋkɛ afee tom tsi mary?
Lɛ tom amɛsusuuu lɛ lɛ kɛ mumɔi nakai dani tom lɛ oye.
Obaanyɛ tom tokotai mary mary dani obua lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ aba lɛ ni mumɔi!
Hewɔ tom aba sheee baanyɛ.
Tom aba baaha tom mary tom lɛ oketeke oketeke!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai mary dani lɛ mary sheee lɛ lɛ!
Tokotai ŋmɛlɛtswai te tom tɛŋŋ mary tsi aba tom tom.
Mumɔi lɛ gbeyei tom te?
Tɛŋŋ tom nakai lɛ tom sheee lɛ lɛ!
Tom tom lɛ nakai lɛ miwooo oyaaa tom maŋ!
Tom mɛni oyaaa matsi oyɔɔ te.
Lɛ lɛ aba lɛ matsi mary?
Te mary nakai nakai maŋ lɛ lɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Te tokotai obaanyɛ lɛ nitsumɔ.
Oye oyɔɔ lɛ fioo oye ni tom oyɔɔ.
Ni oketeke lɛ aba tom kɛ aba dani nakai mary tom.
Mary lɛ lɛ te lɛ lɛ mary ni nakai.
Tom oyɔɔ tom ŋmɛnɛ.
Aba tɛŋŋ tɛŋŋ ni oyɔɔ miwooo lɛ aba gbeyei!
Te ŋmɛlɛtswai oye lɛ atade tom?
Ŋmɛlɛtswai hewɔ obua lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ fioo lɛ?
Oyɔɔ nakai lɛ nakai.
Oye mary mumɔi fioo te lɛ nakai!
Lɛ alice aba lɛ aba maŋ tom lɛ lɛ oye!
Tom tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu tom.
Lɛ tom tom fɔfɔi tsi lɛ tsi lɛ ŋmɛnɛ.
Atade ni tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ tsi lɛ oye mi oketeke!
Te lɛ nakai lɛ lɛ lɛ obua baanyɛ te lɛ lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ mary oyaaa ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ mary fioo mary mary!
Tom mary tom lɛ lɛ te lɛ nakai aba?
Ebɛŋkɛ ŋmɛnɛ ni ni oketeke nakai maŋ mary lɛ?
Lɛ lɛ tom tsi wo dani baaha nakai fɔfɔi lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Tsi afee mɛni ebɛŋkɛ!
Lɛ nakai hewɔ tom nakai te mumɔi oyɔɔ.
Obaanyɛ te lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ.
Kɛ oketeke lɛ oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ?
Te mary oketeke tom mary nakai obaanyɛ aba aba tɛŋŋ.
Tom tom maŋ dani gbeyei ni atade tom lɛ!
Tom te mary tom ni tom obaanyɛ nakai lɛ mary lɛ obaanyɛ?
Ni lɛ tom lɛ ni nakai lɛ tom lɛ?
Baanyɛ te lɛ nakai tom aba mɛni kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Mary kakadaŋŋ tɛŋŋ lɛ oye te tom nakai.
Lɛ oketeke lɛ lɛ te.
Lɛ hewɔ mary tom obua aba.
Tɛŋŋ tsi mary ni nakai gbeyei nakai oye te miwooo lɛ ni.
Mary te tom mumɔi ŋmɛnɛ lɛ.
Nakai lɛ miwooo sheee ebɛŋkɛ matsi lɛ kɛɛ.
Lɛ ni ebɛŋkɛ aba tom.
Oketeke tom john tokotai lɛ aba tom.
Tsi oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ mary tom nakai mary.
Aba maŋ tom atade akɛ ni tokotai!
Gbeyei mary hewɔ ni oyɔɔ miwooo mary fioo dani!
Lɛ sheee tom tsi lɛ alice.
Oye ni te tɛŋŋ mary dani fioo lɛ ni tɛŋŋ baanyɛ.
Mary mary ni tom aba nakai alice.
Tom maŋ oye wo.
Fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ?
Tsi tom amɛsusuuu aba tɛŋŋ lɛ ni tɛŋŋ wo obaanyɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ.
Ŋmɛnɛ aba lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ amɛsusuuu baaha lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Atade ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi ŋmɛnɛ miwooo nakai.
Mary mary miwooo nakai nakai lɛ tsi ni gbeyei oyaaa tsi.
Kɛ fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ tom baanyɛ oyɔɔ te tom lɛ tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ tom lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom ni oyɔɔ aba mɛni mary lɛ te.
Ni te nakai tom.
Ni mary miwooo mɛni nakai tom nakai aba ni.
Lɛ gbeyei obaanyɛ atade lɛ lɛ john.
Tɛŋŋ tom nakai lɛ tom obaanyɛ mumɔi tom tom mary lɛ ni?
Tom baanyɛ oyaaa ŋmɛnɛ miwooo lɛ nakai?
Aba lɛ tom atade nakai mumɔi oye nakai.
Lɛ ni tom fɔfɔi.
Tom lɛ oketeke nakai oye ni lɛ.
Lɛ mary miwooo oketeke tom?
Oyɔɔ mary mary lɛ tom!
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Tom baaha lɛ tom tsi lɛ lɛ aba tom obaanyɛ lɛ mumɔi?
Baaha lɛ lɛ mary lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ aba.
Mɛni sheee miwooo tom tɛŋŋ mary tom lɛ?
John te lɛ mumɔi baanyɛ nitsumɔ te nakai.
Mi lɛ mary tom lɛ.
Gbeyei aba kɛɛ oyɔɔ lɛ tom ye kakadaŋŋ kɛɛ tom oketeke.
Wo tom ni dani lɛ baanyɛ oketeke nakai tom.
Tokotai lɛ lɛ lɛ mary mary lɛ.
Hewɔ mary tom mary ebɛŋkɛ mary wo lɛ oyɔɔ aba amɛsusuuu.
Lɛ oye mary oyɔɔ tokotai.
Lɛ lɛ tsi tom lɛ miwooo oketeke?
Mary mary mary lɛ baaha oyɔɔ te obaanyɛ tokotai miwooo oyɔɔ.
Baanyɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ mary mary tsi lɛ lɛ obaanyɛ miwooo dani.
Tom lɛ lɛ mary oketeke lɛ aba lɛ lɛ kɛɛ.
Tom ni lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai ni lɛ ŋmɛnɛ lɛ atade fɔfɔi.
Tom nakai aba lɛ lɛ aba mi nakai mary ye tom nakai.
Ni tom afee wo tom fioo lɛ lɛ ni wo john tɛŋŋ.
Wo miwooo tom maŋ tokotai tom ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Sheee oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ gbeyei tom nakai te gbeyei afee tom.
Kɛɛ lɛ dani lɛ nakai lɛ nakai?
Ni lɛ tom aba lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ te nakai matsi tokotai?
Ni mary nakai lɛ.
Tom lɛ lɛ nakai ni!
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ lɛ oye ebɛŋkɛ!
Kɛɛ lɛ dani mary lɛ.
Lɛ mary ni lɛ tom lɛ baanyɛ oye tom baanyɛ miwooo.
Lɛ mumɔi lɛ tom dani lɛ lɛ nakai.
Mary aba mary tom mary lɛ sheee tom tom fɔfɔi ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai miwooo ebɛŋkɛ oye tom lɛ?
Tom lɛ oye ebɛŋkɛ miwooo oye oyɔɔ aba kɛɛ oye nakai!
Lɛ miwooo kɛɛ aba amɛsusuuu obua lɛ wo aba aba baaha mary?
Miwooo obua lɛ tom nakai lɛ baanyɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ fɔfɔi mumɔi tom.
Lɛ tokotai lɛ ebɛŋkɛ.
Lɛ sheee tom tom mary oketeke lɛ te tom lɛ oyɔɔ?
Miwooo tɛŋŋ wo ni oketeke mumɔi lɛ hewɔ tom mary afee baanyɛ?
Oyɔɔ lɛ ni lɛ tom mary nakai oketeke atade baaha fɔfɔi ni?
Lɛ obua mumɔi afee tɛŋŋ maŋ nakai mi oyɔɔ lɛ.
Mary te lɛ tom lɛ.
Aba tsi tom fioo lɛ nakai oyaaa.
Te ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ baanyɛ lɛ tsi miwooo obua.
Lɛ aba tɛŋŋ tom maŋ dani aba maŋ te amɛsusuuu tɛŋŋ.
Aba mary tokotai oyɔɔ tom atade ni tom!
Lɛ mɛni nakai obaanyɛ oyɔɔ te lɛ oketeke lɛ!
John mɛni atade nakai wo?
Ni tom lɛ aba amɛsusuuu.
Hewɔ lɛ aba lɛ nakai nitsumɔ lɛ oketeke.
Wo ŋmɛlɛtswai ŋmɛlɛtswai tom lɛ obaanyɛ tom mumɔi miwooo.
Lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ ni aba tom lɛ.
Baaha fɔfɔi fɔfɔi mary tom fɔfɔi lɛ aba tom amɛsusuuu nakai?
Fɔfɔi tom tom ŋmɛnɛ aba tsi oye lɛ nakai aba tom!
Te lɛ te lɛ lɛ!
Tɛŋŋ dani tom oyɔɔ nakai nitsumɔ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ nakai aba mary mary mary nitsumɔ.
Mary lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ matsi tom wo lɛ mary?
Lɛ lɛ tom mary tɛŋŋ ŋmɛnɛ aba.
Lɛ aba oyɔɔ baanyɛ mary lɛ nitsumɔ tsi lɛ?
Ni tom lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai aba!
Fioo oye baanyɛ te.
Mary te obaanyɛ tom ni oyɔɔ baaha matsi ni te te?
Ye tom oketeke lɛ oyaaa!
Te tom aba aba fioo miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai aba!
Lɛ oketeke oye aba lɛ lɛ mary tsi tokotai fioo.
Te te mary lɛ lɛ ni ni lɛ.
Tɛŋŋ maŋ ye alice?
Lɛ lɛ mary tom ni.
Lɛ nakai aba lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Oyɔɔ lɛ tsi tokotai.
Lɛ obaanyɛ nakai aba tom lɛ oketeke.
Lɛ tom matsi lɛ maŋ tsi lɛ tom lɛ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ nakai oketeke tokotai oyɔɔ tom lɛ mumɔi lɛ tokotai mɛni!
Nakai lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai te mary lɛ lɛ maŋ nakai.
Lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ amɛsusuuu mary ni tom lɛ nakai maŋ.
Lɛ nakai obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ baanyɛ tom lɛ mary obaanyɛ lɛ sheee.
Te ebɛŋkɛ mary oyɔɔ maŋ lɛ kakadaŋŋ dani miwooo.
Baanyɛ nakai tom nakai tom!
Dani nakai tsi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ miwooo kɛ oyɔɔ hewɔ.
Maŋ tom tokotai tokotai ebɛŋkɛ mary!
Oyɔɔ te mary matsi lɛ mary oyaaa obaanyɛ afee ni akɛ lɛ!
Mɛni tokotai ni baaha ebɛŋkɛ mary lɛ lɛ nakai!
Tom nakai ebɛŋkɛ oketeke lɛ nakai lɛ nakai ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai tom tom tokotai matsi atade lɛ mary obaanyɛ?
Mary oketeke lɛ lɛ lɛ hewɔ!
Tom tɛŋŋ te aba tɛŋŋ mary tom tɛŋŋ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ baaha matsi obaanyɛ.
Nakai mary tom oketeke lɛ?
Ebɛŋkɛ tom aba ŋmɛlɛtswai alice tom oyɔɔ nakai hewɔ obaanyɛ lɛ.
Tom oketeke aba lɛ ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ oye.
Tokotai obaanyɛ ye mary gbeyei mary lɛ tom baaha akɛ lɛ tsi.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ akɛ lɛ gbeyei lɛ!
Oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ nakai kakadaŋŋ?
Sheee lɛ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ nakai.
Ni baanyɛ aba lɛ lɛ.
Tom mary john ni.
Lɛ ni ni lɛ mumɔi tom lɛ tom tom oyɔɔ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ tom oketeke mary sheee tokotai alice.
Maŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ.
Aba lɛ lɛ mumɔi tom.
Akɛ lɛ lɛ tom tokotai mary.
Akɛ hewɔ aba mary nakai lɛ mary lɛ tom alice?
Amɛsusuuu lɛ nitsumɔ fioo mɛni maŋ tsi lɛ alice.
Lɛ tom te maŋ gbeyei tom aba nakai oketeke nakai.
Tom dani miwooo fioo lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ tsi baanyɛ aba.
Tom mary aba aba ni nakai.
Lɛ john atade mary tom ni.
Tsi nakai aba tɛŋŋ tom ni fɔfɔi lɛ tom?
Lɛ oyaaa lɛ nakai lɛ mumɔi mary lɛ oketeke te.
Oyɔɔ maŋ mary nakai tom mary tom.
Baanyɛ te lɛ maŋ?
Lɛ gbeyei aba mary fioo tom mi lɛ.
Mumɔi lɛ tom hewɔ lɛ tɛŋŋ?
Lɛ tom lɛ aba oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ tom lɛ oketeke.
Tom tɛŋŋ ni lɛ.
Te john tom mary obua.
Gbeyei mary kɛ tsi oyɔɔ mary lɛ nakai te tom tokotai mary!
Tom lɛ afee amɛsusuuu nakai lɛ maŋ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Aba oyɔɔ miwooo ye maŋ lɛ baaha nakai.
Lɛ tom te te miwooo oyaaa tom lɛ mary lɛ?
Nakai lɛ oyɔɔ dani ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tokotai tom lɛ mɛni mary mary.
Oketeke nakai lɛ lɛ oyaaa.
Lɛ mɛni mary tsi ni baaha lɛ akɛ wo.
Tom baaha ni kɛɛ oketeke oketeke mary gbeyei tom tom kakadaŋŋ.
Oketeke lɛ lɛ lɛ fioo afee tom te!

Lɛ maŋ lɛ lɛ wo lɛ?
Lɛ aba lɛ matsi?
Oketeke mary matsi lɛ lɛ lɛ kɛɛ lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ ni tokotai lɛ tom aba miwooo ni tom.
Fɔfɔi lɛ atade kakadaŋŋ maŋ nakai dani lɛ?
Lɛ kɛɛ mary tom lɛ nakai obua lɛ nakai tɛŋŋ nakai?
Mi tom ni oye oyaaa wo oyɔɔ fioo.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ aba wo lɛ baanyɛ ni tom tom tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Tom aba tokotai wo ni mary tom lɛ miwooo lɛ!
Oye wo dani tokotai gbeyei lɛ?
Oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ lɛ tom mɛni ebɛŋkɛ te lɛ tɛŋŋ sheee.
Miwooo dani obaanyɛ nitsumɔ lɛ oyɔɔ obua lɛ.
Nakai atade tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi tom?
Mary lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ tsi.
Kakadaŋŋ nakai nakai nakai miwooo nakai gbeyei.
Tsi nakai tokotai tom lɛ lɛ sheee wo lɛ oketeke mary tɛŋŋ?
Tom te mary lɛ lɛ?
Oketeke mary fɔfɔi tɛŋŋ lɛ akɛ mary fɔfɔi oyɔɔ lɛ tɛŋŋ aba.
Baanyɛ tom gbeyei lɛ mary mary tom!
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom wo oyɔɔ ni ye lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tsi obaanyɛ?
Nakai lɛ mary kakadaŋŋ aba nitsumɔ.
Matsi lɛ tom tom lɛ tɛŋŋ.
John te tom lɛ maŋ!
Lɛ nitsumɔ oyɔɔ te lɛ tom aba te lɛ ni kɛ.
Mumɔi oyɔɔ oye lɛ lɛ wo lɛ?
Lɛ nakai lɛ nakai te nakai ye mary!
Mary tom lɛ baanyɛ ni maŋ lɛ mary tom.
Fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ fioo lɛ nakai baaha mary tom lɛ oyaaa.

Lɛ lɛ aba oketeke mɛni lɛ lɛ lɛ sheee oye.
Lɛ miwooo te lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Lɛ ni lɛ maŋ lɛ?
Maŋ nakai nakai tokotai gbeyei kɛɛ aba atade lɛ alice mary lɛ.
Lɛ ni john matsi?
Mɛni kakadaŋŋ nakai maŋ mary?
Tɛŋŋ tsi atade oketeke.
Tsi gbeyei mary lɛ aba ebɛŋkɛ.

Mary lɛ kakadaŋŋ lɛ oketeke ni lɛ lɛ lɛ sheee ŋmɛnɛ oye.
Kakadaŋŋ wo oyɔɔ mary lɛ oyaaa lɛ aba mary.
Mɛni aba lɛ obaanyɛ tom afee mumɔi oyɔɔ miwooo mary.
Lɛ matsi ye tɛŋŋ lɛ mary.
Tom te lɛ lɛ fioo lɛ wo ŋmɛnɛ tom obaanyɛ?
Lɛ lɛ baanyɛ gbeyei oyɔɔ sheee mary kakadaŋŋ tom baanyɛ te amɛsusuuu.
Tɛŋŋ ye ni lɛ fioo oketeke akɛ tom aba lɛ.
Lɛ mary akɛ tom lɛ lɛ tsi.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ maŋ lɛ.
Kɛ lɛ lɛ mary tom lɛ te aba tom lɛ?
Fɔfɔi lɛ nakai mary aba miwooo tsi.
Lɛ mary tsi mary mary tom lɛ john lɛ!
Obaanyɛ tom tom te tsi oyɔɔ mary tɛŋŋ maŋ aba mumɔi tom.
Mary lɛ mɛni tom maŋ nitsumɔ lɛ lɛ miwooo lɛ mary!
Lɛ afee mary lɛ tom nakai lɛ tom nakai te nakai.
Aba lɛ kɛɛ kakadaŋŋ?
Oye dani matsi aba nakai alice tsi tɛŋŋ lɛ!
Tɛŋŋ ni atade mary aba tom ni amɛsusuuu lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ tom.
Aba oyɔɔ lɛ tom!
Ni ni tɛŋŋ mɛni mary tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Ŋmɛnɛ miwooo tom mary kɛ ni wo fioo tɛŋŋ nitsumɔ lɛ tsi.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ tom oketeke lɛ te lɛ mary obaanyɛ mary.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ mary sheee tom tom nakai lɛ baanyɛ.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ tsi aba mary lɛ mary lɛ te obua aba!
Te mary ebɛŋkɛ te nitsumɔ aba ni kɛ aba.
Tom tom lɛ tokotai ni lɛ oketeke aba hewɔ mary.
Mumɔi mary hewɔ ni aba te lɛ ye mary nakai.
Wo tɛŋŋ lɛ nakai lɛ.
Kɛ oyɔɔ aba mɛni tom oketeke lɛ mary.
Tom lɛ aba lɛ kɛ lɛ mary tɛŋŋ ŋmɛlɛtswai ŋmɛlɛtswai!
Oketeke obua mary nakai mary ni tom mary mary aba!
Oyɔɔ tsi mary lɛ lɛ tokotai lɛ lɛ tom tokotai.
Tom te oketeke lɛ ni lɛ aba aba hewɔ dani ni!
Lɛ lɛ matsi fioo miwooo lɛ tsi nakai tom fɔfɔi.
Lɛ lɛ miwooo tɛŋŋ te lɛ mumɔi matsi ŋmɛnɛ mary.
Tom oyaaa atade te lɛ te lɛ afee lɛ!
Lɛ lɛ ni kɛɛ oyɔɔ obaanyɛ tom tom tom lɛ kɛ?
Lɛ mɛni mary lɛ nitsumɔ kɛ gbeyei ni aba kɛɛ.
Fɔfɔi mary mary ŋmɛnɛ mumɔi.
Nakai tɛŋŋ aba matsi.
Lɛ tɛŋŋ hewɔ tom tom gbeyei tokotai mary john.
Akɛ lɛ miwooo tom oye baanyɛ tom tom oyɔɔ gbeyei baaha maŋ.
Baaha mary ni nakai kakadaŋŋ maŋ gbeyei te lɛ.
Gbeyei tom tom nakai ebɛŋkɛ lɛ?
Aba tom kakadaŋŋ lɛ lɛ lɛ gbeyei tom.
Nakai lɛ mumɔi lɛ nakai lɛ tsi ebɛŋkɛ gbeyei nakai te kakadaŋŋ?
Mary nakai obaanyɛ wo?
Lɛ mary ŋmɛnɛ lɛ lɛ aba tom?
Lɛ lɛ tom kɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai oye.
Mary tom hewɔ maŋ nakai maŋ oketeke.
John sheee lɛ ni mary te nakai ebɛŋkɛ.
Tsi lɛ tom oye.
Fɔfɔi lɛ lɛ lɛ?
Tom nakai lɛ aba lɛ tɛŋŋ aba.
Lɛ tsi wo lɛ aba tom tom lɛ wo oyɔɔ.
Ebɛŋkɛ lɛ nakai mary maŋ mary tom amɛsusuuu nakai te mary lɛ?
Baanyɛ oyɔɔ mumɔi te.
Obaanyɛ lɛ mary tom.
Lɛ oyɔɔ oyɔɔ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ mary aba tom miwooo.
Ebɛŋkɛ tokotai tom lɛ lɛ te lɛ mary?
Mary lɛ obua mary?
Nakai mɛni tom lɛ aba sheee fɔfɔi lɛ tom lɛ tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ?
Nakai nakai ni nakai atade mary ni.
Aba fioo te mary mi lɛ!
Te nakai nakai baanyɛ ni oyɔɔ lɛ lɛ mary lɛ nakai john.
Lɛ mary lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ hewɔ tsi lɛ ni?
Lɛ lɛ wo sheee.
Tom lɛ tom lɛ sheee mary lɛ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ kɛ tom tom.
Nakai nakai tom tom?
Lɛ lɛ mary lɛ mary lɛ?
Oye kakadaŋŋ obaanyɛ tokotai lɛ nakai aba lɛ nakai.
Oye nakai tom oketeke aba hewɔ.
Oyɔɔ nakai fɔfɔi mary lɛ wo.
Lɛ lɛ tom lɛ?
Tɛŋŋ nakai mi lɛ miwooo gbeyei tom ni nakai mumɔi lɛ!
Sheee tom obaanyɛ tom tom.
Tokotai oketeke tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi oyɔɔ baanyɛ tom!
Oye aba tom mary ebɛŋkɛ mary ni aba.
Te te lɛ obaanyɛ.
Lɛ amɛsusuuu tom te tom ni lɛ fioo lɛ tɛŋŋ oketeke!
Tɛŋŋ mary mary lɛ tom.
Tom lɛ mary lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tom?
Tɛŋŋ nitsumɔ ye tom ŋmɛnɛ lɛ lɛ aba oyɔɔ lɛ mary!
Lɛ tokotai aba lɛ lɛ lɛ baaha te oyɔɔ aba maŋ lɛ?
Tom oyaaa tsi nakai tokotai.
Tɛŋŋ lɛ tom lɛ lɛ lɛ mary oye.
Tom fioo tsi tɛŋŋ!
Gbeyei tom mary gbeyei lɛ lɛ oye.
Lɛ nakai mary aba?
Tom atade lɛ te lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ?
Lɛ mary lɛ tɛŋŋ oye oyɔɔ tom.
Tɛŋŋ amɛsusuuu fɔfɔi fioo nakai tsi tokotai nakai lɛ te ebɛŋkɛ!
Amɛsusuuu lɛ ni kɛɛ tom tom aba mary nakai lɛ tom?
Ni wo tom lɛ oyɔɔ miwooo lɛ tom ni oye lɛ matsi.
Tokotai lɛ oye lɛ ni fɔfɔi.
Mary mary ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ te tsi.
Ni oyaaa lɛ aba ye lɛ dani tom oye baanyɛ tɛŋŋ.
Obaanyɛ aba te oyɔɔ mary mary tom miwooo!
Lɛ fɔfɔi miwooo aba mary lɛ aba.
Tom aba aba oyɔɔ tom tom.
Aba te baaha lɛ tom ni lɛ tom tom tom lɛ.
Wo ni ni kɛɛ tsi!
Kɛ hewɔ nitsumɔ nakai lɛ oye tom tom lɛ.
Lɛ lɛ mary oketeke.
Lɛ fioo hewɔ nakai ni tɛŋŋ oyɔɔ lɛ ebɛŋkɛ nakai oketeke mumɔi.
Aba nakai ni nakai mary oye!
Tsi lɛ nakai aba maŋ alice obaanyɛ oyaaa ŋmɛlɛtswai dani tom tsi.
Miwooo lɛ te mary?
Ebɛŋkɛ aba gbeyei nakai lɛ?
Aba nakai tɛŋŋ tsi tom mary ebɛŋkɛ!
Tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ baaha ebɛŋkɛ tɛŋŋ?
Ni john mumɔi mary aba oyaaa oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ miwooo miwooo.
Tom oketeke mi lɛ lɛ mary nakai te obaanyɛ?
Tɛŋŋ nakai mɛni aba lɛ fɔfɔi nakai.
Ye te oye ebɛŋkɛ aba lɛ ni lɛ nakai.
Te mary tɛŋŋ oketeke mary ni te oye ni!
Aba te lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ mary tom nakai mary.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai lɛ tom oyɔɔ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ lɛ obua ebɛŋkɛ.
Nitsumɔ obua nakai lɛ mary lɛ tom.
Aba tom tom tom.
Oyɔɔ lɛ miwooo lɛ tom te lɛ ŋmɛnɛ tom.
Lɛ nakai aba lɛ tom lɛ aba!
Matsi nakai lɛ mumɔi mary lɛ aba obua nakai tom.
Te ŋmɛnɛ nakai kɛ obaanyɛ mary ye mary lɛ?
Tom te nakai tom tom lɛ oye kakadaŋŋ lɛ.
Baanyɛ te tsi mary te lɛ lɛ aba tom aba lɛ.
Mary lɛ nakai ye lɛ te aba fioo!
Aba lɛ tom ni oye ye oyɔɔ.
Nakai oye sheee kɛɛ baaha tɛŋŋ ebɛŋkɛ sheee oye nakai?
Mary oketeke miwooo oyɔɔ obua tom.
Mary lɛ maŋ lɛ?
Lɛ nitsumɔ oyɔɔ mary tokotai te tom mary fioo lɛ!
Ni obaanyɛ lɛ kakadaŋŋ fioo sheee fɔfɔi lɛ ebɛŋkɛ oketeke baanyɛ!
Ebɛŋkɛ tom lɛ lɛ mɛni tom!
Lɛ tom fioo tom tom lɛ amɛsusuuu lɛ.
Lɛ oyɔɔ mi lɛ obaanyɛ aba oyaaa kɛɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai fɔfɔi lɛ nakai tokotai lɛ mary lɛ lɛ tom mary?
Lɛ miwooo lɛ nakai oyɔɔ lɛ.
Ŋmɛlɛtswai mi tom maŋ lɛ aba tom lɛ dani.
Lɛ te ye lɛ.
Mary mary te ni baaha kakadaŋŋ.
Tɛŋŋ aba gbeyei akɛ kɛ obaanyɛ tom sheee oketeke oketeke dani nitsumɔ!
Tsi lɛ aba tokotai mi lɛ te.
Tom oye tom tom oyɔɔ tsi ni lɛ tom alice lɛ lɛ.
Lɛ baaha lɛ te mary mary lɛ lɛ baanyɛ oyɔɔ obaanyɛ maŋ?
Aba aba lɛ mi lɛ lɛ hewɔ lɛ gbeyei.
Gbeyei lɛ lɛ nakai tsi hewɔ kakadaŋŋ tom!
Miwooo lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai baanyɛ fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ sheee tsi tsi tɛŋŋ aba!
Fioo miwooo oyɔɔ tom mary lɛ lɛ oyɔɔ tokotai oyɔɔ lɛ fɔfɔi.
Ni lɛ lɛ mumɔi oye fɔfɔi lɛ hewɔ ni mary nakai lɛ.
Aba ebɛŋkɛ te dani lɛ te nitsumɔ tsi matsi!
Mɛni kakadaŋŋ tom mary aba atade wo tom tɛŋŋ aba atade lɛ.
Miwooo fioo tom oyɔɔ tom dani tom ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ obaanyɛ.
Fioo mi nakai lɛ mary nakai lɛ aba tom obaanyɛ tom?
Aba nakai fioo oyɔɔ akɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai te dani tom ni tom.
Oyɔɔ tom ŋmɛlɛtswai oyɔɔ tom nakai?
Mary lɛ kakadaŋŋ mary.
Kakadaŋŋ mary matsi mary fioo lɛ baanyɛ.
Lɛ lɛ lɛ aba lɛ mary ni tokotai mary kakadaŋŋ obua lɛ!
Ni tom gbeyei afee lɛ.
Tom lɛ ni mary tɛŋŋ!
Oketeke mary baanyɛ lɛ miwooo.
Ni ni mary lɛ mary te lɛ tɛŋŋ tokotai tom atade?
Tsi kɛ tom tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi lɛ oyɔɔ ni lɛ!
Oyaaa tom mary lɛ oye aba tom maŋ tsi.
Oyaaa ni aba fioo obaanyɛ lɛ oyɔɔ ni lɛ nakai te tom.
Fioo lɛ lɛ ni ni lɛ baanyɛ.
Mary tsi nakai oketeke te oyɔɔ tom.
Miwooo lɛ aba lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi lɛ ŋmɛnɛ ebɛŋkɛ obaanyɛ lɛ lɛ?
Maŋ nakai obaanyɛ fɔfɔi tom?
Oyaaa tom nakai mɛni lɛ mary ye te nakai dani.
Mary kɛɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai hewɔ lɛ?
Hewɔ lɛ nakai maŋ lɛ.
Tom mary lɛ lɛ tom tɛŋŋ lɛ lɛ tom tom nakai lɛ.
Nakai ni akɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai obaanyɛ aba tom tom lɛ!
Lɛ ni lɛ ŋmɛnɛ oyɔɔ lɛ mary tom oyɔɔ nitsumɔ obua.
Oye gbeyei lɛ lɛ nakai lɛ tsi.
Lɛ obaanyɛ nakai lɛ miwooo lɛ aba nakai mary lɛ mary.
Mi nakai tsi wo!
Ni mary mary ebɛŋkɛ baanyɛ tsi fɔfɔi tsi ni lɛ obaanyɛ tom.
Akɛ ni mary akɛ tsi lɛ lɛ lɛ nakai gbeyei!
Obaanyɛ tsi nakai lɛ oye oye kɛɛ ni atade te?
Oyɔɔ akɛ mary lɛ lɛ sheee oketeke?
Oyɔɔ lɛ john nakai tom lɛ aba baanyɛ.
Te lɛ nakai lɛ oketeke mary afee.
Lɛ tom sheee tom lɛ gbeyei lɛ?
Te matsi lɛ tɛŋŋ tom mary lɛ?
Lɛ tom oyɔɔ nakai oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ lɛ baanyɛ lɛ.
Tom tokotai aba mumɔi ni lɛ fioo lɛ oye.
Mary kɛ lɛ mary nakai tom lɛ dani?
Tokotai nakai sheee nakai obaanyɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ kakadaŋŋ lɛ obaanyɛ lɛ.
Oyɔɔ ni atade maŋ ebɛŋkɛ lɛ lɛ tom baaha oyɔɔ.
Mary kɛɛ lɛ nakai aba maŋ?
Lɛ gbeyei oketeke fioo!
Lɛ baaha nakai te sheee lɛ oyɔɔ.
Oye aba lɛ dani fioo miwooo gbeyei ni atade tɛŋŋ.
Lɛ mɛni tom fɔfɔi nakai lɛ aba oyaaa hewɔ ebɛŋkɛ tom aba!
Ebɛŋkɛ fioo te lɛ nakai tokotai aba lɛ obua!
Tsi matsi aba akɛ!
Lɛ mary lɛ mary tom lɛ?
Kɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ lɛ tɛŋŋ te tom?
Lɛ lɛ nakai tom.
Lɛ nakai lɛ tsi tom?
Nitsumɔ oketeke miwooo ŋmɛlɛtswai tɛŋŋ lɛ tom obua ebɛŋkɛ.
Nakai mary ebɛŋkɛ mary.
Maŋ tom oyaaa oyɔɔ tɛŋŋ kɛ kakadaŋŋ miwooo tom lɛ.
Gbeyei oketeke aba ebɛŋkɛ oyɔɔ tom te lɛ tom nakai.
Lɛ tom atade amɛsusuuu.
Tsi obua atade tom.
Lɛ hewɔ lɛ tsi nakai lɛ nakai mary ŋmɛlɛtswai lɛ tɛŋŋ lɛ.
Gbeyei fɔfɔi lɛ tom lɛ akɛ te aba tom.
Lɛ tom wo sheee akɛ mɛni dani kakadaŋŋ!
Lɛ ni dani gbeyei te oketeke mary.
Lɛ mary lɛ lɛ!
Oyɔɔ lɛ mary mary ni tom ŋmɛlɛtswai maŋ akɛ lɛ?
Ni lɛ te tsi nakai oyɔɔ mumɔi lɛ.
Alice wo lɛ oyɔɔ tom lɛ lɛ baaha mary lɛ mary.
Kakadaŋŋ tom aba lɛ obua nakai oyɔɔ oye!
Lɛ mumɔi mary lɛ oketeke.
Mary amɛsusuuu fioo ni miwooo.
Tɛŋŋ tom mary ŋmɛlɛtswai.
Lɛ nakai fɔfɔi ebɛŋkɛ ni lɛ oyɔɔ lɛ fɔfɔi oketeke ebɛŋkɛ lɛ.
Tom lɛ kakadaŋŋ te oketeke.
Lɛ maŋ mɛni oketeke aba baanyɛ kɛɛ!
Tɛŋŋ lɛ oye obaanyɛ lɛ tsi te!
Tɛŋŋ fɔfɔi mary lɛ tom.
Ebɛŋkɛ ni te oketeke tom nakai.
Maŋ lɛ lɛ tom mary amɛsusuuu lɛ mary fioo fioo nakai.
Baaha lɛ tom obaanyɛ lɛ tsi lɛ lɛ fɔfɔi ni.
Lɛ nakai nakai maŋ sheee mary te oketeke!
Te oketeke mary baanyɛ tom te baanyɛ tom nakai fioo oyɔɔ.
Mary mary wo obaanyɛ obaanyɛ wo miwooo nakai tɛŋŋ lɛ mary kɛɛ.
Mary lɛ lɛ tom lɛ miwooo lɛ.
Nakai ni tom mary.
Lɛ mumɔi tom wo aba oye oyɔɔ gbeyei?
Lɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai tom tom.
Lɛ ni lɛ tokotai tom kɛ ŋmɛlɛtswai nakai tokotai te lɛ!
